| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_SD_NOTIFY`               | _(Edobeghi)_                     | `scripts/dev/systemd-notify.mjs`                                         | Tọọ `1` iji gbanyụọ systemd sd_notify (Ụdị = notify / WatchdogSec=) ọbụlagodi mgbe ị na-agba ọsọ n'okpuru ngalaba sistemụ. Ihe ngosi bụ enweghị op n'èzí sistemu n'agbanyeghị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CHECK_INTERVAL`          | `3600000`                        | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` / `src/lib/credentialHealth/scheduler.ts` | Ogologo oge (ms) maka onye nhazi nyocha ahụike ndabere ndabere. Opekempe: 10000 (10s).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL`               | `300000`                         | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` / `src/lib/credentialHealth/cache.ts`     | TTL (ms) maka ọkwa ahụike echekwabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CHECK` | `false`                          | `src/lib/credentialHealth/scheduler.ts`                                  | Tọọ `1` ma ọ bụ `true` iji gbanyụọ nyocha ndabere oge nke njikọ ndị na-eweta. Ndị na-achọ ihe (SEARCH_VALIDATOR_CONFIGS na `src/lib/providers/validation/searchProviders.ts`, dịka `tavily-search`) na-ewepu mgbe niile na — "nkwalite" ha bụ ajụjụ a na-akwụ ụgwọ n'ezie, n'ihi ya, a naghị enyocha ahụike na ngụ oge (#9970).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `DEEP_HEALTH_CHECK_ENABLED`                 | `0`                              | `src/app/api/monitoring/health/route.ts`                                 | Tọọ na `1` ka ikwe ka onye na-akpọ oku kwenye ka ọ rịọ `/api/monitoring/health?deep=1`, nke na-egosi n'elu mmecha ya otu ugboro kwa TTL. Ndị na-akpọ oku amaghị aha anaghị akpalite nyocha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_EVENT_LOOP_STALL_THRESHOLD_MS`   | `5000`                           | `src/lib/healthzLag.ts`                                                  | Ọnụ ụzọ nkwụsị (ms) maka ihe ndekọ ihe omume-loop boot: friza ogologo karịa nke a na-edekọ otu ahịrị `event loop stalled` na malitegharịa yana ebe nchekwa na ọnụ ọgụgụ ụgbọ elu, wee kụdaa `eventLoopStall` counter/maximum na `/api/monitoring/health`. Tọọ `0` iji gbanyụọ; uru enweghị ike ịgụ ma ọ bụ nke na-adịghị mma na-adaghachi na `5000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `HOST`                                      | `0.0.0.0`                        | `scripts/dev/run-next.mjs`                                               | Kechie adreesị maka sava Next.js dev/start. Na-ewepụ `0.0.0.0` ndabara mgbe atọrọ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `HOSTNAME`                                  | `127.0.0.1`                      | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`                                    | Adreesị ejikọta nke onye na-agba ọsọ Playwright na-eji mgbe ọ na-ebupute Next.js. Ihe ndabara na `127.0.0.1` maka ule hermetic.** Ejila `omniroute serve`** — jiri `OMNIROUTE_SERVER_HOST` kama (POSIX shells auto-set `HOSTNAME` na aha igwe; `.env` enweghị ike ịkagbu ya).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_SERVER_HOST`                     | `0.0.0.0`                        | `bin/cli/commands/serve.mjs`                                             | Kechie adreesị maka `omniroute serve`. Na-ezere ndakọrịta na mgbanwe `HOSTNAME` shei POSIX (na-edobe aha igwe mgbe niile site na bash/zsh). Daghachi azụ na `0.0.0.0` mgbe ịtọghe. (#6194)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

### Ụdị ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri

```
┌─────────────────────────── Single Port (default) ──────────────────────────┐
│  PORT=20128                                                                 │
│  → Dashboard: http://localhost:20128                                        │
│  → API:       http://localhost:20128/v1/chat/completions                    │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

┌─────────────────────────── Split Ports ─────────────────────────────────────┐
│  DASHBOARD_PORT=20128                                                       │
│  API_PORT=20129                                                             │
│  API_HOST=0.0.0.0                                                           │
│  → Dashboard: http://localhost:20128                                        │
│  → API:       http://0.0.0.0:20129/v1/chat/completions                     │
│  Use case: Expose API to LAN while restricting Dashboard to localhost.      │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

┌─────────────────────────── Docker Production ──────────────────────────────┐
│  PROD_DASHBOARD_PORT=443   PROD_API_PORT=8443                              │
│  → Maps container ports to host ports in docker-compose.prod.yml.          │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

## 4. Nchekwa & Nyocha

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                          | Ọdabara                                                                               | Isi mmalite faịlụ                                        | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MACHINE_ID_SALT`                           | `endpoint-proxy-salt`                                                                 | `src/lib/auth`                                           | Nnu jikọtara ya na njirimara ngwaike maka akara mkpịsị aka igwe. Gbanwee n'otu n'otu maka ịnọpụ iche.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_SALT`                        | _(Nnụ na-ewepụ = random kwa-wụnye nnu nọgidere na `<DATA_DIR>/cli-token-salt.json`) _ | `src/lib/machineToken.ts`                                | nnu HMAC maka inweta akara ngosi CLI mpaghara. Ịtọlite uru a na-atụgharị akara CLI niile na igwe ma na-ebute ụzọ mgbe niile karịa nnu na-adịgide adịgide. Lee `docs/security/CLI_TOKEN.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE`                        | `false`                                                                               | `src/lib/auth`                                           | Na-edobe ọkọlọtọ `Secure` na kuki nnọkọ.** Kwesịrị ịbụ `true`** mgbe na-agba ọsọ n'azụ HTTPS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `REQUIRE_API_KEY`                           | `false`                                                                               | API middleware                                           | Mgbe `true`, arịrịọ proxy `/v1/*` niile ga-enwerịrị igodo API bara uru.** Ọkọlọtọ a anaghị abanye `GET /v1/models`** , nke na-esote ọnọdụ nbanye dashboard (`requireAuthForModels`) kama - yabụ `401` sitere na `/v1/models` pụtara NOT pụtara na echedoro. Lee `docs/security/INFERENCE_AUTH_POSTURE.md` (#13695).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `ALLOW_API_KEY_REVEAL`                      | `false`                                                                               | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`         | Na-enye ohere ikpughe ụkpụrụ igodo API zuru ezu na Dashboard UI. Enwere ike ịhazi ya site na ọkọlọtọ njirimara Dashboard; ihe ize ndụ nchekwa n'ihe gbasara òkè.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `NO_LOG_API_KEY_IDS`                        | _(efu)_                                                                               | `src/lib/compliance/index.ts`                            | Igodo API kewapụrụ rikoma IDs nke na-agafe arịrịọ ntinye akwụkwọ (nkwenye GDPR ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `DEFAULT_RATE_LIMIT_PER_DAY`                | _(Edobeghi = unlimited)_                                                              | `src/shared/utils/apiKeyPolicy.ts`                       | Ndaghachi azụ kwa ụbọchị mmefu ego etinyere na igodo API nke kọlụm `rate_limits` bụ ihe efu. Ewepụghị ma ọ bụ tọgbọ chakoo: enweghị okpu doro anya (#2289, #11017). `0` bụ otu (na-akparaghị ókè). Integer N dị mma na-enyere aka N/day, 5N/week, 20N/month. Ụkpụrụ na-adịghị efu na-adaghachi azụ na windo 1000/day, 5000/week, 20000/month.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `MAX_BODY_SIZE_BYTES`                       | `10485760` (10 MB)                                                                    | `src/shared/middleware/bodySizeGuard.ts`                 | Ogo ahụ kacha anabatara. Ọ jụrụ ibu akwụ ụgwọ gafere oke a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_LARGE_BODY_BYTES`           | `262144` (256 KB)                                                                     | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ezigbo arịrịọ dị na ma ọ bụ n'elu ọnụ ụzọ a were usoro atomiki-mgbazinye nbanye dị arọ nke mpaghara tupu JSON parsing (ụzọ BYTE, gụnyere `POST /v1/responses`). Otu [# 10437](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/pull/10437) mgbapụ ahụike-headroom dị ka nhazi-dị arọ; ka ejiri `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` / [#10110](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/issues/10110) mee ka [#7849](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/issues/7849) ghara ịlaghachi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HARD_MAX_BODY_BYTES`        | `52428800` (50 MB)                                                                    | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Amanye okpu siri ike nkata nkata megide bytes na-agụ n'oge nri oke oke, gụnyere arịrịọ ndị na-efu efu, na-adịghị mma, ma ọ bụ emeghị ihe n'eziokwu `Content-Length`; ngafe na-anata `413`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT`        | _(Edoghị - enweghị okpu-ọnụọgụ arịrịọ)_                                               | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | **#503 - ndị na-agụ akwụkwọ:** Arịrịọ nketa a-COUNT okpu na-ejikọ naanị mgbe edobere ya nke ọma. Ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya (nke ndabara), nnabata nkata dị arọ bụ nke `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` ga-enweta ya - mmefu ego BYTE ewepụtara na akpaghị aka sitere na ezigbo ụlọ ebe nchekwa nke usoro ahụ.** otu usoro** (otu mkpọ V8). ~ 750k-token abụọ jikọtara ọnụ `/v1/responses` abort ~ 12 Gi ikpo (#7849) - a** ebe nchekwa-mmefu ego** ịdọ aka ná ntị, ọ bụghị a siri ike ngwaahịa max nke 2. A ike usoro (obo n'okpuru wụsara ratio) MAY ekweta ọzọ concurrent ogologo `/v1/responses` via `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEALTHY_HEADROOM`. Ọtụtụ iri ndị ahịa SSE ogologo (40-50) bụ mkpọ + `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` / #10110, ọ bụghị "max 2". N'iji kpuru ìsì na-ewelite nke a iji "jiri onye ọbịa" eweghachi #7849. mụbaa** ikpo** ya na** N onwe `DATA_DIR` s** (#11024); emela `replicas>1` n'otu faịlụ SQLite.                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES`         | _(Enwetara akpaaka)_                                                                  | `src/shared/middleware/admissionBudget.ts`               | **#503 - ndị na-agụ akwụkwọ:** mebie maka mmefu ego inest byte enwetara onwe ya (25% nke uko ụlọ nchekwa V8/cgroup siri ike kewara site na mmụba 8x na-agafe agafe). Ọbara ewepụtara na nke doro anya jigidere 8 MiB–2 GiB. Ahụ buru ibu karịa mmefu ego dị irè na-ada ozugbo na `413 body_exceeds_budget`; esemokwu n'etiti ozu ndị nwere ike ịrụ ọrụ n'otu n'otu ka na-anọgide na-emegharị `503`. 40–50 ndị ahịa SSE ogologo oge bụ mkpọmkpọ ego + a, ọ bụghị “max 2” siri ike. Gụọ `chatAdmission.maxInflightBytes` / `budgetSource` / `pressureSeverity` na `/api/monitoring/health` tupu ịmegharị ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEAP_SHED_RATIO`  | `0.75`                                                                                | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Oke-nrụgide wụsara (`heapUsed / heap_size_limit`) maka nnabata BYTE na STRUCTURE arọ (#10183, #10268, #10437). Arịrịọ arọ dị arọ na-aga n'ihu n'oge `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT` ga-awụpụ naanị site na `503` enwere ike ịnwale mgbe ikpo ahụ dị ALSO na ma ọ bụ karịa oke nke a; A na-anabata ya n'obo dị mma site na ime ụlọ ahụike.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEALTHY_HEADROOM` | `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT` (nke bu `1`)                                     | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ejikwa oke ikike maka ụzọ ngwa ngwa obo ahụike (#10437) na** ha abụọ** STRUCTURE na BYTE (`admitChatRequest`, gụnyere ozu ≥ `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_LARGE_BODY_BYTES`). Na-enweghị nke a, arịrịọ ọ bụla na-arụ ọrụ mana ọ nwere ahụike gafere nnabata na-enweghị ụlọ. Ozugbo ọtụtụ mgbazinye ego a na-arụkọ ọrụ na-arụ ọrụ site n'ụzọ ahụ siri ike, arịrịọ ndị ọzọ na-arụsi ọrụ ike na-adaba n'ụzọ SAME bounded-wait/shed ejiri n'okpuru ezigbo nrụgide. `0` na-ewepu uzo uzo kpamkpam.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

A na-agụ ihe ngbanwe dashboard ya (a DB override) tupu uru gburugburu a; `false`, `0`, `no`, ma ọ bụ `off` na nke ọ bụla na-atụgharị onye ọbịa na-enyocha ụzọ `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS=true` si eme, ọbụlagodi mgbe echekwabara dashboard ọkọlọtọ ahụ; ụkpụrụ ndị ọzọ na-ahapụ onye nche ahụ na ọkọlọtọ abụọ dị n'okpuru.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS`     | `false`                                                                               | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuardPolicy.ts`           | Gbanyụọ nlele ndị ọbịa URL nke na-apụ apụ, igwe-metadata ngọngọ gụnyere ( 169.254.0.0/16 niile, metadata.google.internal), n'ụzọ ndị na-eweta na-agbaso usoro nche: nkwado URL, nchọpụta ihe atụ, onye na-eweta-node base URLs (gụnyere oghere rerank rerank), na ule proxy-fallback, n'etiti ndị ọzọ na-eweta nso nso ụzọ image/media adịpụ. Ọ na-enye ohere ebumnuche webhook nkeonwe, belụsọ ebe njedebe cloud-metadata/link-local, nke na-egbochi ya ọbụlagodi ọkọlọtọ a. Arịrịọ nkata ezigara site na `BaseExecutor.execute()` na-enyocha igwe-metadata ha. Ndị na-enye mpaghara na-arụ ọrụ na ndabara: `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS` (ndabara `true`) enyelarị mpaghara na LAN URLs na nkwenye, nchọpụta, na ụzọ ndị na-eweta-ọnụ; Nnwale proxy-fallback na ebumnuche webhook nzuzo na-enyocha naanị ọkọlọtọ a wee gbochie ndị ọbịa local/LAN mgbe ọ na-apụ. Na-ewepụ `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS=false`. Uru echekwara na dashboard toggle na-ebute ụzọ karịa mgbanwe a.                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS`       | `true`                                                                                | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuardPolicy.ts`           | Kwe ka onye na-eweta URLs na adreesị local/private (127.0.0.1, localhost, LAN, nkeonwe) maka nkwado ndị na-eweta, nchọpụta ihe nlere anya, nkata, na ụzọ ọpụpụ ndị ọzọ na-eweta n'akụkụ ụzọ nche na-achị.** `true` dapụtara** (mpaghara-mbụ): onye nche na-egbochi njedebe njedebe-metadata igwe ojii - niile 169.254.0.0/16 gbakwunyere aha nnabata metadata ama ama (metadata.google.internal, metadata.goog, Alibaba/Azure/Oracle IMDS, fd00:ec2::254). Tọọ `false` maka ọnọdụ naanị ọha, nke na-egbochikwa ndị ọbịa nkeonwe na loopback. Onye nche na-enyocha aha nnabata ma ọ bụ IP nkịtị ka edere ya, tupu nyocha DNS ọ bụla; n'ụdị naanị ọhaneze, ihe mkpuchi nchekwa nchekwa na-edozikwa aha nnabata wee jụ azịza nzuzo ọ bụla. `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS=true` na-atụgharị nlele ndị a n'ụzọ ndepụta ahịrị ya. Ndị na-eweta mpaghara arụrụ arụ, dị ka LM Studio, Ollama, vLLM, Llamafile, na Triton, wụpụ ndenye ndị ọbịa niile maka nkwado isi na nkata. Uru echekwara na dashboard toggle na-ebute ụzọ karịa mgbanwe a. (#5066) |
| `AUDIO_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES`               | `false`                                                                               | `src/app/api/v1/_shared/audioProviderNodes.ts`           | Ka ụzọ `/v1/audio/*` (nsụgharị, okwu, ntụgharị asụsụ) jiri oghere ndị na-eweta OpenAI dakọtara na mpụga localhost. Gbanyụọ na ndabara - ntụgharị ọdịyo gaa na onye ọbịa dịpụrụ adịpụ na-agbanwe njirimara egress na ga-abụrịrị mkpebi onye ọrụ doro anya. Loopback/private nodes (localhost, 127.0.0.1, 172.16-31.x) na-anabata mgbe niile na enweghị mmetụta. (#3963)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `RERANK_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES`              | `false`                                                                               | `src/app/api/v1/_shared/rerankProviderNodes.ts`          | Ka `POST /v1/rerank` (na ebe nchekwa loopback rerank nzọụkwụ) jiri oghere ndị na-eweta OpenAI dakọtara na mpụga localhost — igbe LAN ma ọ bụ Tailscale ọgbọ na-agba TEI, Infinity, vLLM, wdg. Gbanyụọ na ndabara - iji ụzọ gaa na onye nnabata anya na-agbanwe njirimara egress na ga-abụrịrị mkpebi onye ọrụ doro anya. A na-anabata oghere loopback (localhost, 127.0.0.1, 172.16-31.x) na enweghị mmetụta. URL dịpụrụ adịpụ ga-agaferịrị amụma URL na-apụ apụ (`OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS` / `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_PROVIDER_NODE_HOSTS`       | _(efu)_                                                                               | `src/shared/network/localNodeHosts.ts`                   | Aha nnabata (rịkọma ma ọ bụ oghere ọcha kewapụrụ) nke na-enyefe-ọnụ ụzọ na-emeso ya dị ka mpaghara - na-abụkarị aha ọrụ Docker/Compose nke reranker, TEI ma ọ bụ audio sidecar na OmniRoute nke onwe Docker netwọk (`http://reranker:8080/v1`), yabụ ọnụ ahụ anaghị achọ akpa nwere koodu IP. Ndị ọbịa edepụtara tozuru oke maka `/v1/rerank`, `/v1/audio/*` na nlele ahụike mpaghara na-agbanyeghi `RERANK_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES` / `AUDIO_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES` maka ọnụ ụzọ ọ bụla dịpụrụ adịpụ, a na-akpọkwa ya ozugbo - ọ nweghị site na `HTTP(S)_PROXY` ma ọ bụ proxy njikọ njikọ. Ha ka na-eziga igodo API njikọ ahụ. Naanị otu akara aha na aha `.internal`/`.local` ka anabatara; ọha FQDNs, IP literal na ígwé ojii-metadata aha na-eleghara anya site na ịdọ aka ná ntị. (#14635)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN`     | `false`                                                                               | `src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts`                        | Mgbe agbanyere OIDC, gbanyụọ nbanye paswọọdụ ka ndị ọrụ nwee ike nyochaa naanị site na OIDC Single Sign-On. A na-anabatakwa utu aha efu `OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN` ; Ọkọlọtọ atụmatụ Dashboard nke otu igodo ahụ na-ebute ụzọ. (#10889)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN`               | `false`                                                                               | `src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts`                        | Bare utu aha `OMNIROUTE_OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN` (#10889).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

### Ndepụta nleba anya siri ike

```bash
# Production security minimum:
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true        # Requires HTTPS
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true           # Authenticate all proxy calls
ALLOW_API_KEY_REVEAL=false     # Never expose keys in UI
CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS=https://your.domain.com
MAX_BODY_SIZE_BYTES=5242880    # 5 MB limit
```

---

## 5. Ntinye ịcha ọcha & PII Nchekwa

OmniRoute na-enye nchekwa nke oyi akwa abụọ: nyocha nke ntụtụ n'akụkụ arịrịọ yana iwepụ PII nzaghachi.

> **⚠️ :** Ihe nchekwa ndị a bụ nchọpụta _kachasị mbọ ike heuristic_, ọ bụghị ngwa ngwa mgbanyụ ọkụ ma ọ bụ sistemu PII DLP. Ha nwere ike iwepụta ihe dị mma (benign persona/RPG kpaliri flagged) yana ihe ọjọọ (leetspeak, spacing, na-abụghị nke Bekee). Ha ezughị naanị maka nnabata. Dezie ụdịdị wee nwalee megide okporo ụzọ gị tupu ị dabere na ha.

### Akụkụ arịrịọ: Nchekwa ịgbanye ngwa ngwa

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                | Ọdabara      | Isi mmalite faịlụ                        | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| --------------------------------- | ------------ | ---------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED`         | `true`       | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Kwado nyocha nke ozi mbata maka usoro ịgba ọgwụ ozugbo.                                                                                                                                 |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE`            | `warn`       | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Amụma ịgba ọgwụ: `warn` = log naanị, `block` = jụrụ arịrịọ ya na 400. Legacy `redact` na-eme ya.** ọ bụghị** ederede ịgba ntụtụ; jiri `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED` maka arịrịọ PII degharịa. |
| `INJECTION_GUARD_MODE`            | _(Edobeghi)_ | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Legacy utu aha maka `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE` - otu omume.                                                                                                                                 |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` | `high`       | `src/shared/utils/injectionSeverity.ts`  | Ogo kacha nta nke `MODE=block` jụrụ: `high` (ndabere), `medium`, ma ọ bụ `low`. A na-ahụ ụkpụrụ nke ọkara ma ọ bụrụ na ewedara ya.                                                      |
| `INJECTION_GUARD_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` | _(Edobeghi)_ | `src/shared/utils/injectionSeverity.ts`  | Legacy utu aha maka `INPUT_SANITIZER_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` - otu omume.                                                                                                                      |
| `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED`           | `false`      | `src/lib/guardrails/piiMasker.ts`        | Mgbe `true`, megharịa PII na arịrịọ mbata (na-adabereghị na ọnọdụ ịgba ntụtụ).                                                                                                          |
| `CREDENTIAL_REDACTION_ENABLED`    | `false`      | `src/lib/guardrails/credentialMasker.ts` | Megharia ụkpụrụ akara igodo API / nzuzo ama ama nke ọma site na ibu request/response. Ịbanye; enyo `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED`.                                                             |

### Azịza-akụkụ: PII Sanitizer

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe               | Ọdabara  | Isi mmalite faịlụ         | Nkọwa                                                                     |
| -------------------------------- | -------- | ------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION`      | `false`  | `src/lib/piiSanitizer.ts` | Nyochaa nzaghachi LLM maka PII agbapụtara tupu ịlaghachi na onye ahịa.    |
| `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION_MODE` | `redact` | `src/lib/piiSanitizer.ts` | `redact` = nkpuchi PII, `warn` = log naanị, `block` = dobe nzaghachi dum. |

### VS Code Tokenized-Route Context Sanitizer

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                  | Ọdabara | Isi mmalite faịlụ                           | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ----------------------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_VSCODE_SANITIZE_CONTEXT` | `1`     | `src/app/api/v1/vscode/contextSanitizer.ts` | Mwepu ọnọdụ onye editọ na-arụsi ọrụ ike (`editorContext`, `activeEditor`, `currentFile`, `selection`, `openTabs`…) site na arịrịọ `/v1/vscode/[token]/*` wee megharịa ọdịnaya nke faịlụ nwere mmetụta agbakwunyere nke ọma. Nchekwa-na-ndabere; tọọ `0` iji gbanyụọ. |

### Ihe atụ

| Ihe atụ              | Nhazi                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| -------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **nnabata ụlọ ọrụ**  | `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED=true`, `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE=block`, `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED=true`, `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION=true` (ngbochi injection + request/response PII redaction; ụdịdị nwere onwe) |
| **Nleba anya naanị** | `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED=true`, `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE=warn` — ndekọ ma ọ dịghị ihe mgbochi                                                                                                          |
| **Ojiji nkeonwe**    | Hapụ niile nwere nkwarụ - efu n'elu                                                                                                                                                                 |
