## 6. Ngwá ọrụ & Usoro ụzọ

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                                          | Ọdabara                       | Isi mmalite faịlụ                               | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ----------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `TOOL_POLICY_MODE`                                          | `disabled`                    | `src/lib/toolPolicy.ts`                         | Na-achịkwa ohere LLM tool/function-calling. `allowlist` = naanị ngwaọrụ edepụtara, `denylist` = niile ma ewezuga edepụtara, `disabled` = enweghị mgbochi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PAYLOAD_RULES_PATH`                              | `./config/payloadRules.json`  | `open-sse/services/payloadRules.ts`             | Ụzọ iji kwụọ ụgwọ njikwa iwu JSON faịlụ (per-model/protocol upstream tweaks).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PAYLOAD_RULES_RELOAD_MS`                         | `5000`                        | `open-sse/services/payloadRules.ts`             | Bugharịa nkeji oge (ms) maka ibugharị faịlụ iwu ịkwụ ụgwọ dị ọkụ. Nke kacha `1000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_PREFER_CLAUDE_CODE_FOR_UNPREFIXED_CLAUDE_MODELS` | `false`                       | `open-sse/services/model.ts`                    | Wepụ: ụzọ efu `claude-*` ụdị IDs sitere na ndị ahịa Claude Koodu site na akaụntụ Claude Code OAuth kama ịchọ prefix nke ndị na-eweta. prefixes ndị na-eweta ihe ka na-emeri. Enwere ike ịhazi ya site na ntụgharị dashboard na ibe ndị na-eweta Claude.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDACT_THINKING`                                 | `true`                        | `open-sse/executors/base/redactThinkingBeta.ts` | Tọọ `false` ka ọ kwụsị OmniRoute ịgbakwụnye `redact-thinking-2026-02-12` Anthropic beta n'onwe ya (ihe mgbochi mbinye aka na-enweghị isi). Arịrịọ na `thinking.display: "summarized"` mafee ya n'agbanyeghị; beta redact nke ndị ahịa kparịtara ụka ma ọ bụ `requestDefaults.redactThinking` dakọtara na CC na-edobe ya. Arịrịọ Claude OAuth dobe beta (ọ bụ akụkụ nke akara mkpisiaka Claude Code OAuth ) ma ọ bụrụ na agbanyeghi ọkọlọtọ njiri mara `CLAUDE_OAUTH_SUMMARIZED_THINKING_UNREDACTED` adịghị.     |
| `BARE_MODEL_DEFAULT_OVERRIDE_LEGACY`                        | `false`                       | `open-sse/services/model.ts`                    | Omume `resolveBareModelToConnectionDefault` nkeha nwere (#4825): dochie aha ụdị ọ bụla na `defaultModel` njikọ ahọpụtara. Gbanyụọ na ndabara, naanị `auto` ma ọ bụ ihe efu ka edochiri; na-edekọ ịdọ aka ná ntị otu ụzọ mgbe ọ nọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `COMBO_CONCURRENCY_PER_MODEL`                               | `3`                           | `open-sse/services/comboConfig.ts`              | Okpu concurrency ihe nlere nke ọ bụla maka combos robin (#9100). Semaphore gburugburu robin combo bụ nke siri ike na arịrịọ ugboro atọ n'otu n'otu na-enweghị nkwụsị, na-ahazi okporo ụzọ dị elu n'azụ okpu ahụ. Ekwenyere na `>= 1`, machiri ya na `<= 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `DISABLE_CONTEXT_WINDOW_CHECKS`                             | `false`                       | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore.ts`                 | Nbanye dị ize ndụ nke na-awụpụ ọnọdụ mpaghara OmniRoute-window / max-input-token maka arịrịọ otu ụdịdị kpọmkwem. Ndị na-eweta elu ka na-amanye oke oke ha; mkpakọ ngwa ngwa na okpu ihe nrụpụta nke ihe nlereanya ahụ ka na-arụ ọrụ. Ụzọ dị irè bụ ọkọlọtọ njirimara DB na-ewepụ > gburugburu ebe obibi agbanwe > ndabara; ọ dịghị Malitegharịa ekwentị a chọrọ.                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`                           | _(Edobeghi)_                  | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`          | Akwụkwọ YAML `providers:` n'ahịrị (RIC-738, D4). Mgbe atọrọ (ya na ma ọ bụ na-enweghị ngọngọ `strategy:` ), `/v1/chat/completions` na-atụgharị gaa na ntinye dakọtara na OpenAI jikọtara ọnụ kama ịbụ ọkpọkọ igwe ojii. Ewepụghị (nke ndabara): ụzọ ahụ dabara ozugbo na ọkpọkọ igwe ojii dị. Lee `docs/routing/SELF_HOSTED_OPENAI_ENTRY.md`.                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS_FILE`                      | _(Edobeghi)_                  | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`          | Ụzọ na faịlụ YAML na-ejide otu akwụkwọ `providers:` dị ka `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`, maka ntinye akwụkwọ na-ahọrọ faịlụ karịa inline env var. Onye ọ bụla na-eme ka ntinye nke onwe ya rụọ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_API_KEY`                             | _(Edoghị - ụzọ mepere emepe)_ | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`          | Igodo API ekekọrịtara nhọrọ maka ntinye ahaziri nkeonwe (D5 scaffold, edobere maka sistemụ oke-otu). Mgbe atọrọ, arịrịọ ga-agụnye `Authorization: Bearer <key>`. Ewepụghị: ụzọ a ghere oghe, dabara n'ụkpụrụ onye na-eweta mpaghara nwere onwe ya (loopback/trusted-network deployment).                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY`                            | _(Edobeghi)_                  | `open-sse/services/routingStrategies.ts`        | Akwụkwọ YAML `strategy:` n'ahịrị maka injin ntugharị deterministic (M2/RIC-740, D3) - blacklist/whitelist, ihe nkwụsịtụ oyi, ọnụ ahịa-ebute ụzọ, mgbagha-amata, yinye ọdịda. Na-ewepụ ngọngọ `strategy:` inline etinyere n'ime `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`, otu igodo. Lee `docs/routing/DETERMINISTIC_ROUTING.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY_FILE`                       | _(Edobeghi)_                  | `open-sse/services/routingStrategies.ts`        | Ụzọ na faịlụ YAML na-ejide otu akwụkwọ `strategy:` dị ka `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY`, maka ntinye akwụkwọ na-ahọrọ faịlụ karịa inline env var.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CONVERSATION_TRACKING`                   | _(Edobeghi)_                  | `open-sse/services/conversationTracker.ts`      | Tọọ `1` ka ịkwụsị ịnakọta akụkọ mkparịta ụka. `resolveConversationId()` na-eweghachite nsonaazụ a na-emebeghị tupu ọ gụọ SQLite ma ọ bụ tụsa akụkọ ozi, yana IDs nke ndị ahịa wetara na-ekpuchikwa. Agbanweghị njikwa usoro-mgbagharị ma anaghị ehichapụ ndekọ dị adị. Maka mbugharị ndị na-adịghị eji nlele mkparịta ụka dashboard ma chọọ ka tebụl tụgharịa kwụsị itolite.                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_NADIR_API_KEY`                                   | _(Edobeghi)_                  | `open-sse/services/autoCombo/nadirStrategy.ts`  | igodo API maka atụmatụ Nwepu-na `nadir` auto-combo router atụmatụ, nke a na-eji mgbe agwakọtaghị `config.nadir.apiKey` nke combo. Nhọrọ: oku enweghị igodo ala na ọkwa Nadir na-amaghị aha, nke nwere oke nha kwa IP na enweghị ike iji ya n'azụ ọnụ ụzọ mbata. Mmetụta; na-ahọrọ ubi ọ bụla combos mgbe combos na-eji akaụntụ dị iche iche.                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_NADIR_BASE_URL`                                  | `https://api.getnadir.com`    | `open-sse/services/autoCombo/nadirStrategy.ts`  | Isi mmalite nke mkpebi Nadir API, ejiri ya mgbe mkpọmkpọghị `config.nadir.baseUrl` nke onwe ya. Naanị achọrọ maka Nadir kwadoro onwe ya, nke na-edobe isi ozugbo n'ime netwọkụ nke gị. A na-anabata `/v1` na-eso ya ma yipụ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_WEEKLY_WINDOW`                           | `provider`                    | `src/lib/usage/apiKeyUsageLimits.ts`            | Window kwa izu maka oke ojiji nke API-key USD (arịrịọ mmanye, `GET /api/keys/{id}/usage-limits`, `@@om-usage`). `provider`: izu ahụ na-agwụ na nrụpụta izu mbụ n'etiti njikọ ndị dị n'elu igodo nwere ike iru, ma ọ bụ ụbọchị 7 na-atụgharị mgbe amaghị nke ọ bụla. `calendar`: izu mpaghara site na Mọnde 00:00 ruo Mọnde na-esote 00:00, yiri igodo ọ bụla. A na-atụ aro `calendar` mgbe ọtụtụ igodo emebere ego na-ekekọrịta otu ọdọ mmiri nke akaụntụ dị elu. Ụkpụrụ ezighi ezi na-adaghachi na `provider`. |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_WEEKLY_WINDOW_TIMEZONE`                  | _(usoro oge mpaghara)_        | `src/lib/usage/apiKeyUsageLimits.ts`            | IANA timezone (dịka ọmụmaatụ `Asia/Shanghai`) nke na-akọwa Monday 00:00 maka windo `calendar` kwa izu. Atọghị ma ọ bụ adịghị mma: mpaghara oge nke usoro OmniRoute ( `TZ` nke onye ọbịa ma ọ bụ akpa, nke na-adịghị edobekarị, ya bụ oge a na-ahazi oge niile, na foto akpa). Agbagharala ya na ọnọdụ `provider`.                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_TOOLS_LIMIT`                                 | `128`                         | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                  | Ọnụọgụ ngwaọrụ kacha anabatara na arịrịọ (OpenAI ndabere). Ọnụ ọgụgụ dị mma naanị; ụkpụrụ edobereghị ma ọ bụ ezighi ezi dowe `128`. Onye na-eweta ya nwere oke known/proactive (`grok-cli` 200, `nvidia` 1536) ka na-etinye okpu nke ya - nke a na-ebuli elu ụlọ ndabara. Ebumnuche Claude-format enweghị okpu ndabara (ozi API enweghị nke ọ bụla); oke mmụta ka na-emetụta.                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROMPT_EMULATE_TOOLS`                            | _(na)_                        | `open-sse/executors/syntxChat.ts`               | SYNTX.ai ngwa ngwa-emulated ngwá ọrụ ịkpọ: unset ma ọ bụ `1`/`true`/`on`/`yes` igbanye mpaghara ngwá ọrụ katalọgụ n'ime ngwa ngwa; uru ọ bụla ọzọ (dịka `0`) na-amafe ntụtụ ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

---

## 7. URLs & Mmekọrịta igwe ojii

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                          | Ọdabara                                                   | Isi mmalite faịlụ                                | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `BASE_URL`                                  | `http://localhost:20128`                                  | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | N'akụkụ sava URL maka ọrụ mmekọrịta ime ka ịkpọ `/api/sync/cloud`. Debe nke a dị ka loopback/container URL ọbụlagodi mgbe ngwa ahụ bụ proxied ọha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CLOUD_URL`                                 | _(efu)_                                                   | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Cloud relay endpoint URL (atụmatụ adịchaghị).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CLOUD_SYNC_TIMEOUT_MS`                     | `12000`                                                   | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | HTTP agwụla maka arịrịọ mmekọrịta igwe ojii.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILD_PROFILE`                   | `full`                                                    | Nhazi nhazi nke ngwugwu Webpack                  | Profaịlụ nrụpụta oge (dobere na `minimal` ka ewepu modul ndị nwere oke n'anụ ahụ na ngwugwu).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_STANDALONE_DIR`                  | _.build/ nke kwụụrụ onwe ya pụta_                         | `scripts/build/colocate-standalone.mjs`          | Mwepụta oge wuo maka ndekọ ndekọ mmepụta nke kwụụrụ onwe ya site na nzọụkwụ nrụgharị nrụpụta gachara. Ọ bụghị nhazi oge ojiri gaa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIST_DIR`                        | `dist/` (mgbọrọgwụ repo)                                  | `scripts/build/prepublish.ts`                    | Mechie oge maka akwụkwọ ndekọ usoro mbipụta (`prepublish.ts`) na-ede ngwugwu CLI n'ime, kama `dist/` n'okpuru mgbọrọgwụ repo. A na-ehichapụ akwụkwọ ndekọ aha tupu arụ ọrụ ahụ. Ọ bụghị nhazi oge ojiri gaa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRET`               | _(efu)_                                                   | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Ihe nzuzo ekekọrịtara ejiri iji nyochaa mbinye aka HMAC-SHA256 nke nzaghachi mmekọrịta igwe ojii.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRETS`              | `false`                                                   | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Tọọ na `true` iji kwe ka njedebe mmekọrịta Cloud ka degharịa nzere mpaghara. Ihe ndabara bụ `false`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_ENFORCE_SIGNATURE`    | `false`                                                   | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Tọọ `true` ka ọ jụ nzaghachi mmekọrịta igwe ojii na-edoghị aka mgbe ahazighị nzuzo nzuzo (#13679). A na-enyocha mbinye aka dị ugbu a mgbe niile - na-ajụkwa mgbe ọ bụla `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRET` adịghị edozi - n'agbanyeghị ọkọlọtọ a. Ihe ndabara na-atụgharị ka etinyere ya na v3.9.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_ZED_IMPORT_LEGACY_ONE_STEP`      | `false`                                                   | `src/app/api/providers/zed/import/route.ts`      | Tọọ `true` ka ọ daa azụ na v3.8.5 otu nzọụkwụ "bubata ihe niile" omume na-enweghị nkwenye onye ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`                      | `http://localhost:20128`                                  | OAuth, Dashboard, mmekọrịta                      | URL chere ihu ọha maka OAuth redirect_uri, njikọ Dashboard, yana URLs ọha emepụtara. Tọọ nke a na URL ọha kwụsiri ike mgbe OAuth azụghachi azụ ma ọ bụ njikọ ihe nchọgharị ewepụtara ga-ejirịrị onye nnabata reverse-proxy host.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_CLOUD_URL`                     | _(efu)_                                                   | Ndị ahịa-akụkụ                                   | mirror n'akụkụ onye ahịa nke `CLOUD_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL`                       | _(Edobeghi)_                                              | `src/shared/services/cloudSyncScheduler.ts`      | Ndaghachi azụ azụ maka `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_PORT`                          | _(Edoghị - daa azụ na `PORT`)_                            | `src/shared/hooks/useDisplayBaseUrl.ts`          | Ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri ọdịda nke ndị ahịa maka ngosipụta URLs mgbe amaghị ebe esitere (SSR/tests); gụọ tupu `PORT`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`                 | _(Edobeghi)_                                              | Onye nbibi sitere na ọha, onyonyo URLs           | Ebe OmniRoute na-eche ihe nchọgharị ebute ụzọ kacha elu ejiri maka ọhaneze URL yana nkwado mmalite ihe nchọgharị na-abụghị dashboard. Tọọ nke a mgbe OpenWebUI ma ọ bụ relay ọzọ rutere OmniRoute site n'ime URL mana ihe nchọgharị onye ọrụ ga-ewepụtarịrị usoro mgbasa ozi sitere na LAN, ọwara, ma ọ bụ ebe ọha. Mee** ọ bụghị** gụnyere `/v1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_MANIFEST_URL`           | _(Edobeghi)_                                              | `open-sse/config/providerPluginManifestUrl.ts`   | Ngwa mgbakwunye zuru oke na-eweta URL kpọsara ndị ahịa sidecar. Mgbe edobere, OmniRoute na-enweta `/api/v1/provider-plugin-manifest` site na mbido arịrịọ ma ọ bụ HOST/PORT.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_PROTOCOL`                 | `http`                                                    | `open-sse/config/providerPluginManifestUrl.ts`   | Protocol eji eme ihe mgbe ị na-enweta ngwa mgbakwunye onye na-eweta ihe ngosi URL site na HOST/PORT na-enweghị mmalite arịrịọ. Tọọ `https` n'azụ proxy ọha TLS na-akwụsị mgbe enweghị `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_MANIFEST_URL` doro anya edobere.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRUST_PROXY`                     | _(Edobeghi)_                                              | `src/server/origin/publicOrigin.ts`              | Ụdị ntụkwasị obi nhọrọ maka nkụnye ndị isi sitere na ọha ezigara. Akwụsịghị = atụkwasịla `Forwarded` / `X-Forwarded-*` maka mkpebi nchekwa. `true` / `loopback` na-atụkwasị obi host/proto naanị site na proxy loopback akara akara. `private` / `lan` na-atụkwasịkwa obi na ndị ọgbọ proxy-LAN nkeonwe, ma hapụkwa àkwà mmiri `API_PORT` idobe `X-Forwarded-For` nke ọgbọ dị otú ahụ. Masịrị `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` doro anya na mmepụta.                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `KIE_CALLBACK_URL`                          | _(Edobeghi)_                                              | `open-sse/utils/kieTask.ts`                      | Nkpọghachi ọhaneze URL maka ọrụ asynchronous kie.ai. Mbugharị kacha kacha elu tupu `OMNIROUTE_KIE_CALLBACK_URL` na `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_KIE_CALLBACK_URL`                | _(Edobeghi)_                                              | `open-sse/utils/kieTask.ts`                      | Ntụgharị asụsụ `KIE_CALLBACK_URL` ọzọ. Daghachi azụ mgbe edobere isi mgbanwe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_URL`                      | _(Edobeghi)_                                              | `open-sse/utils/kieTask.ts`                      | A na-eji mmalite ọha wee dee async callback URLs. Ọdaghachi azụ kacha dị ala maka kie.ai oku azụ; ejirikwa ya dị ka URL ọha na eze maka relays ndị ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_CROF_USAGE_URL`                  | `https://crof.ai/usage_api/`                              | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | CrofAI nchọta nchọta nke ibe ojiji na-eji. Wepụ maka relays / nnwale ndozi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLINEPASS_USAGE_URL`             | `https://api.cline.bot/api/v1/users/me/plan/usage-limits` | `open-sse/services/usage/clinepass.ts`           | ClinePass 5-hour / izu / kwa ọnwa / ọnwa njedebe nke ibe ojiji na-eji. Wepụ maka relays / nnwale ndozi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_OPENCODE_QUOTA_URL`              | `https://opencode.ai/zen/go/v1/usage`                     | `open-sse/services/opencodeQuotaFetcher.ts`      | Official API-key-igodo ziri ezi OpenCode Go njedebe ojiji nke ibe ojiji na-eji. Wepụ maka relays / nnwale ndozi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS`           | `true`                                                    | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                 | Synthesize OpenCode CLI nkụnye eji isi mee (Agent-Agent, x-opencode-client/project, request/session UUIDs) na arịrịọ opencode-go/zen elu nke onye ahịa ezipụghị, yabụ Cloudflare na VPS egress na-anabata ha (#6210/#5997). Na ndabara kemgbe #10571; pụọ na `false`/`0`/`no`/`off`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OPENCODE_USER_AGENT`                       | `opencode/1.18.31`                                        | `open-sse/utils/opencodeHeaders.ts`              | A na-eji onye ọrụ-nnọchite anya mgbe `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` dị na ọ nweghị onye na-eweta `<PROVIDER>_USER_AGENT` akwadoro. Tinyere naanị na ndị mmebe oghe koodu. Na arịrịọ enweghị igodo ọnụ ụzọ mgbago elu, uru ahaziri nke na-adịghị ebu `opencode/<version >= 1.17>` na-eji ndabara dochie ya kama ịjụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_FORWARD_ATTRIBUTION`             | `1` (na)                                                  | `open-sse/utils/opencodeHeaders.ts`              | Bugharịa nkụnye eji isi mee ngwa ndị ahịa (`x-session-id`, `x-title`, `http-referer`) gaa na ndị na-eweta ihe, yabụ arịrịọ na-amalite n'ime ihe eji edepụtara na-edobe njirimara ya site na rawụta ( igodo mbipụta OpenRouter na ndị a). `0`/`false`/`no`/`off` na-edobe njirimara registry/operator wee tụda ụkpụrụ ndị ahịa wetara. nkụnye eji isi mee ahaziri onye ọrụ na-emeri ụkpụrụ ndị ahịa mgbe niile.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OPENCODE_CLIENT`                           | `desktop`                                                 | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                 | Uru maka nkụnye eji isi mee `x-opencode-client` agwakọtara mgbe `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` dị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OPENCODE_PROJECT`                          | `global`                                                  | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                 | Uru maka nkụnye eji isi mee `x-opencode-project` agwakọtara mgbe `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` dị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_EGRESS_IP_LOCK_PROVIDERS`        | _(Edobeghi)_                                              | `open-sse/config/providerErrorRules.ts`          | Ndị na-eweta ọrụ dị mma bụ ndị egress IP na-akwakọba ọdịda nke elu ha (otu ogige 429 na-ekekọrịta njikọ nwanne ọ bụla nke IP). Unset na-eji ndabara-ọkwa efu `opencode,opencode-go,opencode-cli`; `none`/`off`/`false`/`0` na-agbanyụ kascade ahụ kpamkpam; ndepụta nke kewara rikoma na-anọchi ntọala. A na-akwakọba atụmatụ ugwo n'elu akaụntụ ma nwee ike gbanyụọ ya (#9611).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_EGRESS_QUOTA_ROTATION_PROVIDERS` | _(Edobeghi)_                                              | `open-sse/config/providerErrorRules.ts`          | Oke ntụgharị ọjụjụ nke oke ntụgharị. Unset na-ewepụta onye otu ọdọ mmiri na 429 naanị maka ezinaụlọ nwere bọket IP n'elu; `all` na-agbatị akwụkwọ ahụ nye onye ọ bụla na-eweta ya, yabụ oke ọnụego a na-enweta site n'aka onye otu na-eme nhọrọ ọzọ n'ebe ọzọ. A na-eleghara uru ọ bụla na-abụghị `all` anya. Chọrọ `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED` (na ndabara). A na-emechi ụlọ ahịa ahụ site na adreesị egress, ọ bụghị (onye na-eweta, adreesị), ya mere ogige ntụrụndụ 429 gbasaa maka onye ọ bụla na-enye ọrụ ruo mgbe oge ahụ gafere (Nkeji 5, okpukpu abụọ ruo 15). Gụọ kwa nsonaazụ nzaghachi, yabụ mgbanwe na-emetụta na-enweghị mmalite. |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_TIER_REQUEST_CONTRACT`       | _(Edobeghi)_                                              | `open-sse/executors/opencodeFreeTierContract.ts` | Tọọ `off` ka kwụsị imezigharị ahụ nke arịrịọ OpenCode enweghị igodo (ọkọlọtọ nkwanye na ndepụta ngwaọrụ). A ka na-etinye nkụnye eji isi mee. Gụọ kwa arịrịọ, ka mgbanwe na-amalite ozugbo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_TIER_PLACEHOLDER_TOOLS`      | _(Edobeghi)_                                              | `open-sse/executors/opencodeFreeTierContract.ts` | Aha ngwa rịkọm-kewapụrụ iji kwuwapụta na arịrịọ OpenCode enweghị isi nke na-ebughị nke ọ bụla, mgbe ọ nweghị ihe ahụrụla maka ụdị ahụ. Ihe efu daa azụ n'otu ebe a gwara ihe nlereanya ahụ ka ọ ghara ịkpọ. Ọtụtụ ndenye 32, `[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,63}`; eleghara ndenye na-ezighi ezi anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_TIER_CREDENTIAL`             | `public`                                                  | `open-sse/executors/opencodeFreeTierContract.ts` | Ezitere nzere n'okporo ụzọ n'efu (`https://opencode.ai/inference/v1`). Kemgbe 2026-10-21 ụzọ ahụ na-aza 403 `Model access is disabled` na ezigbo igodo API ma na-eje ozi nkịtị na-amaghị aha kama. `key` na-edobe igodo API onye ọrụ, `none`/`off` anaghị eziga nkụnye eji isi mee nzere, a na-ezigara okwu nkịtị ọ bụla ọzọ dị ka akara onye na-ebu ya. Gụọ kwa arịrịọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_OLLAMA_CLOUD_USAGE_URL`          | `https://ollama.com/settings`                             | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Ollama Ntọala igwe ojii URL ejiri mee ihe maka mkpocha oke. Wepụ maka relays / nnwale ndozi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_OLLAMA_CLOUD_BALANCE_URL`        | `https://ollama.com/api/balance`                          | `open-sse/services/ollamaCloudBalance.ts`        | Ollama Cloud dekọtara njedebe nguzozi n'elu (`GET https://ollama.com/api/balance`, `Authorization: Bearer <API key>`). Gụọ otu ugboro na modul ibu. Ejiri mbụ maka Ollama Cloud quota mgbe njikọ ahụ nwere igodo API ; kuki kuki ntọala-ibe ahụ na-anọ dị ka ọdịda azụ. Wepụ maka relays / nnwale ndozi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OLLAMA_USAGE_COOKIE`                       | _(Edobeghi)_                                              | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Kuki Ollama Cloud `__Secure-session` ejiri mee ihe maka mkpochapụ ibe-ibe nhazi. Mmetụta; na-ahọrọ ogige Dashboard njikọ ọ bụla mgbe ahazire ọtụtụ akaụntụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OLLAMA_CLOUD_USAGE_COOKIE`                 | _(Edobeghi)_                                              | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Kuki Ollama Cloud `__Secure-session` ọzọ env var. Mmetụta; na-ahọrọ ogige Dashboard njikọ ọ bụla mgbe ahazire ọtụtụ akaụntụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_OLLAMA_USAGE_COOKIE`             | _(Edobeghi)_                                              | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Ollama Cloud `__Secure-session` kuki env var ọzọ ejiri tupu utu aha dị mkpụmkpụ. Mmetụta; na-ahọrọ ogige Dashboard njikọ ọ bụla mgbe ahazire ọtụtụ akaụntụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEWHISPERER_BASE_URL`          | `https://codewhisperer.us-east-1.amazonaws.com`           | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | CodeWhisperer (AWS Kiro) njedebe njedebe ojiji. Wepụ maka relays / nnwale ndozi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Mgbe ị na-ebugharị n'azụ proxy (nginx, Caddy), tọọ `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` na URL ọha gị kwụsiri ike (dịka, `https://omniroute.example.com`) mgbe OAuth akpọghachite ma ọ bụ njikọ ọha emepụtara ga-eji aha nnabata ahụ. Na-enweghị nke a, OAuth callbacks nwere ike daa n'ihi na redirect_uri agaghị adaba na njikọ ọhaneze ewepụtara nwere ike tụọ aka na isi mmalite akpa.
>
> Debe `BASE_URL` dị ka ime loopback/container URL maka ọrụ nkesa-na-sava. Ejila `Origin` ihe nchọgharị ma ọ bụ aha nnabata ọhaneze maka ihe njiri mara nke ime onwe ya.
>
> Akwụkwọ dashboard enwetara nke ọma anaghị achọ ebe ọhaneze kwụ ọtọ URL: dashboard ahụ na-eziga arịrịọ na-adịghị mma site na iji akara CSRF nwere oge. OmniRoute ka na-emezi nkwado ndabere nke ọha maka njikọta ihe nchọgharị na-abụghị dashboard: ezi ntụkwasị obi URL env vars ọha na-ebu ụzọ tụkwasị obi; A na-eleghara isi `Forwarded` / `X-Forwarded-*` raw zqf ma ọ bụrụ na enyereghị aka `OMNIROUTE_TRUST_PROXY` yana akara ndị ọgbọ proxy ozugbo dị ka ntụkwasị obi. Ejila ntọala CORS dozie arịrịọ dashboard nke sitere na otu; CORS bụ naanị maka ndị ahịa ihe nchọgharị gafere.

## 8. Proxy na-apụ apụ

Ndị na-eweta LLM na-aga elu na-akpọ site na HTTP ma ọ bụ SOCKS5 proxy maka njikwa egress, geo-routing, ma ọ bụ IP masking.

`OMNIROUTE_UPSTREAM_HTTP2_ENABLED` ndabara na `true`. Tọọ ya na `false`, `0`, `no`, ma ọ bụ `off` ịmanye HTTP/1.1 na ndị na-ezigara Undici na `open-sse/utils/proxyDispatcher.ts` na `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`: kpọmkwem egress, HTTP(S)/SOCKS proxies, relays, na ha ọhụrụ-socket mbigharịa. Nke a na-echekwa nkwenye akwụkwọ TLS, nyocha proxy, oke njikọ na ntọala idobe ndụ. Malitegharịa OmniRoute mgbe ị gbanwee ya n'ihi na a na-echekwa ndị na-ezipụ ozi na-emepụta ndị ọrụ relay na mmalite. Mgbanwe ahụ anaghị ejikwa akara mkpịsị aka wreq TLS ma ọ bụ ụgbọ njem akọwapụtara nke onye na-eweta ya.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                       | Ọdabara      | Isi mmalite faịlụ                              | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ---------------------------------------- | ------------ | ---------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`                    | `true`       | `open-sse/executors`                           | Kwado onye nnọchi anya SOCKS5 maka oku elu. Wepụ na `false`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`        | `true`       | Ndị ahịa-akụkụ                                 | Mmata n'akụkụ ndị ahịa maka nnweta SOCKS5.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`             | `true`       | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`             | Ọkọlọtọ njirimara na-na ndabara (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md) ; dashboard DB meriri emerie). Ọdọ mmiri proxy na ntụgharị akaụntụ ọ bụla kwụsịrị ijere onye otu nke dara ada (ajụjụ TCP nyocha, ma ọ bụ 429 site na ya) maka oge na-abawanye ugboro abụọ na nkwugharị ọ bụla, ruo okpu. `false` weghachite nhọrọ dị larịị.                                  |
| `SELECTOR_CONTROL_ALLOWLIST`             | _(Edobeghi)_ | `src/lib/proxySubscription/selectorGuard.ts`   | Nye ndepụta ohere maka ebumnuche njikwa mpaghara-isi nke gafere loopback (comma/space kewapụrụ ndị ọbịa ma ọ bụ IPs, `*` emepe LAN/private mana ọ dịghị mgbe metadata/link-local). A na-anabataghachi azụ azụ http/https na ndabara. Uru dashboard DB na-emeri mgbanwe a mgbe edobere na enweghị efu.                                                                    |
| `PROXY_QUOTA_429_BASE_MS`                | `300000`     | `open-sse/utils/proxyRefusalMemory.ts`         | Oge ntọala (ms) nke 429 ewepụtara, gbagoro okpukpu abụọ na nkwugharị ọ bụla. Gụọ otu ugboro na mmalite. Akpọrọ ya na `1000..3600000`; na-abụghị oke ma ọ bụ enweghị ọgụgụ na-adaghachi na ndabara na ịdọ aka ná ntị.                                                                                                                                                     |
| `PROXY_QUOTA_429_MAX_MS`                 | `900000`     | `open-sse/utils/proxyRefusalMemory.ts`         | Okpu (ms) nke 429 set-side. Gụọ otu ugboro na mmalite. Akpọrọ ya na `1000..3600000`; na-abụghị oke ma ọ bụ enweghị ọgụgụ na-adaghachi na ndabara na ịdọ aka ná ntị. Mgbe okpu dị n'okpuru ntọala, ọ na-adaghachi na ndabara. Iweda okpu na-ebelata ogologo oge ichefu: ndenye na-ehichapụ `2 x max` ka ọ gwụchara.                                                       |
| `PROXY_POOL_SHARED_EGRESS_ORDER`         | `false`      | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`             | Ọkọlọtọ njirimara Nwepu (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md) ; dashboard DB override meriri). Maka ndị na-eweta oke nke adreesị egress kpuchiri, họpụta onye otu ọdọ mmiri na-ekerịta adreesị egress onye otu jụrụ nso nso a dị n'okpuru ndị otu ahụike. Naanị ịtụ, ewepughị ya. Chọrọ `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`, nke na-emepụta mgbama ọjụjụ ọ na-agụ.        |
| `PROXY_WEBHOOK_REBOUND_MS`               | `300000`     | `src/lib/proxyEvents/proxyTransitionBridge.ts` | Window nlọghachi (ms) capping kwugharịa `proxy.set_aside` webhook alerts kwa onye otu ọdọ mmiri ka mwapu nke jụrụ na-eziga otu mmemme. Gụọ na nke ọ bụla emit. Akpọrọ ya na `1000..3600000`; na-anọghị nso ma ọ bụ enweghị ọgụgụ na-adaghachi na ndabara na-agba nkịtị.                                                                                                  |
| `HTTP_PROXY`                             | _(Edobeghi)_ | Node.js ọkọlọtọ                                | HTTP proxy maka oku elu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `HTTPS_PROXY`                            | _(Edobeghi)_ | Node.js ọkọlọtọ                                | HTTPS proxy maka oku elu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ALL_PROXY`                              | _(Edobeghi)_ | Node.js ọkọlọtọ                                | Proxy zuru ụwa ọnụ (na-akwado `socks5://`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_ECHO_URL`               | _(Edobeghi)_ | `src/lib/proxyEchoTarget.ts`                   | Na-atụnye ebumnuche echo-IP nke proxy egress nyocha na-eji na otu URL. Atọghaghị, nyocha ahụ nwara `api64.ipify.org` wee `api4.ipify.org` yabụ ọwara IPv4 naanị adịghị akọpụta anwụ (#9694).                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `NO_PROXY`                               | _(Edobeghi)_ | Node.js ọkọlọtọ                                | hostnames/IPs kewapụrụ rikoma ka ịgafe proxy.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_DISPATCHER_CONNECTIONS` | `32`         | `open-sse/utils/proxyDispatcher.ts`            | Oke sọket ndị na-emekọ ihe n'otu onye na-ezipụ HTTP/SOCKS proxy nke echekwara. iyi SSE dị ogologo dị ka Codex `/v1/responses` chọrọ njikọ karịa otu mgbe ọtụtụ arịrịọ na-ekekọrịta otu proxy ọkwa akaụntụ. Uru dị n'elu `256` ka agbachiela.                                                                                                                             |
| `SOCKS_HANDSHAKE_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `10000`      | `open-sse/utils/socksConnectorWithFamily.ts`   | SOCKS5 aka aka (jikọọ) akwụsịla na ms. Welie ya mgbe otu onye nnabata ọnụ ụzọ ámá nwere nnukwu concurrency (dịka 100 arịrịọ n'otu oge) - ezigbo aka aka nwere ike gafere 10s n'okpuru ọdọ mmiri jupụtara n'agbanyeghị na enwere ike iru proxy, nke na-apụta dị ka `[Proxy Fast-Fail] Proxy unreachable` ụgha. Edebere na `120000`.                                       |
| `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN`                        | `false`      | `src/sse/handlers/chatHelpers.ts`              | Mgbe `false` (ndabara), arịrịọ onye proxy ekenyere na-emezughị bụ** jụrụ (emechiri emechi)** kama ịdaghachi na njikọ kpọmkwem - na-egbochi nkwụsị nke ezigbo IP. Tọọ `true` iji weghachi azụ azụ azụ azụ DIRECT.                                                                                                                                                         |
| `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT`                 | `false`      | `open-sse/executors`                           | Mpempe mkpịsị TLS na-eji wreq-js (na-eṅomi Chrome 124). Mgbochi JA3/JA4.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `TLS_FINGERPRINT_PROVIDERS`              | _(Edobeghi)_ | `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`                 | Ndepụta ohere ndị na-eweta rịkọm maka ụzọ TLS proxied ọhụrụ (`open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`). Direct TLS na-edobe omume ihe nketa ya mgbe edobere ya; naanị ndị na-enye ndị a na-aga site na akwa akara mkpịsị aka Chrome-124. ewepu Groq (`api.groq.com`) ọbụlagodi mgbe edobere listi ikike, n'ihi na Chrome JA3 na-ebute Cloudflare 1010 ebe undici nwa afọ ruru API. |
| `OMNIROUTE_TURNSTILE_IGNORE_TLS_ERRORS`  | `false`      | `open-sse/services/claudeTurnstileSolver.ts`   | Kwe ka ọnọdụ ihe nchọgharị Claude Turnstile Playwright leghara njehie akwụkwọ HTTPS anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

### Ihe atụ

| Ihe atụ                                       | Nhazi                                                                                                                     |
| --------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **SOCKS5 site n'ọwara SSH**                   | `ALL_PROXY=socks5://127.0.0.1:7890`, `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY=true`                                                           |
| **Ụlọ ọrụ HTTP proxy**                        | `HTTP_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `HTTPS_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `NO_PROXY=localhost,internal.corp.com` |
| **Mgbochi mkpịsị aka**                        | `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true` - chọrọ `wreq-js` (gụnyere)                                                                 |
| **Egress na-achịkwa / enweghị ohere ozugbo**  | Hapụ `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=false` (nke ndabara). Arịrịọ na-ada nke ọma mgbe proxy adịghị adị kama isite n'aka ozugbo.          |
| **Legacy/ dev — kwe ka ndaghachi azụ ozugbo** | `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=true`. Na-eweghachi omume tupu ikesi ike: njikọ a na-eji mgbe mkpebi proxy dara.                         |

> **Mara (NVIDIA nkwado uzo - #3226):** NVIDIA API-igodo nkwado nkwado ebe njedebe mgbe a chụpụrụ proxy/TLS-patched zuru ụwa ọnụ (undici dispatcher → 504). `src/lib/providers/validation.ts::directHttpsRequest()` kpachaara anya na-agafe patch proxy maka otu oku nkwado ahụ site na iji `safeOutboundFetch({ bypassProxyPatch: true })`. Nke a bụ akwụkwọ edebere, wezugara - ọ na-eme ya** ọ bụghị** emetụta chat/usage egress. Eji `tests/unit/proxy-bypass-scope-guard-3226.test.ts` kechie uzo ngafe ahụ.

## 9. CLI Ngwakọta Ngwa

Na-achịkwa ka OmniRoute si achọpụta ma wepụta CLI sidecars (Claude Code, Codex, wdg).

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                | Ọdabara                          | Isi mmalite faịlụ                                     | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| --------------------------------- | -------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLI_MODE`                        | `auto`                           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | `auto` = usoro ọchụchọ PATH; `manual` = jiri naanị ụzọ doro anya.                                                                                                                                                                              |

### CLI Profaịlụ Auto-mmekọrịta

Ọkọlọtọ atụmatụ ndị a abanyela na ndabara. Enwere ike ịtụgharị ha na dashboard koodu CLI.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                    | Ọdabara | Isi mmalite faịlụ                                | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ------------------------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CODEX_PROFILES`  | `false` | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Mgbe mmekọrịta ụdị onye na-eweta, na-edegharị faịlụ profaịlụ `~/.codex/*.config.toml` na-akpaghị aka site na katalọgụ dị ndụ. chọrọ `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; adịghị agbanwe agbanwe active/default Codex config, auth, Codex-lb ntọala, ma ọ bụ onye na-eweta nhọrọ.                |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CLAUDE_PROFILES` | `false` | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Ka emechara mmekọrịta ụdị onye na-eweta, na-edegharị faịlụ profaịlụ `~/.claude/profiles/<name>/settings.json` Claude na-akpaghị aka site na katalọgụ dị ndụ. chọrọ `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; anaghị agbanwe agbanwe active/default Claude config, auth, ma ọ bụ onye na-eweta nhọrọ. |

### Docker Ihe Nlereanya

```bash
# Mount host binaries into the container and tell OmniRoute where they are:
CLI_EXTRA_PATHS=/host-cli/bin
CLI_CONFIG_HOME=/host-home
CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES=true
CLI_CLAUDE_BIN=/host-cli/bin/claude
```

`CLI_CONFIG_HOME` na-arụ ọrụ naanị mgbe a na-ejikọta ụzọ ahụ site na onye ọbịa - jikọta ya na ugwu dị ka `~/.codex:/host-home/.codex:rw` (lee profaịlụ `host` na `docker-compose.yml`). A na-eleghara ụzọ nke na-abụghị n'ime ụlọ ndị na-ebu akpa ma ọ bụ n'elu ugwu, n'ihi na a ga-atụfu ihe odide ebe ahụ mgbe emegharịrị akpa ahụ.

Ihe onyonyo a na-agba dị ka `USER node`, yabụ `/root` enweghị mgbakwasa bụ** ọ bụghị** ezigbo ngafe.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                       | Ọdabara        | Isi mmalite faịlụ                   | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ---------------------------------------- | -------------- | ----------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTAINER`                    | _(na akpaaka)_ | `src/shared/utils/containerEnv.ts`  | Mmanye nchọpụta akpa na (`1`/`true`) ma ọ bụ gbanyụọ (`0`/`false`). Naanị achọrọ na oge ọsọ ọsọ, nchọpụta akpaaka na-efunahụ ya.                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_CONTAINER_CONFIG_WRITE` | `false`        | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` | Kwe ka nhazi ngwa ngwa CLI na-ede n'ime ụzọ akpa enweghị n'agbanyeghị. Ihe nhata CLI bụ `--allow-container-write`.                                                                                                                                        |
| `DOCKER_UPDATE_TRIGGER_FILE`             | ịtọhapụ        | `src/lib/system/autoUpdate.ts`      | naanị foto Docker : dashboard** Melite Ugbu a** na-ede arịrịọ JSON na faịlụ a (ga-abụrịrị n'ime `DATA_DIR`) maka onye na-ahụ maka ndị ọbịa ka ọ dọpụta ma megharịa akpa ahụ. Hụ [ntuziaka Docker](../guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md#dashboard-updates-in-docker). |

### CLI Binary (`omniroute`) ndị enyemaka

Ngbanwe ndị a na-emegharị omume ọnụọgụ abụọ `omniroute` CLI (ọ bụghị nchọpụta akụkụ ụgbọ ala dị n'elu).

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                    | Ọdabara                           | Isi mmalite faịlụ                       | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------------------- | --------------------------------- | --------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_LANG`                      | _(sistemu) _                      | `bin/cli/i18n.mjs`                      | Kwado asụsụ mmepụta CLI. BCP-47 mpaghara (dịka `en`, `pt-BR`). Wepụ sistemu mpaghara env vars (LC_ALL, LC_MESSAGES).                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_SHOW_LOG`                  | _(Edobeghi)_                      | `bin/cli/runtime/processSupervisor.mjs` | Tọọ `1` ka ibugharịa stdout/stderr nkesa na ọnụ na ọnọdụ a na-elekọta. Dakọtara na ọkọlọtọ `--log` na `omniroute serve`.                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_TOKEN`                 | _(Edobeghi)_                      | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Agbara akara nyocha igwe dị ka nkụnye eji isi `x-omniroute-cli-token`. Emepụtara akpaaka na ọrụ 8.12.                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `GEMINI_CLI_HOME`                     | _(Edobeghi)_                      | `bin/cli/commands/setup-gemini.mjs`     | mgbọrọgwụ Gemini na-adịgide adịgide; otu agba ọsọ na-eji ụlọ dịpụrụ adịpụ nwa oge.                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHILD_SIGNAL_TIMEOUT_MS`   | `5000`                            | `bin/cli/commands/run.mjs`              | Oge amara na milliseconds tupu mmanye-egbu nwa CLI akara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_SKIP_DEFAULT_DATA_ENV` | _(Edobeghi)_                      | `bin/omniroute.mjs`                     | Tọọ na 1 jiri DATA_DIR doro anya ka ịwụpụ faịlụ gburugburu data-akwụkwọ ndekọ aha nke onye ọrụ ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_SYNC_TOKEN`                | _(Edobeghi)_                      | `bin/cli/commands/sync.mjs`             | Token mmekọrịta maka `omniroute sync bundle` (`GET /api/sync/bundle` na-anabata naanị akara mmekọrịta, ọ bụghị igodo API ). Dakọtara na `--token`.                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_HTTP_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `30000`                           | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Kwa oge HTTP nkwụsị oge (ms) maka arịrịọ sava CLI →.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_READY_TIMEOUT_MS`          | `60000`                           | `bin/cli/utils/pid.mjs`                 | Oge kachasị (ms) CLI na-echere ebe njedebe ahụike nkesa tupu ibipụta ịdọ aka ná ntị nkwụsị oge. Ọ bara uru maka mmalite oyi nwayọ (dịka Windows). A na-edozikwa site na `--ready-timeout`.                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_VERBOSE`                   | `0`                               | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Tọọ na `1` ka ibipụta nchọpụta retry/backoff na stderr n'oge iwu CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLUGIN_PATH`               | _(Edobeghi)_                      | `bin/cli/plugins.mjs`                   | Ndekọ ahaziri maka nchọpụta ngwa mgbakwunye CLI (ngwugwu `omniroute-cmd-*` ). Ọ na-adabara `~/.omniroute/plugins/` mgbe ewepụtaghị ya. Naanị CLI - ọ dịghị erute n'ihe nyocha ngwa mgbakwunye ihe nkesa, nke `OMNIROUTE_PLUGINS_DIR` na-atụ aka (ngalaba 2).                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_NO_UPDATE_NOTIFIER`        | _(Edobeghi)_                      | `bin/omniroute.mjs`                     | Wepu nlele mmelite mgbe edobere ya na uru ọ bụla na-adịghị efu: ọkwa CLI na ọpụpụ usoro yana ọkọlọtọ “mmelite dị” dashboard (`GET /api/system/version` wee wụpụ npm, GitHub na nlele ozi). Ọkwa CLI na-anọkwa na CI, ọnọdụ dị jụụ, yana mmepụta anaghị ede.                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCTOR_LIVENESS_URL`       | _(nke ewepụtara)_                 | `bin/cli/commands/doctor.mjs`           | Ndụ URL nke `omniroute doctor` nyochara . Nhọrọ `livenessUrl` mmemme na-ebute ụzọ; ma ọ bụghị ya, a na-eji ọnụ ahịa a ọnụ. Atọghị, CLI na-enweta njedebe `/api/health/degradation` nke dashboard mpaghara.                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_CATALOG_PATH`     | `src/shared/constants/providers/` | `bin/cli/provider-catalog.mjs`          | Ụzọ katalọgụ onye na-enye iwu nke ndị na-eweta CLI. Nhọrọ `catalogPath` mmemme na-ebute ụzọ. A na-eji ụzọ zuru oke eme ihe dị ka; ikwu ụzọ dozie site na mgbọrọgwụ oru ngo. Ewepụghị ya, CLI na-eji akwụkwọ ndekọ aha ndị na-eweta ya, yana faịlụ `providers.ts` nke ketara dị ka ọdịda. |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_TEST_MODEL`       | _(ndabara onye na-eweta ya)_      | `bin/cli/provider-test.mjs`             | Ihe nlere ọdịda zuru ụwa ọnụ maka ule njikọ ndị na-eweta CLI. Ahaziri ụkpụrụ njikọ ụzọ ụzọ, ụdị onye na-eweta kpọmkwem gabigara, uru a, emesia ụdị nnwale arụnyere n'ime onye na-eweta ya.                                                                                               |

| `OMNIROUTE_BASE_URL`                            | chọpụta akpaaka                                     | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | URL doro anya maka ngwaọrụ MCP/A2A iji ruo OmniRoute. Na-ewepụ nchọpụta akpaaka localhost.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`                             | _(Edobeghi)_                                        | MCP/A2A                                                     | igodo API maka ngwa MCP n'ime yana oku nka A2A.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_ID`                          | _(Edobeghi)_                                        | `open-sse/mcp-server/audit.ts`                              | Igodo ID maka njirimara ndekọ ndekọ ndekọ MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ROUTER_API_KEY`                                | _(Edobeghi)_                                        | Ihe nketa                                                   | Aha nke aha maka `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_HISTORY_RETENTION_DAYS`          | `30`                                                | `src/lib/a2a/taskManager.ts`                                | Ụbọchị nke akụkọ ihe mere eme A2A dobere na nchekwa data mpaghara tupu ịsachapụ kwa ụbọchị ihichapụ ahịrị. Atọghị, na-abụghị ọnụọgụ, ma ọ bụ `<= 0` daa azụ na `30`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_MEMORY_HITS`                     | `1`                                                 | `src/lib/a2a/taskExecution.ts`                              | Kill-switch maka njiri mara ebe nchekwa A2A. Tọọ na `0` ka ịwụpụ nchọta ncheta ncheta maka ọrụ kpamkpam; uru ọ bụla ọzọ (gụnyere enweghị ntọala) na-eme ka ọ rụọ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_ENABLED`                 | `false`                                             | `src/app/api/issue-agent/runs/route.ts`                     | Na-enyere ndị nnọchi anya offline/local Issue aka ndekọ njedebe njedebe. Hapụ nwere nkwarụ ọ gwụla ma ọ na-arụ ọrụ usoro ndekọ aha nke mpaghara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_TIMEOUT_MS`              | _(Edobeghi)_                                        | `src/lib/issueAgent/execution.ts`                           | Oge agwụla (ms) maka otu onye nnọchite nke mbipụta na-agba ọsọ. Ejidebere ya na oke ime; daa azụ na ndabara arụnyere n'ime ya mgbe edobere ma ọ bụ na ezighi ezi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT`                             | _(Nkọwa nke na-arụ ọrụ) _                           | `bin/cli/program.mjs`, `bin/cli/api.mjs`                    | CLI remote-mode context/profile maka iwu `omniroute` ; na-akagbu ọnọdụ nọ n'ọrụ na ụlọ ahịa ihe ọnọdụ mpaghara. Dakọtara na `--context <name>`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

Mgbe enyere ya, nzere na-anọgide na `config.json` mode `0600` na CLI na-ewepụta ịdọ aka ná ntị ọdịda otu oge; ezubere maka kpachara anya rụọ ọrụ headless/container.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_ENFORCE_SCOPES`                  | `false`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | Manye njikwa ohere dabere na obosara na oku ngwaọrụ MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
