Gbanyụọ ụkpụrụ: `0`, `false`, `off`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                | `10000`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/fetchTimeout.ts`                       | Akwụsị mmefu ego (ms) maka njikwa ime ihe nkesa MCP-agụ (ahụike, ike resilience, combos, oke, ojiji).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_UPSTREAM_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `60000`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/fetchTimeout.ts`                       | Wepu mmefu ego (ms) maka MCP hops na-echere onye na-eweta (`route_request`, `web_search`, `web_fetch`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_CORPUS_CACHE_SIZE`                   | `5`                                                 | `src/lib/localCorpus/configured.ts`                         | Onu ogugu kachasi ihe nleba anya nke mpaghara-corpus echekwabara na ebe nchekwa (LRU, otu n'ime ndekọ aha mgbọrọgwụ indexed). Ejidebere na opekempe `1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `MODEL_SYNC_INTERVAL_HOURS`                     | `6`                                                 | `src/shared/services/modelSyncScheduler.ts`                 | Ogologo oge mmekọrịta katalọgụ ụdị n'ime awa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_SYNC_INTERVAL_MINUTES`         | `70`                                                | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Oke ọnụego onye na-eweta ya na oge ntuli aka oke.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_SYNC_SPACING_MS`               | `1500`                                              | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Oghere (ms) n'etiti nsonye oke OAuth n'usoro n'ime nnukwu mmekọrịta; A na-enweta njikọ OAuth n'otu n'otu ka ịzenarị ịgbawa elu. `0` na-apụ apụ (n'otu oge).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_MIN_INTERVAL_MS`         | `250`                                               | `open-sse/services/quotaFetchThrottle.ts`                   | Obere nkeji (ms) n'etiti ntinye oke n'usoro n'usoro preflight/monitor nke onye ọ bụla; Oghere netwọk n'otu oge na-akpọ ọtụtụ akaụntụ na otu IP anaghị agbawa elu. Ejikọtara n'ime Codex (`/wham/usage`), DeepSeek, Bailian (saịtị abụọ a na-ebubata), OpenCode, na ndị na-ebubata oke Crof (#6009, #6911). Ụzọ izipu `usage.ts::getUsageForProvider` zuru oke (github/glm/minimax/nanogpt/xai/etc.) ekpuchibeghị — nke iche. Cache emetụtaghị ya. `0` gbanyụọ; kpọgidere `0..60000`. |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_JITTER_MS`               | `120`                                               | `open-sse/services/quotaFetchThrottle.ts`                   | Arịrịọ ndị ọzọ na-enweghị usoro (ms), atụnyere n'ọkwa ọ bụla echekwabara n'elu iyi. Ihe ndabara na 120; kpọgidere `0..60000`; `0` na-ewepu naanị jitter. Tọọ `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_MIN_INTERVAL_MS=0` iji gbanyụọ trotul dum. Jitter anaghị ekwe nkwa mgbochi mmachibido iwu elu.                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

Ọrụ karịrị ya na-adaba emeghe (enweghị mgbako) kama itolite ikpo ọnụ ụzọ ámá.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `COMPRESSION_PIPELINE_BREAKER_ENABLED`          | `false`                                             | `open-sse/services/compression/pipelineEngineBreaker.ts`    | T02 stacked-pipeline kwa-engine sekit-na-emebi nna ukwu mgba ọkụ.** Nwepu (nke mbụ)** — mgbe ọ na-arụ ọrụ, injin na-atụba ugboro ugboro n'ofe arịrịọ na-awụpụ (fail-emeghe) maka oyi; gbanyụọ = byte-omume ihe nketa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `COMPRESSION_PIPELINE_BREAKER_THRESHOLD`        | `3`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/pipelineEngineBreaker.ts`    | Ọdịda nke arịrịọ gafere n'usoro tupu igwe nbiji emepe emepe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `COMPRESSION_PIPELINE_BREAKER_COOLDOWN_MS`      | `30000`                                             | `open-sse/services/compression/pipelineEngineBreaker.ts`    | Milliseconds igwe meghere meghere ka amafere tupu nyocha ọkara mepere emepe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `COMPRESSION_CCR_RETRIEVAL_RAMP_FACTOR`         | `2`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/engines/ccr/index.ts`        | T08/H8 CCR iweghachite-nzaghachi ramp: nke ọ bụla tupu iweghachite ngọngọ echekwara na-ebuli `minChars` ya dị irè n'ahịrị (ọdịnaya na-eweghachi ugboro ugboro na-akpakọ obere; `>=3` eweghachite = anaghị agbanye ya). `1` na-agbanyụọ rampụ ahụ (ọnụọgụ abụọ na-awụ elu naanị).                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `COMPRESSION_CCR_DURABLE_STORE`                 | `true`                                              | `open-sse/services/compression/engines/ccr/index.ts`        | Ụlọ ahịa ngọngọ CCR na-adịgide adịgide (#9061). Jiri SQLite kwado ụlọ ahịa ebe nchekwa ka ihe mgbochi ga-adị ndụ nchụpụ LRU, TTL, ịmalitegharị, ma ọ bụ weghachite mbudata na ihe atụ ọzọ. Tọọ `false` ka idowe ihe mgbochi na ebe nchekwa naanị. Ihe mgbochi karịrị 512KB na ígwé ojii runtimes na-anọ ebe nchekwa naanị n'agbanyeghị.                                                                                                                                              |
| `COMPRESSION_PREFIX_FREEZE_ENABLED`             | `false`                                             | `open-sse/services/compression/prefixFreeze.ts`             | T08/H5 hụrụ n'iji prefix freeze master mgba ọkụ.** Nwepu (nke mbụ)** — mgbe ọ na-aga, a na-ahụta usoro `>=` n'ọnụ ụzọ dị ka prefix nwere ike ịchekwa ma chekwaa ya site na mkpakọ ọbụlagodi maka ndị na-eweta ihe na-efunahụ cache static heuristic (friji naanị _preserves_, anaghị agbanwe agbanwe).                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `COMPRESSION_PREFIX_FREEZE_THRESHOLD`           | `3`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/prefixFreeze.ts`             | Nlebanya maka sistemụ ozugbo tupu ewere ya dị ka nganiihu kwụsiri ike kpọnwụrụ akpọnwụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_BOOTSTRAPPED`                        | `false`                                             | `src/app/(dashboard)/dashboard/page.tsx`                    | Tọọ `true` na edemede bootstrap ka nhazi mbụ gasịrị. Na-achịkwa ọhụhụ ọkachamara ntọlite.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_BODY_PROJECT_OVERRIDE`         | `0`                                                 | `open-sse/executors/antigravity.ts`                         | Ọpụpụ mgbapụ: kwe ka òtù arịrịọ kagbuo ubi ọrụ Antigravity.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_CREDITS`                           | `off`                                               | `open-sse/services/antigravityCredits.ts`                   | Google Otu AI kredit amụma: `off` anaghị agbanye kredit, `retry` na-adụ otu ugboro mgbe oke ruru 429, na `always` gbara na arịrịọ mbụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_MAX_429_ATTEMPTS`                  | `2`                                                 | `open-sse/services/antigravity429Guard.ts`                  | Upstream 429s kwadoro kwa Antigravity (`agy`/`antigravity`) arịrịọ n'ofe ndị ọbịa niile (1-12) tupu 429 laghachi azụ na onye na-akpọ oku. A na-aza ahụ nke mefuru ya na mpaghara na iziga ọzọ (2 min, okpukpu abụọ ruo nkeji 30, ma ọ bụ ihe nlegharị anya nke mgbago elu), onye na-ahụ maka nkata anaghị amaliteghachi arịrịọ ahụ mgbe 429 gasịrị.                                                                                                                                  |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_ALLOW_SIGNATURE_BYPASS`            | `0`                                                 | `open-sse/translator/request/openai-to-gemini.ts`           | Kwe ka onye ntụgharị akwụkwọ arịrịọ Antigravity ka ọ hapụ nnwapụta mbinye aka CLI ya siri ike mgbe elu na-ajụ ezigbo mbinye aka (ụdị debug/antiquated-CLI ). Enweghị efu na-eme ka ngafe ahụ dị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `AGY_TOKEN_FILE`                                | `~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/antigravity-oauth-token` | `src/app/api/providers/agy-auth/apply-local/route.ts`       | Wepu ụzọ faịlụ Antigravity CLI (agy) maka mbubata nbanye mpaghara na-achọpụta onwe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

### OAuth CLI Bridge (N'ime)

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe  | Ọdabara         | Isi mmalite faịlụ               | Nkọwa                                             |
| ------------------- | --------------- | ------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`  | chọpụta akpaaka | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Ihe nkesa URL maka akwa mmiri CLI↔OmniRoute auth. |
| `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`   | _(Edobeghi)_    | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Ihe nrịbama maka àkwà mmiri CLI.                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID` | `cli`           | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Onye ọrụ ID maka nnọkọ akwa mmiri CLI.            |
| `SERVER_URL`        | _(Edobeghi)_    | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Aha nke aha maka `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`.              |
| `CLI_TOKEN`         | _(Edobeghi)_    | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Aha nke aha maka `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`.               |
| `CLI_USER_ID`       | _(Edobeghi)_    | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Aha nke aha maka `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID`.             |

## 11. OAuth nzere onye na-enye

Asambodo arụnyere maka** mmepe localhost** . Maka mbugharị ndị dịpụrụ adịpụ, debanye aha nke gị na njikwa onye nrụpụta nke ọ bụla.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                          | Onye na-enye                  | Ihe ndetu                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLAUDE_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | Koodu Claude (Anthropic)      | Onye ahịa ọha - ọ nweghị ihe nzuzo achọrọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_REDIRECT_URI`                  | koodu Claude                  | Wepu redirect URI. Ihe ndabara: `https://platform.claude.com/oauth/code/callback`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CODEX_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                     | Codex / OpenAI                | Onye ahịa ọha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `GEMINI_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | Gemini (Google)               | Chọrọ ndakọrịta `_SECRET`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `GEMINI_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                | Gemini (Google)               | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `KIMI_CODING_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`               | Kimi coding (Moonshot)        | Onye ahịa ọha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `MUSE_CODE_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                 | Koodu Muse (Meta)             | Mweghara nhọrọ maka ọha Muse CLI ngwaọrụ-asọpụta onye ahịa id. Hapụ ahazighị ka iji onye ahịa ọhaneze esiri esi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`               | Antigravity (Google)          | Chọrọ ndakọrịta `_SECRET`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`           | Antigravity (Google)          | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | GitHub Copilot                | Onye ahịa ọha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `GHE_COPILOT_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`               | GHE Copilot                   | Mweghara nhọrọ maka GitHub Enterprise Copilot's OAuth id ahịa. Daghachi na ndabara ọhaneze `GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` mgbe edobereghị ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `COPILOT_INTEGRATION_ID`                    | GitHub Copilot                | Mwechapụ nhọrọ maka GitHub Copilot ndị ahịa mwekota ID ezigara na `Copilot-Integration-Id` na `Editor-Plugin-Version` nkụnye eji isi mee. Ihe ndabara na `copilot-developer-cli`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `WINDSURF_API_KEY`                          | Windsurf / Devin (v3.8)       | ndaghachi azụ azụ API nke `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts` na-eji mgbe enweghị nzere njikọ ọ bụla dị. Nhọrọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CLI_DEVIN_BIN`                             | Devin CLI (v3.8)              | Ụzọ omenala gaa na ọnụọgụ abụọ CLI Devin (`devin`). `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts` edozira ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                | GitLab Duo (v3.8)             | OAuth onye ahịa ID maka GitLab Duo. Debanye aha ngwa na `https://gitlab.com/-/profile/applications` na redirect URI `<NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL>/callback` na scopes `api, read_user, openid, profile, email`. Daghachi azụ na `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`            | GitLab Duo (v3.8)             | OAuth nzuzo nzuzo maka GitLab Duo. Nhọrọ - PKCE eruba anaghị achọ ihe nzuzo. Daghachi azụ na `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `GITLAB_DUO_BASE_URL`                       | GitLab Duo (v3.8)             | Wepu GitLab base URL ( GitLab ji aka ya kwado). Ihe ndabara na `https://gitlab.com`. Daghachi azụ na `GITLAB_BASE_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `GITLAB_BASE_URL`                           | GitLab Duo (v3.8)             | Ndaghachi azụ azụ maka `GITLAB_DUO_BASE_URL`. A na-eji ya mgbe ụdị `_DUO_` adịghị edozi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | GitLab Duo (v3.8)             | Ndaghachi azụ azụ maka `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` nke `src/lib/oauth/constants/oauth.ts` riri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                | GitLab Duo (v3.8)             | Ndaghachi azụ azụ maka `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET` nke `src/lib/oauth/constants/oauth.ts` riri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `QODER_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                 | Qoder                         | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `QODER_OAUTH_AUTHORIZE_URL`                 | Qoder                         | Tọọ iji mee ka Qoder OAuth nwee ike.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `QODER_OAUTH_TOKEN_URL`                     | Qoder                         | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `QODER_OAUTH_USERINFO_URL`                  | Qoder                         | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `QODER_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                     | Qoder                         | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `QODER_PERSONAL_ACCESS_TOKEN`               | Qoder                         | Kpọmkwem igodo API ndaghachi azụ (gafere OAuth).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `QODER_CLI_WORKSPACE`                       | Qoder                         | Oghere ọrụ ID maka Qoder CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_QODER_WORKSPACE`                 | Qoder                         | Aha aha maka `QODER_CLI_WORKSPACE`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `QODER_CLI_CONFIG_DIR`                      | Qoder                         | Wepu Qoder CLI config dir (nnọkọ PAT dịpụrụ adịpụ, na-ezere imechi nbanye ihe nchọgharị).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `QODER_CLI_MAX_CONCURRENCY`                 | Qoder                         | Nke kacha na-agba ọsọ n'otu oge qodercli (bipụta spawns ma ọ bụ ACP kpaliri); mmezi arịrịọ kwụ n'ahịrị FIFO. Ewepụghị (ndapụta) = enweghị okpu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `QODER_CLI_QUEUE_TIMEOUT_MS`                | Qoder                         | Ogologo oge ole ka arịrịọ Qoder na-echere oghere qodercli (naanị na-eji okpu setịpụ) tupu ọ daa na 429 `qodercli_busy` + `Retry-After`. `120000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `QODER_CLI_TRANSPORT`                       | Qoder                         | `print` (ndabere) spawns `qodercli --print` kwa arịrịọ; `acp` na-edobe otu usoro `qodercli --acp` na-adịgide adịgide kwa PAT.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `QODER_CLI_ACP_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`             | Qoder                         | njem ACP : gbuo usoro qodercli na-adịgide adịgide mgbe oge a na-adịghị arụ ọrụ gasịrị. `600000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `QODER_CLI_ACP_STARTUP_TIMEOUT_MS`          | Qoder                         | njem ACP : njedebe maka qodercli ịza ACP `initialize`. `60000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `QODER_CLI_ACP_MAX_SESSIONS_PER_PROCESS`    | Qoder                         | njem ACP : megharịa usoro qodercli na-adịgide adịgide mgbe ọtụtụ nnọkọ a gachara. `500`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `BLACKBOX_WEB_VALIDATED_TOKEN`              | Weebụ Blackbox                | N'ihu `tk` akara izipu ka `validated` na `/api/chat`. Achọrọ mgbe Blackbox na-amanye ndakọrịta token; ma ọ bụghị ya, OmniRoute na-adaghachi na UUID na-enweghị usoro. Lee mbipụta #2252.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `VISION_BRIDGE_BASE_URL`                    | Vision Bridge nche okporo ụzọ | OpenAI-dakọtara base URL maka oku ọhụụ-akwa na-abụghị Anthropic. Edebere na ihe nketa OpenAI URL env ma ọ bụ api.openai.com. Tụtụ aka na OmniRoute's `/v1` self-loop ma ọ bụ ihe ọ bụla OpenAI-compat endpoint (Gemini OpenAI-compat, OpenRouter). mbipụta #2232. Mgbe URL bụ OmniRoute nke onwe `/v1`, sub-arịrịọ nkọwa na-eziga `x-omniroute-admission-bypass: internal` wee gosi site na mkpebi onwe-loop nzere (`sk_omniroute` sentinel na mpaghara mode, ma ọ bụ `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY` / `ROUTER_API_KEY` — #1350) ya mere `REQUIRE_API_KEY=true` deployments na-arụ ọrụ. |
| `VISION_BRIDGE_API_KEY`                     | Vision Bridge nche okporo ụzọ | igodo API maka URL dị n'elu. Na-ewepụ onye na-eweta OpenAI / Google env vars maka oku ọhụụ-àkwà mmiri na-abụghị nke Anthropic. ụdị Anthropic na-edobe ụzọ igodo Anthropic raara onwe ha nye. mbipụta #2232.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_VISION_BRIDGE_NEGATIVE_CACHE_MS` | Vision Bridge nche okporo ụzọ | Ogologo oge ole ka a na-echekwa nsonaazụ ntụgharị "enweghị onye ga-eji eme ihe, na ms (ndabere `30000`). Ụkpụrụ ezighi ezi ma ọ bụ nke na-adịghị mma na-adaghachi na ndabara; `0` gbanyụọ cache adịghị mma. Isi mmalite: `src/lib/guardrails/visionBridgeRouter.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

> [!WARNING]
>
> 1. Gaa na [nzere Google Cloud Console →](https://console.cloud.google.com/apis/credentials) 2. Mepụta OAuth 2.0 Client ID (ụdị: "ngwa Weebụ") 3. Tinye URL nkesa gị dị ka ikike redirect URI 4. Dochie ụkpụrụ nzere na `.env`.
