## 12. Onye na-enye ọrụ-onye nnọchi anya na-agbagha

Wepụ nkụnye eji isi mee `User-Agent` ezigara onye ọ bụla na-eweta elu. A na-edozi nke a n'ike n'ike n'oge ọ na-agba ọsọ site na klaasị ndị mmebe:

```
process.env[`${PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`]
```

> **Isi mmalite:** `open-sse/executors/base.ts` → `buildHeaders()`

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe | Uru ndabara | Mgbe imelite |
| -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT` | `claude-cli/2.1.280 (external, cli)` | Mgbe Anthropic wepụtara ụdị CLI ọhụrụ |
| `CLAUDE_DISABLE_TOOL_NAME_CLOAK` | `false` | `executors/base.ts` + `executors/cliproxyapi.ts` | Tọọ na `1`/`true` ka ibugharịa aha ngwa ngwa ngwa ndị ọzọ na Anthropic n'ụzọ abụọ a na-ejikọta mmadụ ( OAuth ala na CLIPROxyAPI). Site na ndabara, onye mmebe ahụ na-akọwapụta aha ndị na-abụghị Claude-Code ngwaọrụ (Claude Code canonical mapping ebe otu dị, ma ọ bụghị PascalCase) wee tụgharịa ha na nzaghachi site na `_toolNameMap`, yabụ anaghị ajụ ihe eji arụ ọrụ agwọ_case dị ka akara mkpịsị aka ndị ahịa ndị ọzọ. Na-emezigharị naanị. |
| `CODEX_USER_AGENT` | `codex-cli/0.155.0 (Windows 10.0.26200; x64)` | Mgbe OpenAI na-emelite Codex CLI |
| `CODEX_CLIENT_VERSION` | `0.155.0` | Wepụ ụdị onye ahịa Codex na-adabereghị na eriri UA zuru ezu |
| `GROK_CLI_CLIENT_VERSION` | `1.0.41` | Wepụ `x-grok-client-version` maka ntinye, mmekọrịta nlereanya, na koodu OAuth ngwaọrụ, gbakwunyere Grok CLI Onye ọrụ-Nnọchite anya maka ntinye na mmekọrịta ụdị. Nabata SemVer na nhọrọ prerelease/build metadata, ruo mkpụrụedemede 128 ka ewepụchara oghere ọcha. Ụkpụrụ atọghị, nke efu, adịghị mma, ma ọ bụ akara nwere nkwụsịtụ na-eji ntụtụ ebugoro. Ntinye njupụta nke onye ọrụ-onye nnọchite anya na-ejigide ụzọ. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_VERSION` | `2.1.280` | Wepụ ụdị koodu Claude akpọsara na-adabereghị na `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT`. Anthropic ọnụ ụzọ ụfọdụ ụdị na uru a (#12417). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_BUILD_REVISION` | `1e2` | Wepụ suffix agwa 3 OmniRoute gbakwunyere na `cc_version=` na ngọngọ ịgba ụgwọ Claude. Bump n'akụkụ `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_VERSION` - ịpịnye naanị ụdị ahụ na-akpọsa ụzọ `version.revision` enweghị ezigbo ọnụọgụ abụọ. |
| `GITHUB_COPILOT_CLI_VERSION` | `1.0.91` | Wepụ ụdị Copilot CLI akpọsara na-adabereghị na `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` |
| `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` | `GitHubCopilotChat/0.54.0` | Mgbe GitHub Copilot Chat na-emelite |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_USER_AGENT` | `antigravity/2.0.1 darwin/arm64` | Mgbe Antigravity IDE na-emelite |
| `KIRO_USER_AGENT` | `AWS-SDK-JS/3.0.0 kiro-ide/1.0.0` | Mgbe Kiro IDE na-emelite |
| `KIRO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` | `kiro-cli` | Wepụ Kiro social device-koodu `clientId` (ID ọha) |
| `KIRO_VERIFY_FULL_CRC` | `false` | Nbanye: ozi CRC zuru oke n'otu-frame na iyi mmemme Kiro (mezie iyi ndị mebiri emebi) |
| `KIRO_CREDIT_PRICE_USD` | `0.02` | USD kwa Kiro kredit; na-atụgharị `meteringEvent` kredit ka ọ bụrụ ọnụ ahịa arịrịọ ọ bụla |
| `KIRO_REQUIRE_CREDITS` | `false` | Daa nzaghachi Kiro nke na-akọ enweghị kredit meter kama idekọ ya akwụghị ụgwọ |
| `QODER_USER_AGENT` | `Qoder-Cli` | Mgbe Qoder CLI na-emelite |
| `CURSOR_USER_AGENT` | `Cursor/3.3` | Mgbe Cursor emelitere |

> [!TIP]
> Ị nwere ike ịgbakwunye onye ọrụ-onye nnọchi anya gabigara maka** nke ọ bụla** onye na-eweta na-eji ụkpụrụ `{PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`. Onye mmebe ahụ na-ewuli aha env var nke ọma.

---

## 13. CLI ndakọrịta mkpịsị aka mkpịsị aka

Mgbe enyere ya aka, OmniRoute na-atụgharị ihe nkụnye eji isi HTTP na mpaghara ahụ JSON ka ọ dabara na mbinye aka nke ngwa CLI gọọmentị. Nke a na-ebelata ohere nke ịdekọ akaụntụ mgbe ị na-echekwa proxy IP gị.

**Isi mmalite:** `open-sse/config/cliFingerprints.ts`, `open-sse/executors/base.ts`

### Onye na-eweta ya

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe       | Nkwalite | Mmetụta                          |
| ------------------------ | -------- | -------------------------------- |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CODEX`       | `=1`     | Mimics Codex CLI mbinye aka      |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLAUDE`      | `=1`     | Mimics Claude Koodu mbinye aka   |
| `CLI_COMPAT_GITHUB`      | `=1`     | Mimics GitHub Copilot mbinye aka |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ANTIGRAVITY` | `=1`     | Mimics Antigravity mbinye aka    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CURSOR`      | `=1`     | Mimics Cursor mbinye aka         |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KIMI_CODING` | `=1`     | Mimics Kimi Ntinye mbinye aka    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KILOCODE`    | `=1`     | mbinye aka koodu Mimics Kilo     |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLINE`       | `=1`     | Mimics Cline mbinye aka          |

### Gburugburu ụwa

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe | Nkwalite | Mmetụta                                                              |
| ------------------ | -------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ALL`   | `=1`     | Kwado ndakọrịta akara mkpịsị aka maka** niile** ndị na-enye otu oge. |

### Kimi Ndokwa CLI njirimara na-ewepụ

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe      | Ọdabara            | Isi mmalite faịlụ                        | Nkọwa                                                   |
| ----------------------- | ------------------ | ---------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------- |
| `KIMI_CLI_VERSION`      | `1.36.0`           | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Wepụ ụdị Kimi CLI ezitere n'oge oku OAuth/API.          |
| `KIMI_CODING_DEVICE_ID` | _(dabara ejidere)_ | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Wepụ ngwaọrụ Kimi eweghara ID ejiri na nkụnye ndị ahịa. |

> [!NOTE]
> Njirimara a na-arụ ọrụ n'akụkụ ihe nbibi nke onye ọrụ-onye ọrụ (§12). Sistemụ akara mkpịsị aka ahụ na-ejikwa ịtụ nkụnye eji isi mee na ịtụ mpaghara ahụ, ebe onye nnọchi anya onye ọrụ na-ejide eriri UA akọwapụtara. Enwere ike ịgbanye ha abụọ n'onwe ha.

---

## 14. API Key enye

igodo API maka ndị na-eweta na-eji nyocha ozugbo.** Nhazi ọkacha mmasị:** Dashboard → Ndị na-enye → Tinye igodo API.

Ịtọ ntọala site na mgbanwe gburugburu ebe obibi bụ ihe ọzọ maka nnyefe Docker ma ọ bụ enweghị isi.

Ụkpụrụ amatara: `{PROVIDER_ID}_API_KEY`

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe | Onye na-enye                                            |
| ------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------- |
| `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY` | DeepSeek                                                |
| `NVIDIA_API_KEY`   | NVIDIA NIM                                              |
| `JINA_AI_API_KEY`  | Jina AI (Ntọala API + Reader fallback)                  |
| `JINA_API_KEY`     | Jina AI (utu aha maka `JINA_AI_API_KEY`)                |
| `GEMINI_API_KEY`   | Gemini (Google AI Studio) agbakwunyere + nkata fallback |
| `GOOGLE_API_KEY`   | Gemini (utu aha maka `GEMINI_API_KEY`)                  |

> [!NOTE]
> E wepụrụ ndenye `${PROVIDER}_API_KEY` static maka Groq, xAI, Mistral, mgbagwoju anya, Together AI, Fireworks, Cerebras, Cohere, Nebius, na Qianfan na v3.8.0 n'ihi na oge ịgba ọsọ anaghị agụkwa ha - ndị na-enye ọrụ na-adabere nanị na Dashboard / `data/provider-credentials.json` / DB ezoro ezo. Hụ ngalaba _Audit: Wepụrụ / Nwụrụ Anwụ Variables_ na ala nke akwụkwọ a maka ụzọ ọpụpụ.

> [!TIP]
> A na-echekwa igodo ndị edobere site na Dashboard na SQLite wee buru ụzọ na-agbanwe mgbanwe gburugburu ebe obibi.
>
> **Jina:** ntinye `jina-ai/…`, rerank, nkewa, ngalaba, na `jina-search` eme** ọ bụghị** kwụọ igodo ụyọkọ env mgbe njikọ `jina-ai` (ma ọ bụ nkekọrịta `jina-reader`) dashboard dị - `getProviderCredentials` bụ nke mbụ. A na-eji `JINA_AI_API_KEY` / `JINA_API_KEY` naanị mgbe enweghị igodo dashboard enwere ike iji ya dị. Ndekọ oku na-ekwu na ọdịda env dị ka `connection_id=env:JINA_AI_API_KEY`. Kaadị Reader (`jina-reader`, `r.jina.ai`) anaghị ejere `/v1/embeddings` ma ọ bụ `/v1/rerank`.
>
> **Gemini:** `gemini/gemini-embedding-2` (alias `google/gemini-embedding-2`) na-ebu ụzọ jikọọ dashboard `gemini`. A na-eji `GEMINI_API_KEY` / `GOOGLE_API_KEY` naanị mgbe enweghị igodo dashboard enwere ike iji ya dị. Ndekọ oku na-ekwu na ọdịda env dị ka `connection_id=env:GEMINI_API_KEY`. Okporo ụzọ multimodal nke ala na-eji `x-goog-api-key` megide `:embedContent` / `:batchEmbedContents` - N OpenAI `input` ihe na-aghọ N vectors.

---

## 15. Ntọala nkwụsị oge

Ụkpụrụ niile dị** millisekọnd** . Mkpebi etiti etiti na `src/shared/utils/runtimeTimeouts.ts`.

### Ọchịchị Oge agwụla

```
REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS (global override)
├─→ FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS (upstream provider calls, default: 600000)
│   ├─→ FETCH_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   ├─→ FETCH_BODY_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   ├─→ TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   │   └── TLS_FIRST_BYTE_WATCHDOG_MS (independent, default: 10000)
│   ├── RESPONSES_FIRST_BYTE_TIMEOUT_MS (independent, default: 15000)
│   ├── OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MS (independent, default: 0 = off, suggested: 30000)
│   ├── OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MAX_ROTATIONS (independent, default: 2)
│   ├── FETCH_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS (independent, default: 30000)
│   └── FETCH_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (independent, default: 4000)
├─→ STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, default: 600000)
├─→ STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (independent; unset: 1260000, raised by declared budgets; set: hard cap; 0 disables)
├─→ STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, default: 80000)
├─→ STREAM_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS (caps adaptive readiness extensions, default: 180000)
└─→ API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, default: 30000)
    ├─→ API_BRIDGE_SERVER_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS (derived, default: 300000)
    ├── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS (default: 60000)
    ├── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (default: 5000)
    └── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_SOCKET_TIMEOUT_MS (default: 0 = disabled)
```

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                              | Ọdabara                                                           | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ----------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`                            | _(Edobeghi)_                                                      | Ụzọ mkpirisi zuru ụwa ọnụ - na-ewepụ ma `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` na `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS` ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                              | `600000`                                                          | Ngụkọta oge arịrịọ HTTP maka oku ndị na-eweta n'elu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`                        | `600000`                                                          | Oke juu n'etiti bytes nke dị n'elu mmiri tupu ite ime. Ụdị echiche gbatịrị agbatị anaghị akwụsịtụ > 90s.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `1260000`                                                         | Ogologo iyi SSE kacha arụ ọrụ oge ndụ; anaghị emegharị na bytes elu ma ọ nọọrọ onwe ya na `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`. Mgbe edobereghị, ndabara bụ `timeoutMs` nke kachasị n'ụdị zqc na ndekọ (1200000, Codex) gbakwunyere oke 60000, yana ebumnuche na-ekwupụta mmefu ego ogologo oge (njikọ `timeoutMs`, wee kwa-ụdị, wee nye ọkwa-ọkwa, dịka GLM's 3000000) na-enweta mmefu ego ahụ gbakwunyere 60000. Uru dị mma bụ okpu siri ike maka ebumnuche ọ bụla nke ekwuputara mmefu ego enweghị ike ibuli. Tọọ `0` iji gbanyụọ maka ebumnuche ọ bụla.                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_SSE_COMMENTS`                        | _(enweghị nkwarụ)_                                                | Ma OmniRoute nwere ike wepụta ahịrị nkọwa SSE `:` (dịka ọmụmaatụ nku obi `: keepalive` na `x-omniroute-*` metadata trailers). Agbanyụrụ site na ndabara (#10524) ebe ọ bụ na ndị ahịa OpenAI dakọtara siri ike JSON.parse ahịrị SSE ọ bụla wee daa na nkwupụta `:` ; `data:` obi mgbawa anaghị emetụta. Tọọ `on`/`true`/`1`/`yes` ịweghachi azụ. Nke `open-sse/utils/sseHeartbeat.ts` na-eji .                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `SSE_COALESCE_MS`                               | `0` (apụ)                                                         | Wepụ: jikọta mmemme Claude `content_block_delta` na-esochi (otu ngọngọ index na ụdị delta) ezigara ndị ahịa Claude, na-agbanye opekata mpe N ms ọ bụla. Enweghị efu; `signature_delta` na ihe omume oke anaghị ejikọta ọnụ. Na-emetụta ụdị delta ọ bụla ma ọ bụrụ na edobere mgbanwe kwa ụdị n'okpuru. Eji `open-sse/utils/sseDeltaCoalesce.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `SSE_COALESCE_THINKING_MS`                      | `0` (apụ)                                                         | Ntugharị kwa ụdị nke `SSE_COALESCE_MS` maka `thinking_delta`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `SSE_COALESCE_TEXT_MS`                          | `0` (apụ)                                                         | Ntugharị kwa ụdị nke `SSE_COALESCE_MS` maka `text_delta`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `SSE_COALESCE_INPUT_JSON_MS`                    | `0` (apụ)                                                         | Ntugharị kwa ụdị nke `SSE_COALESCE_MS` maka `input_json_delta`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `SSE_FRAME_COALESCE_MS`                         | `0` (apụ)                                                         | Wepụ: ogbe SSE ezigara n'ime N ms n'ime otu dee onye ahịa. Agba nke mbụ na okpokolo agba (`[DONE]`, `message_stop`, `error`, `response.completed`/`failed`/`incomplete`) anaghị egbu oge; na batching na, ITL na-atụle oghere ogbe. Eji `open-sse/utils/sseOutputSink.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `SSE_STALL_WATCHDOG`                            | `timer`                                                           | Mmejuputa Byte-stall elele maka nzaghachi mgbasa ozi. `timer` na-eweghachi otu ngụ oge n'otu ntabi anya elu (ọkụ kpọmkwem na mmefu ego ụlọ ahịa); `interval` na-edobe otu nlele kwa iyi na oge `clamp(budget/4, 1s, 5s)`, yabụ ụlọ ahịa na-ere ọkụ n'ime oge + mmefu ego. Eji `open-sse/utils/streamStallWatchdog.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS`                   | `80000`                                                           | Oge iji nweta mmemme na-abụghị ping SSE mbụ. Na-eketa `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` mgbe atọrọ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `STREAM_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `180000`                                                          | mpio nkwado mmemme izizi kachasị maka arịrịọ nkwanye ugwu buru ibu, ngwa ọrụ dị arọ ma ọ bụ nnukwu ebumnuche.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_POLICY_ENABLED`           | `true`                                                            | Gbuo-mgbanwe maka `/goal` heuristic. Tọọ `false`/`0`/`off` iji gbanyụọ nchọpụta zuru oke - oge nkwụsị njikere na mgbake iyi anaghị ebuli elu site na arịrịọ body/headers, na-ebelata mmụba oge nkwụsị nke ndị ahịa na-achịkwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS` | `600000`                                                          | Window ịdị njikere mmemme mbụ maka ndị nnọchite `/goal` achọpụtara na-agba ma ọ bụ arịrịọ manyere `x-omniroute-agent-goal`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_STREAM_RECOVERY`          | `true`                                                            | Kwado mgbake mmiri mbụ na-akpaghị aka maka ndị ọrụ `/goal` achọpụtara. Tọọ `false`/`0`/`off` iji gbanyụọ nbanye nke ebumnuche-kpọmkwem. Nke a nwere ike naanị ADD mgbake n'elu ndabara onye ọrụ - ọ naghị ewepụ mwepu ntọala `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED`/DB doro anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_DROP_NONSTANDARD_EVENTS`       | `true`                                                            | Wepụ mmemme `codex.*` SSE na-abụghị ọkọlọtọ (dịka `codex.rate_limits`) nke na-eji 502. ON dapụtara (#11014) mebie OpenAI SDK's `responses.stream()`. Tọọ `0`/`false`/`no`/`off` ka ibugharịa ha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS`                  | _(Edobeghi)_                                                      | Wepụ ụgbọ njem ihe nkesa Codex. ebe njedebe WebSocket (`ws://`/`wss://`) nke mpaghara `codex app-server` sidecar. Mgbe ejikọtara ya na token, a na-ebugharị arịrịọ Codex n'elu JSON-RPC n'akụkụ ụgbọ ala kama azịza HTTP API. Enwere ike ịtọlite kwa-njikọ site na `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerUrl`. Eji `open-sse/executors/codex/appServerConfig.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN`            | _(Edobeghi)_                                                      | Enyere akara capability/bearer inline n'ime ihe nkesa ngwa. Otu-njikọ gabigara: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerToken`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN_FILE`       | _(Edobeghi)_                                                      | Ụzọ faịlụ na-ejide akara ike-ihe nkesa (site na `codex app-server --ws-token-file`). A na-eji ya mgbe emebere `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN`. Otu-njikọ mebie: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerTokenFile`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_CWD`                 | `/tmp`                                                            | Akwụkwọ ndekọ aha na-arụ ọrụ ntụgharị ngwa-ihe nkesa na-abanye. Njikọ ọ bụla mebie: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerCwd`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_APPROVAL`            | _(Edobeghi)_                                                      | Amụma nnabata gafere na ntụgharị ngwa-nkesa (dịka `never`, `on-request`). Otu-njikọ mebie: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerApprovalPolicy`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_SANDBOX`             | _(Edobeghi)_                                                      | Amụma igbe igbe gafere na ntụgharị ngwa-nkesa (dịka `read-only`, `workspace-write`, `danger-full-access`). Mgbe ewepụtaghị ihe mmebe ahụ na `workspace-write` (agbasiri ike; na mbụ `danger-full-access`). Otu-njikọ mebie: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerSandbox`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_AUTO_APPROVE`        | `false`                                                           | Na-akwado nkwado nke ngwa-ihe nkesa na-akpaghị aka (command/file/permission na onye ọbịa). Gbanyụọ na ndabara - a na-agọnarị mkpali onwe ya; Oku ngwa ngwa ngwa anaghị emetụta (ha na-eme njem `item/tool/call` passthrough dị iche). Nabata `true`/`1`/`yes`. Otu-njikọ mebie: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerAutoApprove`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `FETCH_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Oge ịnata nkụnye eji isi mee nzaghachi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_FETCH_START_TIMEOUT_CAP_MS`          | `110000`                                                          | Oke nchere maka isi okwu nzaghachi na arịrịọ nkwanye (ms). `0` gbanyụọ okpu a; onye na-eweta nke ọma `fetchStartTimeoutCapMs` na-ebute ụzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `30000` (30s)                                                     | Opekempe nzaghachi-mmalite nchere (ms) maka mbọ onye nnọchi anya ọ bụla. Oge nkwụsịtụ na-emegharị otu ugboro na oghere ọhụrụ; tọọ `0` iji gbanyụọ agbụ ma jigide omume gara aga.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`     | `300000` (minti 5)                                                | Nzaghachi-mmalite ala (ms) maka mbọ ọnweghị onye nnọchi anya nke onye lekwasịrị anya ya bụ private/LAN/loopback (dịka ọmụmaatụ `localhost`, `host.docker.internal`, `192.168.x.x`), yabụ anaghị ebipụ mmalite oyi Ollama site na ndabara 30s dị n'elu. Na-arụ ọrụ dị ka ala: nchere dị irè bụ ibu nke uru a na `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`; `0` ma ọ bụ uru na-ezighi ezi na-ewepụ ala mpaghara. Ọnweghị mmetuta ndị nnabata anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_RESPONSE_RETRY_TIMEOUT_MS`    | `600000` (10 min)                                                 | Ulo elu (ms) maka mgbali RETRY ọhụrụ-socket ka agbachara mbọ nzaghachi-mmalite oge n'elu (#13703). Na-emetụta naanị mgbe onye na-akpọ oku etinyelarị akara ngosi njedebe nke ya (njikọ edoziziri / ihe nlereanya / onye na-eweta /`FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` cascade); mgbaàmà ahụ bụ ezigbo njikọ na ọkụ na mbụ n'ụzọ a chọrọ, yabụ nke a bụ ihe nkwụghachi azụ na-emesapụ aka karịa okpu dị larịị - na-enweghị ya, nnwale ahụ ejirikwa windo `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS` dị mkpụmkpụ ahụ dị ka mkpebi agbakọtara na 504'd ụdị echiche nwayọ nwayọ-TTFB. A naghị ekwe ka ọ dị n'okpuru ala dị larịị n'elu; mgbe onye na-akpọ oku na-enyeghị mgbaama njedebe oge ọ bụla, ịnwale ọzọ na-eme ka ala dị larịị agbanweghị. |
| `FETCH_BODY_TIMEOUT_MS`                         | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Oge iji nweta ahụ nzaghachi zuru oke.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `FETCH_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `30000`                                                           | oge nguzobe njikọ TCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `FETCH_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`                    | `4000`                                                            | Nọgide na-adị ndụ oghere oge nkwụsị ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS`                         | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | TLS akara mkpisiaka proxy (wreq-js) agwụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `TLS_FIRST_BYTE_WATCHDOG_MS`                    | `10000`                                                           | Na-agbatị oge-na-mbụ-byte na ahụ wreq-js TLS-mkpịsị aka mkpịsị aka ahụ kpọmkwem; `TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS` naanị enweghị ike ijide ahụ kwụsịrị ebe ọ na-edozi ozugbo ndị nkụnye eji isi mee bịarutere (#12656). Oge nkwụsị na-akagbu onye na-agụ wreq wee daa azụ na onye na-ezipụ direct/proxy ; `0` gbanyụọ onye nche.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `RESPONSES_FIRST_BYTE_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `15000`                                                           | OpenCode executor naanị, na naanị mgbe ọkọlọtọ njirimara `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_STALL_ROTATION` dị (nke ndabara): na-egbochi ichere byte ahụ nke mbụ nke nzaghachi nzaghachi mgbe ndị nkụnye eji isi mee ya (#13484). A na-emepe iyi nzaghachi na `response.created`, ya mere ịgbachi nkịtị gafere windo a bụ ụlọ ahịa: a na-eme ka akaụntụ ahụ dị jụụ na arịrịọ ahụ na-atụgharị na akaụntụ ọzọ otu ugboro; ụlọ ahịa nke abụọ na-ada ngwa ngwa. `0` gbanyụọ onye nche ọbụlagodi na ọkọlọtọ na-agbanye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MS`            | `0` (= kwụsịrị)                                                   | OpenCode executor naanị: kpachie nchere maka isi okwu nzaghachi elu na oku nzaghachi na-ebugharị mgbe akaụntụ ọzọ ka dị - arịrịọ kwụ n'ahịrị kwụsịrị ichere wee gaa n'ihu kama ijide windo nkụnye eji isi mee n'uju. Naanị oku nzaghachi agbasa ozi na-emetụta (Oyi nzaghachi na-emepe na `response.created`, ya mere ịgbachi nkịtị enwere kwụ n'ahịrị, ọ bụghị ọgbọ); emecha nkata na oku na-abụghị iyi, na mpio ahụ na-ebelata ụlọ mmalite mmalite dị mma, ọ dịghị mgbe ọ ga-agbatị ya. Uru atụnyere mgbe ị na-enyere: `30000`. `0` gbanyụọ.                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MAX_ROTATIONS` | `2`                                                               | OpenCode executor naanị: ugboro ole n'otu arịrịọ, ndị nkụnye eji isi mee echere n'elu nwere ike ịkwaga na akaụntụ ọzọ. Akaụntụ ikpeazụ fọdụrụ na-edobe windo nkụnye eji isi mee oge niile.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OPENCODE_PARK_AND_RESUME`                      | `false`                                                           | OpenCode executor naanị: jiri obi mgbawa dọba arịrịọ ahụ ka emechara 429s na-agafe agafe ugboro ugboro (ma ọ bụ akara akara ọdọ mmiri ọhụrụ), wee megharịa otu ụkwụ nwere ihe ruru akaụntụ usoro 3 kama ịwepụ ụgbọ mmiri niile (#13924). Gbanyụọ na ndabara: 429 ọ bụla na-atụgharị gaa na akaụntụ ọzọ dịka ọ dị na mbụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OPENCODE_PARK_REPLAY_SERVE_SETASIDE`           | `false`                                                           | OpenCode executor naanị: mgbe akaụntụ ọ bụla dị njikere na ogige ntụrụndụ na-amaliteghachi site na ebe nchekwa proxy-ajụ, jeere ha ozi kama ịmegharị ihe ọ bụla (#14851, sochiri ruo #14750). Gbanyụọ na ndabara: ụkwụ na-emegharị ahụ tọgbọ chakoo n'ọnọdụ ahụ, dịka ọ dị na mbụ #14750.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `STREAM_READINESS_STALL_RETRY`                  | `false`                                                           | Mkparịta ụka gụgharia naanị: mgbe ahụ mbụ dị elu kwụsịrị tupu ya emee mmemme enwere ike, wepụta otu mbọ nke abụọ site n'otu ụzọ ntụgharị ahụ yana otu mmefu ego njikere na enweghị ntaramahụhụ akaụntụ. Gbanyụọ na ndabara: ahụ nke mbụ kwụsịrị na-ada arịrịọ na-enweghị nnwale ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OPENCODE_POOL_RESELECT`                        | `false`                                                           | OpenCode executor naanị: mgbe 429 sitere na onye na-eweta egress-bucketed na akaụntụ proxy-enweghị n'okpuru ọnọdụ ọdọ mmiri ambient, jụọ njikọ ọdọ mmiri maka onye ọzọ maka mbọ ọzọ kama ịnwale otu adreesị egress ahụ. Iwu, anaghị ewepu: ọdọ mmiri ike gwụrụ na-edobe omume dị ugbu a. Gbanyụọ na ndabara: 429 ọ bụla na-atụgharị gaa na akaụntụ ọzọ dịka ọ dị na mbụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OPENCODE_POOL_STRAIN_MARKER_PATH`              | _(Edobeghi)_                                                      | OpenCode executor naanị: mebie ụzọ nke ọdọ mmiri-strain markker na-agụ tupu adọba ụgbọala (`{since, reason, ttl_s}`, ndabara `/tmp/opencode-pool-strain.json`, #13924). Ogige ihe nrịbama ọhụrụ na-agụghị; anọghị ya ma ọ bụ stale daa azụ na counter gbawara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS`                   | `30000`                                                           | Oge nkwụsị proxy hop maka arịrịọ àkwà mmiri `/v1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`        | `5000`                                                            | Bridge na-adị ndụ oge agwụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_SOCKET_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `0`                                                               | Ọgbụgba oghere oghere (nkwarụ 0 = ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

| `OMNIROUTE_DEFAULT_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `120000`                                                          | Ndaghachi nke `src/shared/utils/fetchTimeout.ts` na-eji mgbe emebere `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_PROBE_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `8000`                                                            | Oge nkwụsị (ms) maka ntọala `validationRead` na `modelsProbe` na `src/shared/network/safeOutboundFetch.ts`. Welie maka akara ngwụcha nwayọ (Cerebras, Cloudflare AI, Groq) iji gbochie ịkụnye n'etiti active/error na dashboard. Daghachi na 8000ms maka ezighi ezi (< 1000) ma ọ bụ ụkpụrụ na-abụghị ọnụọgụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

Ntugharị agbagharị agbagharị agbagharị ga-adarịrị tupu oge client/agent (~ 30s) yabụ ndị na-akpọ oku na-ahụ ọdịda kpọmkwem ọsọ ọsọ kama ịnọpụ oge na-agbago elu. Edebere na `29000` ka ọ na-ebu ụzọ gbaa ọkụ mgbe niile.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

| `OMNIROUTE_CLAUDE_TLS_GRACE_MS`                 | `10000`                                                           | Amara oge ngwụcha JS zuru oke agbakwunyere n'elu oge ngwụcha nke obodo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`                 | `30000`                                                           | Oge ngwụcha nke arịrịọ nwa afọ wreq-js (`perplexityTlsClient.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_TLS_GRACE_MS`                   | `10000`                                                           | Amara oge ngwụcha JS zuru oke agbakwunyere n'elu oge ngwụcha nke obodo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_SEARCH_HINT`                    | `0` (apụ)                                                         | agbakwunyere "I nwela ọchụchọ webụ arụnyere. Gbanyụọ na ndabara - Ọ na-achọ ihe mgbagwoju anya, na ahịrịokwu ahụ na-abanye na nzaghachi dị ka meta-nkọwa maka nzuzo ndị ahịa. Tọọ `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` iji weghachi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_GROK_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`                 | `60000`                                                           | Oge ngwụcha nke arịrịọ nwa afọ wreq-js (`grokTlsClient.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_GROK_TLS_GRACE_MS`                   | `10000`                                                           | Amara oge ngwụcha JS zuru oke agbakwunyere n'elu oge ngwụcha nke obodo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_NOTION_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `30000`                                                           | Nwa afọ wreq-js arịrịọ oge nkwụsị (`notionTlsClient.ts`); `notion-web` na-ebuli ya kwa arịrịọ `180000` ruo ogologo ọgbọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_NOTION_TLS_GRACE_MS`                 | `10000`                                                           | Amara oge ngwụcha JS zuru oke agbakwunyere n'elu oge ngwụcha nke obodo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_BROWSER_POOL`                        | `on`                                                              | Ọdọ mmiri ihe nchọgharị Playwright ekekọrịtara maka nkata kuki webụ na-akwado ihe nchọgharị (`browserPool.ts`); tọọ `off` iji gbanyụọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OBSCURA_BIN`                                   | `auto-detect`                                                     | Ụzọ na ọnụọgụ abụọ `obscura` ejiri dị ka injin mbụ site na ọdọ mmiri ihe nchọgharị na Cloudflare Playground executor (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`); achọpụtara na akpaghị aka site na PATH sistemu mgbe edobere ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OBSCURA_CDP_ENDPOINT`                          | _(Edobeghi)_                                                      | Tụtụ aka na Obscura (`http://host:port`) na-agba ọsọ ugbua kama ịmụba otu; modul enweghị usoro ahụ (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OBSCURA_PORT`                                  | `random free port`                                                | ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri doro anya maka `obscura serve` spawn; a na-ahọrọ ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri efu na-akpaghị aka mgbe ịtọghe ya (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `WEB_COOKIE_USE_BROWSER`                        | `0`                                                               | Họrọ arịrịọ nkata kuki webụ banye n'ụzọ nke ihe nchọgharị na-akwado (`browserBackedChat.ts`); `1` ka enyere.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `KIMI_WEB_BASE_URL`                             | `https://www.kimi.ai`                                             | Base URL maka webụ Kimi (international kimi.ai Connect-RPC) executor (`kimi-web.ts`); mebie naanị maka ebe ngwụcha mirror/proxy.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `KIMI_WEB_CHAT_URL`                             | `<KIMI_WEB_BASE_URL>/apiv2/kimi.gateway.chat.v1.ChatService/Chat` | Ebe ngwụcha nkata zuru oke maka onye ọrụ webụ Kimi (`kimi-web.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOGIN_BROWSER_PATH`                  | _(Achọpụtara ya na akpaghị aka)_                                  | Ụzọ na usoro Chrome/Edge nke enwere ike ime maka ntinye ihe nchọgharị Adobe Firefly mmekọrịta (`adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`); na-ewepụ nchọta akpaaka kwa-OS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_STANDALONE_DIR`                      | _.build/ nke kwụụrụ onwe ya pụta_                                 | Mwepụta oge wuo maka akwụkwọ ndekọ aha mmepụta nke kwụụrụ onwe ya site na nzọụkwụ nrụpụta mgbe emechara (`scripts/build/colocate-standalone.mjs`); wuo ngwá ọrụ, ọ bụghị oge ojiri gaa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

Mgbalị ebumnuche combo na-eketa oge nkwụsị nke arịrịọ gbagoro agbago (`FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`, ma ọ bụ `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` mgbe ọ na-enye ndabara ndabara). Tọọ `targetTimeoutMs` na ngwakọta, ndabara ndabara, ma ọ bụ onye na-eweta gabiga naanị iji mee ka ngwakọta daa ngwa ngwa; ụkpụrụ ndị dị n'elu oge mgbago elu ugbu a ka etinyere na oge nkwụsị elu.

`comboTimeoutMs` bụ mmefu ego elekere mgbidi dị iche iche gafere ebumnuche ọdịda. Hapụ ya na-atọgheghị ma ọ bụ `0` ka idowe ntugharị na-akparaghị ókè (nkwụsị-nkwụsị siri ike nkeji iri na `comboPredicates.ts` ka na-emetụta). Uru dị mma na-anọchi netwọk nchekwa ahụ maka combo. Debe `comboTimeoutMs` ogologo oge karịa `targetTimeoutMs` ya mere ọdịda ka na-enwekwa oge mgbe ebumnuche mbụ jiri nwayọ.

### Ebe ndị na-emebi sekit

Ntụzigharị ihe mgbaji sekit nke ọkwa onye. Ihe ndabara na-egosipụta ụkpụrụ agbatịgoro ejiri kemgbe v3.6 maka njikọ 500 +.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                                            | Ọdabara   | Isi mmalite faịlụ                    | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------------------------------------------------------------- | --------- | ------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_OAUTH_THRESHOLD`                   | `8`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Ọgba ọdịda ọdịda sochiri anya maka ndị na-eweta OAuth tupu onye nbibi ahụ agawa.                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_OAUTH_RESET_MS`                    | `60000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Tọgharịa windo (ms) maka onye na-eweta OAuth.                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_API_KEY_THRESHOLD`                 | `12`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Ọnụ mbata ọdịda sochiri anya maka ndị na-eweta igodo API.                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_API_KEY_RESET_MS`                  | `30000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Tọgharia mpio (ms) maka onye na-eweta igodo API.                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_LOCAL_THRESHOLD`                   | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Ọnụ mbata ọdịda na-esochi maka ndị na-eweta mpaghara (Ollama, LM Studio, ...).                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_LOCAL_RESET_MS`                    | `15000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Tọgharia mpio (ms) maka onye na-emebi ihe na-eweta mpaghara.                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`          | `10`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Ọkwa ndị na-eweta: ọdịda dị n'ime mpio tupu ndị na-eweta OAuth niile abanye n'ime oyi.                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`          | `900000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Ọkwa ndị na-eweta: windo ọdịda-mkpọtụ (ms) maka ndị na-eweta OAuth.                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_COOLDOWN_MS`                | `300000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Ọkwa ndị na-eweta: cooldown (ms) ozugbo agafere ụzọ ndị na-eweta OAuth.                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_DEGRADATION_THRESHOLD`      | `5`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | onye na-eweta OAuth na-abanye DEGRADED n'ọtụtụ ọdịda a.                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_MAX_BACKOFF_MULTIPLIER`     | `8`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | OAuth onye na-eweta max resetTimeout escalation multiplier.                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_BACKOFF_ESCALATION_COUNT`   | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | onye na-eweta OAuth na-abawanye mgbe nke a gachara ọtụtụ okirikiri mepere emepe.                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`        | `15`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Ọkwa onye na-eweta: ọdịda dị n'ime mpio tupu onye na-eweta igodo API niile abanye n'ime oyi.                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`        | `1800000` | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Ọkwa ndị na-eweta ihe: windo ọdịda ọdịda na-atụgharị (ms) maka ndị na-eweta igodo API.                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_COOLDOWN_MS`              | `600000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Njikwa ọkwa onye na-eweta: cooldown (ms) ozugbo agbaru igodo igodo API.                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_DEGRADATION_THRESHOLD`    | `7`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Onye na-eweta igodo API na-abanye DEGRADED n'ọtụtụ ọdịda a.                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_MAX_BACKOFF_MULTIPLIER`   | `4`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Onye na-eweta igodo API kacha nrụpụtaTimeout nrịbawanye ụba.                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_BACKOFF_ESCALATION_COUNT` | `3`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Onye na-eweta igodo API na-abawanye ka ọtụtụ okirikiri mepere emepe gachara.                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`          | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Ọkwa ndị na-eweta ihe: ọdịda tupu ndị na-eweta mpaghara abanye n'ime jụụ.                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`          | `300000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Ọkwa ndị na-eweta: Window ọdịda ọdịda (ms) maka ndị na-eweta mpaghara.                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_COOLDOWN_MS`                | `60000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Ọkwa ndị na-eweta: cooldown (ms) ozugbo agafere oke ndị na-eweta mpaghara.                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_COOLDOWN_MS`                               | `60000`   | `open-sse/services/apiKeyRotator.ts` | Ntugharị igodo ọ bụla (ms) etinyere site na ntụgharị igodo ọtụtụ igodo API mgbe 429 elu na-ebu enweghị `Retry-After` (#14573). Ụkpụrụ na-abụghị nke ziri ezi ma ọ bụ na-abụghị ọnụọgụ na-adaghachi na ndabara. |
| `PIN_DROP_BACKOFF_LEVEL`                                      | `2`       | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`         | Omimi azụ azụ ebe a na-ahụta onye na-eweta pin cache adịghị mma ma tụba pin ahụ maka ọdịda.                                                                                                                    |
| `PIN_DROP_GRACE_MS`                                           | `20000`   | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`         | Window mgbochi-flap (ms) na-anabata obere oyi na-adịru nwa oge tupu ịdobe pin cache.                                                                                                                           |

### Ihe atụ

| Ihe atụ                             | Nhazi                                                   |
| ----------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Ọgbọ koodu na-adị ogologo oge**   | `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS=900000` (15 min)                    |
| **Na-ekekọta mkpokọta iyi oge ndụ** | `STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS=1260000` (21 min)             |
| **Ọsọ-ọdịda maka mmepụta API**      | `API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS=10000`                     |
| **Ụdị echiche agbatịkwuru**         | `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS=300000` (nkeji 5 n'etiti nkeji) |
