## 21. Proxy Health

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                                | Ọdabara                  | Isi mmalite faịlụ                                                                           | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PROXY_FAST_FAIL_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `2000`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth.ts`                                                                    | Nlele ahụike na-ada ngwa ngwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `PROXY_LATENCY_WINDOW_HOURS`                      | `3`                      | `src/lib/db/proxies.ts`                                                                     | Window oge (awa) maka ịgbakọ nkezi latency nke proxies ndị chọrọ ntuli aka na atụmatụ ọdọ mmiri emebere nke latency kachasị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL_MS`                       | `30000`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth.ts`                                                                    | Nyocha ahụike cache TTL.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_UNHEALTHY_CACHE_TTL_MS`             | `2000`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth.ts`                                                                    | Cache TTL maka nyocha ahụike proxy dara ada. Debe nke a dị mkpụmkpụ karịa `PROXY_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL_MS` ka oge nkwụsị proxy na-adịru nwa oge n'okpuru nnukwu concurrency, nwaa ngwa ngwa na-enweghị gbanyụọ ngwa ngwa-ada maka proxies nwụrụ anwụ n'ezie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_ENABLED`                            | `true`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                          | Tọọ `false` iji gbanyụọ onye nhazi ahụike proxy ndabere nke na-enyocha proxies edebanyere aha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_INTERVAL_MS`                        | `600000`                 | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                          | N'azụ ahụike-onye na-ahazi oge mkpochapụ na ms (nke kacha nta `60000`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_RELAY_INTERVAL_MS`                  | `21600000`               | `src/lib/proxyHealth/relayCadence.ts`                                                       | Oge nyocha na ms maka proxies Edge-relay (`deno`/`vercel`/`cloudflare`), onye nyocha ya na-akpọlite iche iche enweghị ihe nkesa ( `60000` kacha nta).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_RECOVERY_INTERVAL_MS`               | `600000`                 | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                          | Oge mgbake ndabere na ms: ugboro ole a na-enyocha proxies na-adịghị mma na mbụ ka onye nnọchiteanya eweghachitere laghachi na ntụgharị na-enweghị ịmalitegharị. Uru ndị dị n'okpuru `60000` daa azụ na ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_URL`                           | `https://httpbin.org/ip` | `src/lib/proxyHealth/probeTarget.ts`                                                        | Ebumnuche nyocha ikike iru nke onye nhazi na ebe njedebe `/api/settings/proxies/auto-test`. Tụtụ ya aka na internal/self-hosted URL iji zere ndabara ọhaneze.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_CONCURRENCY`                   | `10`                     | `src/lib/proxyHealth/probeTarget.ts`                                                        | Nyocha malitere otu ugboro n'otu ogbe, nke onye nhazi oge na ebe njedebe `/api/settings/proxies/auto-test` na-ekekọrịta. N'ala na 1 ma kpuchie na 50.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_STAGGER_MS`                    | `100`                    | `src/lib/proxyHealth/probeTarget.ts`                                                        | Igbu oge na ms n'etiti ọpụpụ nyocha abụọ n'ime ogbe. Na-enweghị ya, batch dum na-ahapụ n'otu oge na egress IP na-ekekọrịta nwere ike imebi ebumnuche nwere oke. Tọọ na `0` iji gbanyụọ oghere; dị na 5000.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_USE_PROVIDER_TARGET`                | `true`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth/providerProbeTarget.ts`                                                | Tọọ “ụgha” ka ịkwụsị inyocha ezigbo onye ọbịa nke onye na-eweta proxy ekenyere (`GET /models`, enweghị igodo API ) ma jiri `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_URL` mgbe niile kama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_AUTO_DEACTIVATE`                    | `false`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth/statusPolicy.ts`                                                       | Mgbe `false` (nke ndabara), ihe nleba anya iru eru akpaghị aka (onye nhazi + bọtịnụ `/api/settings/proxies/auto-test` "Nnwale niile")** naanị ọgụgụ** and never dee a proxy's status — naanị onye ọrụ na-esetịpụ active/inactive, ya mere a flaky nyocha enweghị ike strand onye nnọchiteanya e kenyere (#6246). Tọọ `true` ka iweghachite ule-na ịtọ omume.                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `FLUSH_EMPTY_RETRY_ENABLED`                       | `false`                  | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                          | Ọkọlọtọ njirimara Nwepu (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md) ; dashboard DB override meriri). `true` (ma ọ bụ `1`, `yes`) na-emegharị ntụgharị ntụgharị efu na-atụgharị site na ụzọ nzere nkịtị (ruo `STREAM_RECOVERY.EMPTY_TURN_RETRY_MAX` emegharị) kama ikpughe 200 efu ma ọ bụ ọdịnaya efu 502.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `EMPTY_STREAM_SIBLING_RECOVERY_ENABLED`           | `false`                  | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                          | Ọkọlọtọ njirimara Nwepu (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md) ; dashboard DB override meriri). `true` (ma ọ bụ `1`, `yes`) na-akwado Claude/Codex nyocha ọdịnaya efu na-emefu ihe dị adị na-anwale na njikọ ọzọ tozuru oke mgbe ntụgharị `empty_content` gasịrị, na-enweghị nkwụsịtụ ma ọ bụ mkpuchi ihe nlereanya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `STREAM_LATE_OUTPUT_PROBE_ENABLED`                | `false`                  | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                          | Ọkọlọtọ njirimara Nwepu (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md) ; dashboard DB override meriri). `true` (ma ọ bụ `1`, `yes`) na-aga n'ihu na-enyocha otu nchikota ebugharị na 16 ka 64 nke mbụ a na-ahụ anya ka na-egosi iyi ahụ dị ka mmepụta ewepụtara (mpụta 5xx naanị ya na-arịọ arịrịọ kama mkpuchi naanị ihe nlereanya).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROXY_POOL_EGRESS_OBSERVATION`                   | `false`                  | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                          | Ọkọlọtọ njirimara Nwepu (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md) ; dashboard DB override meriri). `true` (ma ọ bụ `1`, `yes`) na-egosi nlele naanị ọdọ mmiri egress n'okpuru ọdọ mmiri proxy na dashboard (iche egress IPs, njikọ na nke a kacha hụ n'azụ otu IP n'ime awa 24 gara aga, site na ndekọ proxy). Ejighị ya mee njem.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROXY_OPERATOR_EGRESS_ENABLED`                   | `false`                  | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                          | Ọkọlọtọ njirimara Nwepu (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md) ; dashboard DB override meriri). `true` (ma ọ bụ `1`, `yes`) na-anabata adrees ndị ọrụ chụpụrụ akara ụbọchị nke onye otu ọdọ mmiri wee jikọta ha na akwụkwọ akụkọ a na-agụ maka ngosi na usoro ọdọ mmiri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `PROXY_OPERATOR_EGRESS_WINDOW_HOURS`              | `24`                     | `src/lib/db/proxyOperatorEgress.ts`                                                         | Window nlele n'ime awa maka ahịrị ndị ọrụ na-ebugharị egress (gụọ na agụ ọ bụla; ọnweghị ịmalitegharị ọzọ achọrọ). Akpọrọ ya na 1..168; na-anọghị nso, na-abụghị ọnụọgụ, ma ọ bụ efu na-adaghachi azụ na 24 na otu usoro ịdọ aka ná ntị zuru oke, ọ dịghị mgbe a tụfuru.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE`                               | `false`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                          | Tọọ `true` ka onye nhazi oge wepụ proxies na-akpaghị aka ka ọ gafechara ọdịda ugboro ugboro.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE_AFTER`                         | `3`                      | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                          | Ọdịda n'usoro tupu onye nhazi oge wepụrụ proxy (mgbe `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE=true`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `PROXY_AUTO_DISABLE`                              | `false`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                          | Tọọ `true` ka onye nhazi mee ka onye nhazi mee ihe nro-gbanyụọ (ọnọdụ `dead`, ọ dịghị mgbe ehichapụ) proxy mgbe ọdịda ugboro ugboro ugboro ugboro, kama iwepụ ya. Nhọrọ na-adịghị emebi emebi na `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE`: proxy na-apụ na mkpebi pool/rotation ozugbo (nyocha ọnọdụ ndụ nke scope-pool resolution na-ewepụrịrị ya) ma na-emegharị ya ozugbo ozugbo ọ malitere ịgafe nyocha ọzọ. Na-ekekọrịta oke `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE_AFTER`. Ọ bụrụ na ọkọlọtọ abụọ bụ `true`, `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE` na-emeri.                                                                          |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_PASSIVE_SKIP`                       | `false`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                          | Ịbanye na-agafe agafe: mgbe `true`, onye nhazi ahụ na-amagharị nyocha ndụ maka njedebe njedebe nke okporo ụzọ mmepụta ihe na-adịbeghị anya gosipụtara ya (ọ dịkarịa ala otu ihe ịga nke ọma na enweghị proxy ọdịda na windo, kpọmkwem otu onye na-eweta hụrụ). Gbanyụọ na ndabara: a na-enyocha proxy ọ bụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `PROXY_PASSIVE_WINDOW_MS`                         | `300000`                 | `src/lib/proxyHealth/passiveVerdict.ts`                                                     | Ohere dị na ms nke mmepụta okporo ụzọ na-agafe agafe na-ele anya (bounded `60000..599999`). Mkpirisi karịa nkeji oge mkpochapụ nkeji iri ka ọdịda na-adịbeghị anya na-achịkwa kama ịgbanye ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTROL_PLANE_PROXY_DIRECT_FALLBACK`   | `false`                  | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`                                            | Kwe ka OAuth na onye na-eweta nkwado na-aga ka ọ gafere proxy etinyere wee jikọọ ozugbo mgbe nnweta proxy tupu nleba anya dara. Ụzọ dị irè bụ Ọkọlọtọ atụmatụ DB na-ewepụ > env var > ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `RATE_LIMIT_MAX_WAIT_MS`                          | `30000` (30s)            | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts`                                                            | Ọdabara** kwụ n'ahịrị-echere** mmefu ego: Ogologo oge ole arịrịọ nwere ike chere oghere onye na-eweta ya wee nọrọ ọdụ n'ahịrị tupu ajụ ya, na-eruteghị elu. A na-ekpochapụ oge oge ozugbo ọrụ ahụ malitere ime - a na-ekekọta igbu ya iche site na `RATE_LIMIT_EXECUTION_MAX_WAIT_MS`. Env na-esetịpụ ndabara naanị: `resilienceSettings.requestQueue.maxWaitMs` nọgidere na-adịgide adịgide, wee bụrụ njikọ `rateLimitOverrides.maxWaitMs` kwa, onye ọ bụla meriri ya.                                                                                                          |
| `RATE_LIMIT_EXECUTION_MAX_WAIT_MS`                | `600000` (10 min)        | `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`                                                     | Ulo ụlọ ruo ogologo oge arịrịọ anabatara nwere ike nọrọ na mmezu tupu ndoputa oke ọnụego ya agwụ - ewepụrụ site na mmefu ego chere kwụ n'ahịrị ka ịmalite ịmalite ngwa ngwa n'ọnụ ụzọ ndị na-adịghị abawanye anaghị ewe oge (#12027).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `RATE_LIMIT_MAX_QUEUE_DEPTH`                      | `0` (nkwarụ)             | `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`                                                     | Okpu nnabata kwụ n'ahịrị: jụ na 429 `queue_full` ozugbo ọtụtụ arịrịọ a kwụgoro n'ahịrị. `0` = enweghị oke (ndabara).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `RATE_LIMIT_AUTO_ENABLE`                          | _(Edobeghi)_             | `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`                                                     | Mee ka ọnụọgụ nchekwa net on/off mee ka akpaaka n'agbanyeghị ntọala Dashboard na-adịgide adịgide (`resilienceSettings.requestQueue.autoEnableApiKeyProviders`). Na-anabata `true`/`1`/`on` ịmanye, `false`/`0`/`off` ịgbapụ. Mgbe ọ na-arụ ọrụ (nke ndabara), njikọ igodo API na-arụ ọrụ na-echekwa oke oke na-akpaghị aka. Ịtọ n'otu njikọ `rateLimitProtection: false` na-ewepụ nchedo doro anya mana ọ naghị apụ na netwọk nchekwa a; idobe env var a na `false` bụ usoro zuru ụwa ọnụ iji mee njikọ igodo API na-enweghị ahịrị oke-oke.                                      |
| `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_ENABLED`                       | _(gbanyụọ → ) _          | `open-sse/services/providerCooldownTracker.ts`                                              | Wepụ arịrịọ nleba anya zuru ụwa ọnụ provider/connection cooldown nsuso. OFF na ndabara (na-ekpuchi njikọ Cooldown / Onye na-eweta Circuit Breaker). Nabata `true`/`1`/`on` iji mee ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_MIN_MS`                        | `5000`                   | `open-sse/services/providerCooldownTracker.ts`                                              | Opekempe ajụkwa (ms) tupu emegharịa provider/connection dara ada. Agụnyere nke ukwuu site na ọdịda na-esochi. A na-eji naanị mgbe `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_ENABLED`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_MAX_MS`                        | `300000` (minti 5)       | `open-sse/services/providerCooldownTracker.ts`                                              | Okpu kacha mma (ms) tupu anwalegharịa provider/connection dara n'agbanyeghị. A na-eji naanị mgbe `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_ENABLED`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED`                         | _(gbanyụọ → ) _          | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` (mkpụrụ) → `open-sse/services/streamRecovery.ts` (echiche) | **Kedu:** mgbake n'ụzọ doro anya nke iyi gbagoro agbago elu (ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri free-claude-code). Jide windo SSE oghere ruo `STREAM_RECOVERY.HOLDBACK_MS` (750 ms) ya mere _pre-commit_ cutoff - nke na-eme tupu byte ọ bụla erute onye ahịa - ga-emepe ọzọ wee nwagharịa na-adịghị ahụ anya.** Mgbe iji mee ka:** flaky/upstreams nke na-emekarị 0-byte-truncate na mmalite iyi; hapụ OFF ma ọ bụrụ na ịnweghị ike ị nweta ihe ruru 750 ms nke akara oge-na-mbụ na iyi ọ bụla. Nabata `true`/`1`/`on`. Mkpụrụ ntọala Resilience nọgidere; ntọala Dashboard na-emeri otu oge atọrọ. |
| `STREAM_RECOVERY_MIDSTREAM_ENABLED`               | _(gbanyụọ → ) _          | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` (mkpụrụ) → `open-sse/services/streamRecovery.ts` (echiche) | **Kedu:** mid-iyi n'ihu (Fase 4.4) - mgbe a _post-commit_ truncation (bytes abanyelarị onye ahịa), rịọgharịa ya na akụkụ akụkụ ederede dị ka onye inyeaka prefill na kpachie suffix efu. Ederede dị larịị OpenAI-dakọtara naanị iyi; Ọ dịghị mgbe ọkụ na ngwá ọrụ oku na-efe efe.** Mgbe iji mee ka:** ogologo ọgbọ na-ebipụ n'etiti azịza na ị na-anabata ọdụ enwetara na-abata dị ka otu gbawara kama token-site-token. N'adabereghị na `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED` (profaịlụ ihe egwu dị iche). Nabata `true`/`1`/`on`.                                                         |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_ENABLED`              | _(gbanyụọ → ) _          | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` → `open-sse/services/throughputWatchdog.ts`                | Ịbanye na-arụsi ọrụ ike-iyi bara uru na-ahụ maka mmepụta. Na-achọpụta iyi na-aga n'ihu na-eziga chunks mana ọ nọ n'okpuru ọnụego ntinye aka ahaziri; obi mgbawa, mmemme ojiji, deltas efu, na usoro tool/reasoning adịghị ewere ka ọganihu. Kewapụ na oge arụghị ọrụ na njedebe siri ike.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_WARMUP_MS`            | `30000`                  | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                  | Oge amara tupu nlebanya mmepụta ihe, gbanyere 0–600000 ms.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_WINDOW_MS`            | `30000`                  | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                  | Window mmepụta bara uru na-atụgharị, kechiri ya na 1000–600000 ms; achọrọ otu windo zuru ezu tupu ime ime.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_MIN_BYTES_PER_SECOND` | `4`                      | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                  | Ọnụego ntinye ihe enyemaka UTF-8 kacha nta (proxy token conservative), kenyere 1–1000000.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_MIN_USEFUL_BYTES`     | `1`                      | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                  | Opekempe arụpụtaghị ihe bara uru na-enweghị efu, a na-ewere na ọ ga-atụ ihe, ejiri ya na 1–1000000 bytes.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS`                          | `3000`                   | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                               | Ogologo oge stagger (ms) n'etiti nlele ahụike onye na-eweta na mmalite.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `HEALTHCHECK_JITTER_MIN_MS`                       | `500`                    | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                               | Opekempe jitter enweghị usoro (ms) agbakwunyere n'elu `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS` n'etiti nlele ahụike onye na-eweta, iji gbochie nwapu (Isiokwu #1220).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `HEALTHCHECK_JITTER_MAX_MS`                       | `5000`                   | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                               | Oke jitter enweghị usoro (ms) agbakwunyere n'elu `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS` n'etiti nlele ahụike onye na-eweta, iji gbochie nwapu (Isiokwu #1220).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `HEALTHCHECK_BATCH_SIZE`                          | `20`                     | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                               | Nleba anya n'otu oge maka nha nke mmalite mmalite-nyocha ahụike; ụkpụrụ ndị buru ibu na-elele njikọ ndị ọzọ n'otu aka ahụ, ụkpụrụ ndị dị nta na-ebelata ibu mgbawa (Nke #7875, nlọghachi nke #7719).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `REQUEST_RETRY`                                   | `2`                      | `src/sse/services/cooldownAwareRetry.ts`                                                    | Ọnụọgụ nke nnwagharị akpaaka na nzaghachi nju oyi kpara akpa tupu iweghachite mperi na onye ahịa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `MAX_RETRY_INTERVAL_SEC`                          | `30`                     | `src/sse/services/cooldownAwareRetry.ts`                                                    | Ogologo oke azụ azụ (sekọnd) n'etiti nlegharị anya jụrụ oyi. Ejiri uru a kpuchie ya n'agbanyeghị `Retry-After` dị elu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `HEADROOM_URL`                                    | `http://localhost:8787`  | `src/lib/headroom/detect.ts`                                                                | Proxy URL. Usoro ndụ dashboard (`api/headroom/*`) na-ebupụta `headroom-ai` CLI mpaghara na loopback na ndabara; mebie naanị iji tụọ aka na proxy sidecar Docker dị na mpụga.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

### Stream-nkwalite nlegharị anya

A na-enyocha omume njide mgbake site na hardcoded constants na `open-sse/config/constants.ts` (`STREAM_RECOVERY`), egosiri ebe a maka ntụnye aka - ịgbanwe ha chọrọ ndezi koodu, ọ bụghị env var:

- `STREAM_RECOVERY.HOLDBACK_MS = 750` - Ogologo oge ole ka echere windo SSE oghere ka enwere ike ịnwagharị n'oge tupu byte ọ bụla etinyere onye ahịa ya.
- `STREAM_RECOVERY.BUFFER_MAX_BYTES = 65536` - okpu siri ike na windo ejidere; mee (flush + passthrough) ozugbo ọtụtụ bytes na-agbakọta, n'agbanyeghị oge.
- `STREAM_RECOVERY.EARLY_RETRY_MAX = 4` — max transperent re-mepee nke mgbago iyi ka njide azụ ka na-emezughị.

> **Oke ọnụ ahịa mmịfe-window onye na-eweta ya (enweghị env var):** FCC-ported per-provinder sliding-window rate-limit _fallback_ dị na koodu (`open-sse/services/providerDefaultRateLimit.ts`, wired site `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`) mana ụgbọ mmiri nwere** maapụ ndabara efu** ma nwee** enweghị onye ọrụ env var** taa - a na-akwado ya naanị site na ntinye nko / koodu. Edepụtaghị ya ụma na tebụl dị n'elu. The per-`(token, IP)` relay limiter nke _does_ nwere knob bụ `RELAY_IP_PER_MINUTE` (§3 Network & Ports).

| `CURSOR_STREAM_DEBUG`                 | _(Edobeghi)_                     | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | utu aha `CURSOR_DEBUG` dakọtara azụ azụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CURSOR_DUMP_FILE`                    | _(Edobeghi)_                     | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Ụzọ faịlụ nhọrọ nke na-enweta chunks Cursor decoded mgbe `CURSOR_DEBUG=1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CURSOR_STREAM_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `300000`                         | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Gbanye oge nkwụsị na-abaghị uru (ms) maka onye mmebe Cursor.                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CURSOR_KV_GRACE_MS`                  | `2000`                           | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Window amara (ms) mgbe onye na-ede egwu kv_after_text soft terminator mgbe bytes ka na-echekwa - na-enye oge oku oku exec_mcp na-esochi ya iji mechaa etiti ya.                                                                                                                            |
| `CURSOR_TOOL_DIRECTIVE`               | enyere (`!== "0"`)               | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Ngwá ọrụ na-enye ntụziaka na-eme ka onye na-ede egwú-2.5 na-enye oku ngwá ọrụ na-atụkwasị obi. Tọọ `0` iji gbanyụọ.                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_SYSTEM_INSTRUCTION_APPEND` | _(Edobeghi)_                     | `open-sse/translator/request/claude-to-openai.ts`, `open-sse/translator/response/openai-to-claude.ts` | Sistemu akọwapụtara onye ọrụ ngwa ngwa ederede agbakwunyere na ntụgharị ozi sistemu AFTER (ntụtụ ntụgharị mgbe emechara), rute n'ụzọ codex/Responses na `/v1/messages`. A na-ejikwa ya dị ka nganiihu ntụzịaka wepụrụ na ngọngọ okwu mmalite nke sistemụ. Hapụ na-akpaghị aka iji gbanyụọ. |

Gbanyụọ site na ndabara — ndị heuristics bụ ndị bekee-prose ekara na mutate nzaghachi ugwo, otú a zaghachi na legitimately meghere na ndị dị otú ahụ a ngalaba ga-atụfu ya.              |

## 23. GitHub Njikọta

Hapụ ndị ọrụ ka ha si na Dashboard kọpụta nsogbu ozugbo.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe    | Ọdabara      | Isi mmalite faịlụ                          | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                    |
| --------------------- | ------------ | ------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GITHUB_ISSUES_REPO`  | _(Edobeghi)_ | `src/app/api/v1/issues/report/route.ts`    | Ebe nchekwa n'ụdị `owner/repo`.                                                                                                          |
| `GITHUB_ISSUES_TOKEN` | _(Edobeghi)_ | `src/app/api/v1/issues/report/route.ts`    | GitHub Token nnweta nkeonwe nwere oke `issues:write`.                                                                                    |
| `GITHUB_TOKEN`        | _(Edobeghi)_ | ndị na-enyere aka n'ụdị triage / igwe ojii | Ihe nrịbama ohere GitHub a na-eji dị ka ọdịda azụ maka `GITHUB_ISSUES_TOKEN` ma ndị enyemaka igwe ojii na-eri na `src/lib/cloudAgent/*`. |

## Ọnọdụ ntinye

Maka ntụzịaka azụ azụ azụ SRE (omume ts/bifrost/auto, 9router vs CLIProxyAPI placement, and high-throughput fallback strategy), ịhụ [Relay Backend Strategy](/docs/reference/RELAY_BACKEND_STRATEGY.md).

### Mmepe Mpaghara Opekempe

```bash
JWT_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 48)
API_KEY_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32)
INITIAL_PASSWORD=dev123
PORT=20128
NODE_ENV=development
```

### Docker Production

```bash
JWT_SECRET=<generated>
API_KEY_SECRET=<generated>
INITIAL_PASSWORD=<generated>
STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY=<generated>
DATA_DIR=/data
PORT=20128
API_PORT=20129
NODE_ENV=production
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true
NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=https://omniroute.example.com
BASE_URL=http://localhost:20128
OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB=8192
CORS_ORIGIN=https://your-frontend.example.com
```

### Air-Gapped / CI

```bash
JWT_SECRET=test-jwt-secret-for-ci
API_KEY_SECRET=test-api-key-secret-for-ci
INITIAL_PASSWORD=testpass
NODE_ENV=production
OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_BACKGROUND_SERVICES=true
APP_LOG_TO_FILE=false
```

### VPS na Reverse Proxy (nginx + Cloudflare)

```bash
JWT_SECRET=<generated>
API_KEY_SECRET=<generated>
STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY=<generated>
PORT=20128
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true
NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=https://omniroute.example.com
BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:20128
CORS_ORIGIN=https://omniroute.example.com
ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true
CLI_COMPAT_ALL=1
```

---

## 24. Skills Sandbox (v3.8.0 +)

A na-etinye oke na knobs nchekwa mgbe usoro nka (`src/lib/skills/`) na-eme akpaaka akọwapụtara nke onye ọrụ na gburugburu igbe ájá.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                | Ọdabara                                       | Isi mmalite faịlụ                                                  | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| --------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`       | `10000` (10 s)                                | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Oge ngwụcha elekere mgbidi kwa-mgbe maka koodu nka nwere igbe aja. Okpu siri ike; ihe ọ bụla ọzọ na-egbu.                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `SKILLS_EXECUTION_TIMEOUT_MS`     | _(dabere azụ na `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`)_ | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Oge ngwụcha nka nka nka dị elu. Tọọ elu karịa `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS` iji kwe ka usoro ọrụ ọtụtụ nzọụkwụ dị.                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `SKILLS_MAX_FILE_BYTES`           | `1048576` (1 MB)                              | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Oke bytes nwere ike ịgụ site na faịlụ ọ bụla nwere igbe ájá.                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `SKILLS_MAX_HTTP_RESPONSE_BYTES`  | `256000` (250 KB)                             | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Oke bytes weghaara site na nzaghachi HTTP ọ bụla n'ime nka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `SKILLS_MAX_SANDBOX_OUTPUT_CHARS` | `100000`                                      | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Okpu siri ike na mkpụrụedemede stdout/stderr lọghachiri site na oku igbe igbe.                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED`  | `false`                                       | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Tọọ `1`/`true` iji kwe ka netwọk pụọ n'ime igbe ájá. Emepụtara ya** dịpụrụ adịpụ** maka nchekwa.                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `SKILLS_ALLOWED_SANDBOX_IMAGES`   | _(efu)_                                       | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Ndepụta ohere nke rịkọm nke kewapụrụ rịkọm anabatara maka mkpochapụ igbe ájá. Ihe efu pụtara naanị ndabara arụnyere n'ime ya.                                                                                                                                                     |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_DOCKER_IMAGE`     | _(ndabara arụnyere n'ime ya)_                 | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Oyiyi akpa ejiri mee ihe mgbe a na-amịpụta igbe ájá na-akwado Docker. Wepu ka ị tụnye onyonyo ntọala agbasiri ike.                                                                                                                                                                |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_RUNTIME`          | `auto`                                        | `src/lib/skills/sandbox.ts`, `src/lib/skills/containerProvider.ts` | Oge ịgba ọsọ nke akpa maka ịkụ ọkpọ: `auto` \| `docker` \| `apple` \| `wsl` \| `orbstack` \| `podman`. `auto` na-ahọrọ oge ịgba ọsọ kacha arụnyere n'otu onye ọbịa OS (Apple Container/OrbStack na macOS, WSL Container on Windows, Podman na Linux), na-adaghachi azụ na Docker. |

> [!CAUTION]
> Ịkwado `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED=true` na-emepe ụzọ mgbapụ site na koodu nka aka ike. Jikọta ya na `OUTBOUND_SSRF_GUARD_ENABLED=true` yana amụma `CORS_ORIGIN`/proxy siri ike na mbugharị nkekọrịta.

## 25. Onye na-enye oke, Tunnels, Backups & Misc Runtime

Ebe njedebe oke onye na-enye, ọwara netwọk (Tailscale, Ngrok, MITM debug proxy), ọdọ mmiri 1Proxy egress, nchekwa data ndabere na obere ihe njiri mara ọ bụla na-echigharịkwuru site na oyi akwa ma ọ bụ edemede.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                             | Ọdabara                                                                     | Isi mmalite faịlụ                                                         | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ---------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REDIS_URL`                                    | `redis://localhost:6379`                                                    | `src/shared/utils/rateLimiter.ts`                                         | eriri njikọ Redis maka backend njedebe ọnụego.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ALIBABA_CODING_PLAN_HOST`                     | _(onye ọbịa mmepụta)_                                                       | `open-sse/services/bailianQuotaFetcher.ts`                                | Wepụ onye ọbịa na-eji weta Alibaba Bailian codeing-plan quotas.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `ALIBABA_CODING_PLAN_QUOTA_URL`                | sitere na onye ọbịa                                                         | `open-sse/services/bailianQuotaFetcher.ts`                                | Ntughari URL zuru oke maka Alibaba Bailian.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `QWEN_CLOUD_COOKIE`                            | _(Edobeghi)_                                                                | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Kuki nnọkọ njikwa maka Qwen Cloud / Model Studio Personal Token Atụmatụ ọnụ ụzọ ámá (igodo API enweghị ike ịgụ ya). Detuo nkụnye eji isi mee `Cookie` niile - o nwere `login_qwencloud_ticket` - site na oku `api.json` ọ bụla gaa `cs-data.qwencloud.com` na home.qwencloud.com › Ịgba ụgwọ › ndenye aha (F12 › Network). Mmetụta na-enwe mmetụta na nnọkọ; na-ahọrọ ubi-njikọ `qwenCloudCookie` Dashboard.                                                                                                                  |
| `QWEN_CLOUD_SEC_TOKEN`                         | _(Edobeghi)_                                                                | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Akwụkwọ ntuziaka `sec_token` gafechara maka ọnụ ụzọ njikwa njikwa Token Plan. Mmetụta; mgbe ịtọghe onye na-ebubata na-edozi ya na dashboard HTML site na iji kuki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `QWEN_TOKEN_PLAN_HOST`                         | `https://cs-data.qwencloud.com`                                             | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Ndị na-elekọta ọnụ ụzọ ámá kwụsịrị maka onye na-ebubata atụmatụ Token nkeonwe (dịka `bailian-singapore-cs.alibabacloud.com` maka ihe njikwa ihe nkiri Studio Model).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `QWEN_TOKEN_PLAN_DASHBOARD_URL`                | `https://home.qwencloud.com/`                                               | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Dashboard URL na-eji edozi `sec_token` site na nbanye HTML.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `XIAOMI_MIMO_CONSOLE_COOKIE`                   | _(Edobeghi)_                                                                | `open-sse/services/usage/xiaomi-mimo.ts`                                  | Kuki nnọkọ njikwa maka Xiaomi MiMo Token Atụmatụ oke API (`platform.xiaomimimo.com/api/v1/tokenPlan/usage` + `tokenPlan/detail` - igodo ntụnye API enweghị ike ịgụ ya). Detuo nkụnye eji isi mee `Cookie` niile - ọ nwere `api-platform_serviceToken` na `userId` - site na arịrịọ ọ bụla maka `platform.xiaomimimo.com` na ibe Atụmatụ Token (F12 › Network). Mmetụta na-enwe mmetụta na nnọkọ; na-ahọrọ ubi-njikọ `xiaomiMimoConsoleCookie` Dashboard.                                                                      |
| `XIAOMI_MIMO_MONTHLY_TOKEN_LIMIT`              | `4100000000`                                                                | `open-sse/services/usage/xiaomi-mimo.ts`                                  | Ego mmefu kwa ọnwa (Ebe E Si Nweta) maka Xiaomi MiMo na-esochi OmniRoute a na-eji eme ihe mgbe ahazighị kuki njikwa. Ihe ndabara na ọkwa Lite 4.1B; tọọ ya na kredit nke atụmatụ (Standard 11B/Pro 38B/Max 82B). Otu njikọ `providerSpecificData.monthlyTokenLimit` ga-emeri n'elu env var a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

| `CONTEXT_KEEP_LATEST_IMAGES`                   | `2`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/contextManager.ts`                                     | Ole n'ime onyonyo inline kacha ọhụrụ ị ga-edobe mgbe a na-akwacha ndị agadi ka ha dabara na windo gbara ya gburugburu (#8560).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

| `OMNIROUTE_EMERGENCY_FALLBACK`                 | enyere                                                                      | `open-sse/services/emergencyFallback.ts`                                  | Tọọ `false` (ma ọ bụ `0`) iji gbanyụọ ndaghachi azụ mmefu mmefu ego mberede nke na-atụgharị arịrịọ dara ada na ụdị `nvidia`/`openai/gpt-oss-120b` efu. Ụzọ dị irè bụ ọkọlọtọ njirimara DB mebie > env var > ndabara; ọ bụrụ na ọ dịghị, ọrụ ahụ na-adaghachi na uru env raw.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `COMMAND_CODE_CALLBACK_PORT`                   | _(Edobeghi)_                                                                | `src/app/api/providers/command-code/auth/shared.ts`                       | Ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri mpaghara ejiri maka ịkpọghachi ụdị OAuth sitere na koodu Command CLI helper.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `COMMAND_CODE_VERSION`                         | `0.33.2`                                                                    | `open-sse/executors/commandCode.ts`                                       | Uru ezigara dị ka nkụnye eji isi `x-command-code-version` gaa na Usoro Iwu elu. Wetuo iji mebie ụdị CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

Wepu maka koodu iwu self-hosted/alternative API.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `MITM_LOCAL_PORT`                              | `443`                                                                       | `src/mitm/server.cjs`                                                     | Ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri jikọtara mpaghara maka MITM debug proxy.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `MITM_DISABLE_TLS_VERIFY`                      | `0`                                                                         | `src/mitm/server.cjs`                                                     | Tọọ `1` iji gbanyụọ nkwenye TLS (naanị mmepe).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `MITM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`                         | `60000`                                                                     | `src/mitm/socketTimeouts.ts`, `src/mitm/server.cjs`                       | Oge nkwụsị nke oghere na-adịghị arụ ọrụ (ms) maka njikọ proxied; A na-akwatu oghere ndị na-adịghị arụ ọrụ nke gafere nke a ka ọ ghara ịgbasa ọwara oghere ọkara mepere emepe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `BRIDGE_PORT`                                  | `20129`                                                                     | `bin/antigravity-bridge.mjs`                                              | N'ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri Antigravity MITM na-ege ntị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

| `CERT_DIR`                                     | `<DATA_DIR>/mitm`                                                           | `bin/antigravity-bridge.mjs`                                              | Wepụrụ ndekọ ahụ àkwà mmiri na-agụ ihe TLS ya. A na-ahọrọ ụdị cert dị ka ihe nkesa AgentBridge na-eme: `server.crt`/`server.key` maka ntinye akwụkwọ nketa, ma ọ bụghị `ca.crt`/`ca.key` (mgbọrọgwụ CA, akwukwo kwa onye nnabata; `MITM_ROOT_CA_ENABLED=true` manyere ya). Akwa mmiri na-apụ na mmalite ma ọ bụrụ na faịlụ maka ụdị arụ ọrụ na-efu.                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `MITM_VERBOSE`                                 | `1`                                                                         | `src/mitm/server.cjs`, `src/mitm/_internal/bypass.cjs`                    | verbosity log-mkpebi ụzọ: mechie `0`, ụkpụrụ dị elu na-abanyekwu mkpebi bypass/route.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `MITM_ROOT_CA_ENABLED`                         | `false`                                                                     | `src/mitm/manager.ts`                                                     | Tọọ `true` ka ị banye na mgbọrọgwụ-CA + kwa-host-akwụkwọ asambodo (#6684). Nrụnye ọhụrụ na-enweta ya na-akpaghị aka; tinye akwụkwọ nwere akwụkwọ ntụkwasị obi nke dị adịbu na-edobe asambodo SAN a kapịrị ọnụ ọ gwụla ma abanyela.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `MITM_CERT_MODE`                               | `legacy`                                                                    | `src/mitm/manager.ts`, `src/mitm/server.cjs`                              | Tọọ BY onye njikwa MITM maka usoro proxy spawned (`root-ca` \| `legacy`) - na-egosipụta mkpebi mbata nke asambodo; ebughi n'obi ka edobe ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_NO_SUDO`                            | `0`                                                                         | `src/mitm/systemCommands.ts`                                              | Tọọ `1` (eziokwu) ka ịwepụ `sudo` na-eduga na iwu MITM cert-trust - maka ntinye aha enweghị mgbọrọgwụ / aha njirimara ebe onye ọrụ na-eji aka ya tụkwasị CA (dịka ọmụmaatụ site na usoro ntinye CA-certs Node).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `SKIP_ANTIGRAVITY_DNS`                         | _(Edobeghi)_                                                                | `src/mitm/dns/provision.ts`                                               | Tọọ `true` ka ịwụpụ ndenye DNS `/etc/hosts` maka aha nnabata Antigravity kpamkpam - maka akpa na-enweghị sudo/root dị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_DNS_WRITE`                     | _(Edobeghi)_                                                                | `src/mitm/dns/dnsConfig.ts`                                               | Tọọ `1` ka ịfefe ide na faịlụ ndị ọbịa mgbe ndenye adding/removing DNS - maka gburugburu ule n'ime igbe ma ọ bụ naanị ịgụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_SYSTEM_TRUST`                  | `0`                                                                         | `src/mitm/cert/install.ts`, `src/mitm/tproxy/caTrust.ts`                  | Test/CI-only nche: tọọ `1` ka ọ mee cert ntụkwasị obi install/uninstall a no-op ka suite ghara ịgbanwe ụlọ ahịa ntụkwasị obi OS. Tọọ na-akpaghị aka site na ntọlite ule yana usoro ọrụ CI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CHANGELOG_BASE_REF`                           | _(na akpaaka)_                                                              | `scripts/check/check-changelog-integrity.mjs`                             | Ntụgharị ntọala doro anya maka ọnụ ụzọ mgbochi CHANGELOG-eri (ndabere na ngalaba ntọala PR na CI, ma ọ bụ `release/v*` kachasị elu).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `FREE_PROXY_AUTO_SYNC_ENABLED`                 | `false`                                                                     | `src/lib/freeProxyProviders/scheduler.ts`                                 | Tọọ `true` iji mee ka nhazi nhazi mmekọrịta akpaaka n'azụ azụ. Ịbanye, gbanyụọ na ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `FREE_PROXY_AUTO_SYNC_INTERVAL_MS`             | `1800000`                                                                   | `src/lib/freeProxyProviders/scheduler.ts`                                 | Ọkara-mmekọrịta akpaaka na millisekọnd (ndụ 30 ndabara).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

Tọọ `false` iji gbanyụọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

| `FREE_PROXY_1PROXY_MAX`                        | `500`                                                                       | `src/lib/freeProxyProviders/oneproxy.ts`                                  | Oke proxies enwetara kwa mmekọrịta site na 1proxy.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

Tọọ `false` iji gbanyụọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `FREE_PROXY_PROXIFLY_QUANTITY`                 | `100`                                                                       | `src/lib/freeProxyProviders/proxifly.ts`                                  | Ọnụọgụ proxies ị ga-enweta kwa mmekọrịta Proxifly.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

Bump ma ọ bụrụ na onye ọbịa na-ejikọta 6379. Ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri dị n'ime akpa ahụ nọrọ 6379.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDIS_BIND_HOST`                    | `127.0.0.1`                                                                 | `bin/cli/commands/redis.mjs`                                              | Ngwa ngwa onye ọbịa nke 1-pịa Redis launcher na-ebipụta. Onye nrụpụta na-amalite Redis WITHOUT okwuntughe, yabụ na-ejikọta `0.0.0.0` onye ọ bụla na-elekọta gị LAN bụ Redis na-akwadoghị - naanị gbasaa nke a ma ọ bụrụ na ị debere paswọọdụ n'onwe gị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `REDIS_BIND_HOST`                              | `127.0.0.1`                                                                 | `docker-compose.yml`                                                      | Docker interface Host interface na-ebipụta Redis sidecar na (#9286). The dere Redis na-agba ọsọ na-enweghị `requirepass`; Ngwa ngwa na-erute ya n'elu netwọk mejupụtara (`redis:6379`) - ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri ebipụtara bụ naanị maka ngwa n'akụkụ ndị ọbịa. `0.0.0.0` na-ekpughere Redis na-edoghị anya na LAN dum.                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `REDIS_PORT`                                   | `6379`                                                                      | `docker-compose.yml`                                                      | Ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri maka ndị dere Redis sidecar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `APP_BIND_HOST`                                | `127.0.0.1`                                                                 | `docker-compose.yml`, `docker-compose.prod.yml`                           | Docker interface Host interface na-ebipụta ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri dashboard/API/live-WS nke ngwa na (#12568). Site na mbupu `REQUIRE_API_KEY=false` dị ka ndabara `.env.example`, `0.0.0.0` na-ekpughere `/v1` LLM proxy na-amaghị aha na LAN/WAN dum - naanị gbasaa otu `REQUIRE_API_KEY=true` ma ọ bụ proxy n'ihu na-akwado nkwenye nke ya.                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `QDRANT_BIND_HOST`                             | `127.0.0.1`                                                                 | `docker-compose.yml`                                                      | Docker interface Host interface na-ebipụta akụkụ ebe nchekwa Qdrant na (#12578). Otu echiche LAN-nkpughe dị ka `REDIS_BIND_HOST`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `BIFROST_BIND_HOST`                            | `127.0.0.1`                                                                 | `docker-compose.yml`                                                      | Docker interface Host-Compose na-ebipụta sidecar Bifrost router na (#12578). Otu echiche LAN-nkpughe dị ka `REDIS_BIND_HOST`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `REDIS_KEY_PREFIX`                             | `omniroute:`                                                                | `src/shared/utils/rateLimiter.ts`                                         | A na-etinye prefix oghere aha na igodo Redis OmniRoute ọ bụla (ihe njedebe ọnụego, cache auth, ụlọ ahịa oke, ihe mgbawa sekit ọkụ). Na-egbochi ndakpọ isi mgbe ejiri ngwa ndị ọzọ na-ekerịta ihe atụ Redis (#11042).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN`             | _(Unset — Usoro adịghị arụ ọrụ)_                                            | `src/lib/api/internalServiceAuth.ts`                                      | Ekekọrịtara nzuzo maka njirimara-ichekwa ime REST hops (#9260): akụrụngwa OmniRoute na-akpọ ụzọ OmniRoute mpaghara ndị ọzọ na-eziga ya ka `x-omniroute-internal-service-token` ka e wee chekwaa njirimara onye ọkpụkpọ mbụ. Tụnyere `timingSafeEqual`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN_FILE`        | _(Edobeghi)_                                                                | `src/lib/api/internalServiceAuth.ts`                                      | Ụdị faịlụ nzuzo dị iche iche nke akara ọrụ ime: ụzọ na faịlụ nke ọdịnaya ewepụsịrị ya bụ akara ngosi. | `OPENROUTER_PROVIDER_STATS_TTL_MS`             | `86400000` (24h)                                                            | `src/lib/catalog/openrouterProviderStats.ts`                              | Cache TTL maka OpenRouter na-eweta-stats foto, na milliseconds.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

| `QDRANT_HOST`                                  | `qdrant`                                                                    | _(profaịlụ ụyọkọ Nwepu) _                                                 | Aha nnabata nke Qdrant sidecar mgbe `--profile memory` na-arụ ọrụ. Ihe ndabara na-atụ aka na aha ọrụ qdrant na netwọk; mebie maka mbugharị mpụga. Na-ahapụ onye na-ahụ maka njedebe n'ihu (dịka ngwa desktọpụ VibeProxy ) enyo iwu ntụgharị nke ya n'elu injin-adaghachi azụ azụ azụ.                                                                                                                                                               |
