| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATION_DISABLE_TAG_WITHOUT_RESET` | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Igwefoto nke n'ihu-njedebe "Edola mkpado dị ka oke-oke na-enweghị oge nrụpụta" mmasị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_429`                      | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Ndaghachi azụ kwa-ọkwa na-enye ohere maka mperi `429`. Mgbe `false` (na `OMNIROUTE_ROTATION_ENABLED=true`), `429` anaghịzi akpalite ntụgharị akaụntụ wee weghachi ya na onye ahịa kama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_429_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Ọnụọgụ nke njehie `429` n'ime `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_429_WINDOW_SECONDS` achọrọ tupu atụgharị akaụntụ ahụ. `1` (ndabere) na-atụgharị ozugbo, na-echekwa omume akụkọ ihe mere eme.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_429_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Window na-amị amị (sekọnd) nke a na-agụta njehie `429` na `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_429_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_500`                      | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Ndaghachi azụ nke ọ bụla na-enyere aka maka njehie nkesa `5xx` (ewezuga `502`, nke nwere klaasị nke ya). Mgbe `false`, njehie ndị a anaghịzi akpalite ntụgharị akaụntụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_500_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Ọnụọgụ nke njehie `5xx` n'ime `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_500_WINDOW_SECONDS` achọrọ tupu atụgharị akaụntụ ahụ. `1` (ndabere) na-atụgharị ozugbo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_500_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Window na-amị amị (sekọnd) nke a na-agụta njehie `5xx` na `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_500_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

Mgbe `false`, `502` anaghịzi akpalite ntụgharị akaụntụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Ọnụọgụ nke njehie `502` n'ime `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_WINDOW_SECONDS` achọrọ tupu atụgharị akaụntụ ahụ. `1` (ndabere) na-atụgharị ozugbo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Window na-amị amị (sekọnd) nke a na-agụta njehie `502` na `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

Nke a bụ mgbakwunye naanị - ọ naghị egbochi omume injin dị adị ebe `400` na-ebu ederede rate-limit/quota ka na-adaba n'agbanyeghị ọkọlọtọ a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Ọnụọgụ nke njehie `400` n'ime `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_WINDOW_SECONDS` chọrọ tupu atụgharị akaụntụ ahụ (naanị a ga-atụle mgbe `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_400=true`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Window na-amị amị (sekọnd) nke a na-agụta njehie `400` na `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

### Claude Warmup Scheduler

Igwe ọkụ na-ebugharị cron maka njikọ Anthropic OAuth ahọpụtara, yabụ na-emepe windo njedebe ọnụego elekere 5 site na arịrịọ dị obere ahazi kama ịbụ nke mbụ (#8848). Agbanyụrụ onye nhazi oge belụsọ ma `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_ENABLED` bụ eziokwu** na** akara njikọ na `settings.claudeWarmup.connections`; ndepụta njikọ efu pụtara na ọ nweghị ihe na-ekpo ọkụ ọbụlagodi na env var on.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe             | Ọdabara                     | Isi mmalite faịlụ            | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ------------------------------ | --------------------------- | ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_ENABLED`     | _(gbanyụọ → ) _             | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Nna-ukwu mgba ọkụ maka nhazi oge ikpo ọkụ. Nabata `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` (okwu enweghị mmetụta, edoziri). Uru ọ bụla ọzọ, ma ọ bụ enweghị ntọala, na-ahapụ onye nhazi oge. |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_CRON`        | `0 7 * * *`                 | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Okwu cron ubi ise maka akara ọkụ ọkụ, enyocha ya na `America/Los_Angeles` ( mpaghara nrụpụta nke Anthropic) n'agbanyeghị elekere onye ọbịa.                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_CONCURRENCY` | `3`                         | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Njikọ ole ka a na-ekpo ọkụ n'otu n'otu n'otu akara. Ejidere na `1`-`10`; uru na-abụghị ọnụọgụ na-ada azụ na `3`.                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_MODEL`       | `claude-3-5-haiku-20241022` | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Nlereanya ejiri maka arịrịọ ikpo ọkụ. Wepụ naanị ma ọ bụrụ na ndabara adịghị na atụmatụ gị; họrọ ụdị dị ọnụ ala nke ka na-emepe windo.                                    |

### Nchọgharị-Nbanye VNC sessions & Data-Dir Alias

Chromium +VNC ejiri mee ihe maka njide nzere nbanye na ihe nchọgharị (`/api/vnc-session`), gbakwunyere utu aha `DATA_DIR`. Nhọrọ niile - ndabara VNC na-elekwasị anya na foto `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local` ekpokọtara ma bụrụ nke a na-eleghara ya anya maka foto akpa omenala, ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri, ma ọ bụ nlegharị anya okirikiri ndụ.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                    | Ọdabara                                                                  | Isi mmalite faịlụ                           | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IMAGE`                 | `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local`                                           | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | akara ngosi Docker maka akpa nbanye Chromium +VNC. Mepụta `docker/vnc-browser/chromium` ma ọ bụ tụọ nke a na onyonyo ahaziri iche.                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCKER_BIN`                | `docker`                                                                 | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ọnụọgụ ọnụọgụ ọsọ nke akpa ejiri ewee akpa VNC (dịka atọrọ na `podman`).                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_VNC_PORT`    | `3000`                                                                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri VNC/noVNC kpughere n'ime akpa ahụ.                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_CDP_PORT`    | `9223`                                                                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Chrome DevTools Protocol ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri n'ime akpa.                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_PROFILE_DIR` | `/config`                                                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ụzọ ndekọ profaịlụ Chromium n'ime akpa ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_PROFILE_DIR`           | `$HOME/.omniroute/browser-login-profiles`                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Akwụkwọ ndekọ aha nnabata na-ejide profaịlụ ihe nchọgharị-nbanye.                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IDLE_MS`               | `600000` (10 min)                                                        | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge nkwụsị (ms) na-adịghị agwụ agwụ tupu ewepụta oge VNC na-adịghị arụ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_MS`                | `1800000` (30 min)                                                       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Okpu siri ike (ms) na otu oge VNC ndụ.                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_SESSIONS`          | `4`                                                                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ọnụ ọgụgụ kacha nke oge VNC n'otu oge.                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_READY_MS`              | `45000`                                                                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge agwụla (ms) na-eche ihe nchọgharị echekwabara ka ọ bụrụ CDP-njikere.                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_HARVEST_MS`            | `20000`                                                                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge nkwụsị (ms) maka iweta session/cookies ejidere mgbe nbanye gwụchara.                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CHROMIUM_ARGS`         | `--remote-debugging-port=9222 --no-first-run --no-default-browser-check` | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ọkọlọtọ ahịrị iwu agbakwunyere na Chromium akpasara.                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_NETWORK`               | `omniroute-vnc-browser-login`                                            | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Netwọk Docker raara onwe ya nye akpa nbanye VNC na-esonye (#12571) kama akwa akwa ndabara, yabụ arịa ụmụnne enweghị ike iru ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri CDP ya.                                                          |
| `VIBEPROXY_DATA_DIR`                  | _(Edobeghi)_                                                             | `open-sse/services/notionThreadSessions.ts` | **Aha aha Legacy** maka `DATA_DIR`, a na-enyocha naanị mgbe ma `DATA_DIR` na `OMNIROUTE_DATA_DIR` na-atọpụghị. Achọta ebe nchekwa ihe ntanetị Notion webụ-thread (`<dir>/notion-web-thread-sessions.json`). |

---

## 26. Nwale & E2E Harness

A na-eji `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`, `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs`, `scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`, na `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs`. Hapụ uru ọ bụla n'okpuru adịghị eguzobe na mbugharị mmepụta.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                     | Ọdabara                        | Isi mmalite faịlụ                         | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| -------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_BOOTSTRAP_MODE`         | `auth`                         | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | E2E bootstrap mode (`auth`, `fresh`, `reuse`) maka onye na-agba ọsọ Playwright.                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_PASSWORD`               | daa azụ na `INITIAL_PASSWORD`  | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | agbanyere paswọọdụ onye nchịkwa n'ime gburugburu Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_LOCAL_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                         | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Gbanyụọ ntuli aka nyocha ahụike mpaghara n'oge Playwright na-agba.                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_TOKEN_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                         | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Gbanyụọ akara akara ahụike OAuth n'oge ule.                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEALTHCHECK_SKIP_PROVIDERS` | _(Edobeghi)_                   | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`             | Ewepụrụ ndị na-eweta rịkọm kewapụrụ na nchacha ntule ume ọhụrụ (dịka `codex,openai`). Ezubere iche maka ịgbanyụ nyocha ahụike zuru oke - ndị na-enye obere TTL na-enye ume ọhụrụ ebe ndị na-enye ohere na-emeghachi omume naanị. |
| `OMNIROUTE_HIDE_HEALTHCHECK_LOGS`      | `true`                         | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Mechie mkpọtụ nlele ahụike na Playwright stdout.                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLAYWRIGHT_SKIP_BUILD`      | `0`                            | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Mafee mmepụta Next.js tupu Playwright amalite (njikarịcha CI ).                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_UNINSTALL_HOOK`        | `0`                            | `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs`             | Mafee OmniRoute iwepụ nko (nke CI na-eji mee ka `node_modules` adịghị mma).                                                                                                                                                      |
| `ECOSYSTEM_SERVER_WAIT_MS`             | `180000`                       | `scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`     | Chere oge (ms) maka ihe nkesa ahụ ka ọ dị mma tupu ya agbaa ule ecosystem/protocol.                                                                                                                                              |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_URL`                   | `http://127.0.0.1:20128/login` | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | URL ihe njide anwụrụ ọkụ Electron na-atụ anya na ngwa ngwugwu ga-eje ozi.                                                                                                                                                        |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `45000`                        | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Ngụkọta oge nkwụsị (ms) tupu eriri anwụrụ ọkụ akwụsị.                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_SETTLE_MS`             | `2000`                         | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Dezie mpio (ms) ka ibe ibe gachara.                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_APP_EXECUTABLE`        | _(na akpaaka)_                 | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Ụzọ doro anya gaa na ngwugwu Electron enwere ike ime ya.                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_DATA_DIR`              | _(tmpdir) _                    | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Akwụkwọ ndekọ data maka ọsọ anwụrụ ọkụ Electron.                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_KEEP_DATA`             | `0`                            | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Tọọ `1` iji chekwaa ndekọ data anwụrụ ọkụ ka emechara.                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_STREAM_LOGS`           | `0`                            | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Tọọ `1` ka ịkwanye ndekọ Electron ka ọ bụrụ stdout n'oge ọsọ ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_COLD_RESTART`          | `0`                            | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | #7592: malitegharịa megide otu data dir wee kwupụta mmalite nke abụọ na-ahọrọ onye ọkwọ ụgbọ ala SQLite.                                                                                                                         |
| `CLI_DEVIN_BIN`                        | _(PATH anya)_                  | `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts`         | Wepụ ụzọ ọnụọgụ abụọ Devin CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

### Pipeline ntụgharị akwụkwọ

`scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` ji ya (iwu `npm run i18n:run` ). Emebere mgbanwe isii niile na ndabara - tọọ ha na `.env` naanị na igwe ga-enwe ike ịme ntụgharị akwụkwọ docs.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                         | Ọdabara      | Isi mmalite faịlụ                        | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------------------------------------------ | ------------ | ---------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_URL`            | _(Edobeghi)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`       | Mepee URL ntọala dakọtara na AI maka azụ ntụgharị asụsụ.                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_KEY`            | _(Edobeghi)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`       | Ihe nrịbama maka ntụgharị asụsụ azụ azụ (abanyebeghị).                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_MODEL`              | _(Edobeghi)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`       | Id ụdị, dịka `gpt-4o-mini` ma ọ bụ `cx/gpt-5.4-mini`.                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_FALLBACK_MODELS`    | _(Edobeghi)_ | `scripts/i18n/lib/translate-backend.mjs` | Yinye ihe atụ nke kewapụrụ rikoma nhọrọ nwalere n'usoro mgbe ụdị nke mbụ dara (oke 429, 401/402, 5xx, nkwụsị oge, netwọk); Achọgharịghị arịrịọ (400/413/422) emejọrọ ebe ọzọ.                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_TIMEOUT_MS`         | `60000`      | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`       | Oge nkwụsị nke otu-arịọrọ na millisekọnd.                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_REASONING_EFFORT`   | _(Edobeghi)_ | `scripts/i18n/lib/translate-backend.mjs` | Njikwa ntụgharị uche nhọrọ, ezigara dị ka ọ dị na azụ azụ mgbe edobere ya.                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_CONCURRENCY`        | `4`          | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`       | Arịrịọ ntụgharị asụsụ ndị yiri ya mgbe ị na-agafe ọtụtụ faịlụ / mpaghara.                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_ENGINE`             | `llm`        | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`       | `llm` na-eji azụ azụ n'elu; `google` tụgharịrị site na njin Google na-echekwa ma ọ nweghị nke mgbanwe LLM chọrọ.                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_GOOGLE_FALLBACK`    | `1`          | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`       | Site na injin `llm`, mkpirisi ihe nlereanya ahụ enweghị ike imepụta (onye nche mmepụta dara na mbọ ọ bụla, ma ọ bụ njehie ụdị) bụ nke Google tụgharịrị kama ịdaba na mpaghara. `0` gbanyụọ ya. |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_GOOGLE_API_KEY`     | _(Edobeghi)_ | `scripts/i18n/lib/google-translate.mjs`  | Igodo ntụgharị asụsụ ojii nke nhọrọ v2. Unset na-eji njedebe webụ enweghị igodo (oke oke, ọ dị mma maka ebili mmiri mgbe ụfọdụ).                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_GOOGLE_CONCURRENCY` | `6`          | `scripts/i18n/lib/google-translate.mjs`  | Arịrịọ Google yiri nke mpaghara ọ bụla nke ọsọ na-ekekọrịta.                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_GOOGLE_TIMEOUT_MS`  | `30000`      | `scripts/i18n/lib/google-translate.mjs`  | Oge nkwụsị nke otu-arịọrọ maka injin Google, na millise seconds.                                                                                                                               |

## 27. Nri radar ( Bochum nkeonwe)

Nhọrọ mgbakwunye gated site na ọkọlọtọ njirimara RADAR_ENABLED (ndapụta - ọkọlọtọ njirimara na-atụgharị site na Settings/DB, ọ bụghị env var; lee [docs/frameworks/RADAR.md](../frameworks/RADAR.md#flag-radar_enabled-default-off) ). Ngbanwe anọ ndị mbụ dị n'okpuru bụ nhọrọ nhọrọ maka ntinye aka nke onwe ma ọ bụ ndụdụ na isi igodo nkwado. Nke ise, `RADAR_ADMIN_URL`, bụ njikọ dị iche na-enweghị ndabara na panel arụmọrụ nke onye nwe ya. Hụ [docs/frameworks/RADAR.md](../frameworks/RADAR.md) maka doc modul zuru oke yana [mmalite ya na ngwụcha ngwụcha yana usoro ntọala](../frameworks/RADAR.md#end-to-end-activation-and-guided-setup).

Onye na-agụ ọkwa ọkwa Home/Changelog anaghị ahazi ya site na mgbanwe gburugburu ebe obibi na ọ dabereghị na ọkọlọtọ njirimara RADAR_ENABLED. Ọ na-agụ ebe nchekwa ọha `news.json` URL ekwuputara na `src/shared/utils/releaseNotes.ts` na GET naanị; IDs nchụpụ ka dị na nchekwa ihe nchọgharị mpaghara.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe            | Ọdabara                                      | Isi mmalite faịlụ                                            | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                        |
| ----------------------------- | -------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `RADAR_FEED_URL`              | `https://radar.omniroute.online`             | `src/lib/radar/{sync,referralsSync,offersSync,intelSync}.ts` | Base URL kesara site na katalọgụ bịanyere aka na ya iche, ntinye aka, onyinye nkwado na nri Intel. Wepụtụ aka na ọrụ ejiri aka gị ma ọ bụ nke agbadoro anya. |
| `RADAR_FEED_PUBKEY`           | _(igodo ndabara etinyere)_                   | `src/lib/radar/pinnedKeys.ts`                                | Ed25519 igodo ọha (base64-DER SPKI ma ọ bụ PEM) ejiri iji nyochaa mbinye aka nri site na ndepụta ahaziri iche.                                               |
| `RADAR_CONTRIBUTOR_CLAIM_URL` | `https://radar.omniroute.online/auth/github` | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | URL bọtịnụ dashboard "Abụ m onye ntinye aka ga-emepe (GitHub OAuth supporter-key claim flow).                                                                |
| `RADAR_SUPPORTER_PLANS_URL`   | `https://radar.omniroute.online/planos`      | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | URL bọtịnụ dashboard "kwado ọrụ ahụ" ga-emepe ( ibe payment/plans ).                                                                                         |
| `RADAR_ADMIN_URL`             | _(Edobeghi)_                                 | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | Njikọ ọrụ naanị onye nwe ya-njikọ. achọrọ HTTPS belụsọ maka HTTP loopback SSH n'ihu; ụkpụrụ atọpụghị ma ọ bụ ezighi ezi emepụtaghị ihe nsoroụzọ.             |

---

## Nyocha: Wepụrụ / Nwụrụ Anwụ mgbanwe

Ụdị mgbanwe ndị a pụtara na nsụgharị `.env.example` gara aga mana nwere** enweghị ntụaka oge ojiri gaa** na codebase dị ugbu a. E wepụrụ ha:

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                                                                                                                                                              | Ihe kpatara ya                                                                                                                            |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `STORAGE_DRIVER=sqlite`                                                                                                                                                         | Agaghị agụ site na faịlụ isi mmalite ọ bụla. SQLite bụ naanị ọkwọ ụgbọ ala akwadoro - enweghị nhọrọ achọrọ.                               |
| `INSTANCE_NAME=omniroute`                                                                                                                                                       | Gaa na ndebiri docs/env ochie mana ejighi ya n'oge ojiri gaa. Nwere ike ịlaghachi na njirimara ọtụtụ ihe atụ n'ọdịnihu.                   |
| `SQLITE_MAX_SIZE_MB=2048`                                                                                                                                                       | Edeghị ya na koodu isi mmalite. Ọnweghị oke nchekwa data nwere oke n'ụzọ artificial.                                                      |
| `SQLITE_CLEAN_LEGACY_FILES=true`                                                                                                                                                | Edeghị ya na koodu isi mmalite. O yikarịrị ka ewepụrụ nhichapụ ihe nketa.                                                                 |
| `CLI_ROO_BIN`                                                                                                                                                                   | Edebanyeghị aha na `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`.                                                                                   |
| `CLI_KIMI_CODING_BIN`                                                                                                                                                           | Edebanyeghị aha na `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` (Kimi Coding na-eji OAuth, ọ bụghị ọnụọgụ abụọ CLI ).                              |
| `IFLOW_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` / `IFLOW_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                                                                                                                           | Edeghị ebe ọ bụla na koodu isi mmalite.                                                                                                   |
| `CEREBRAS_API_KEY` / `COHERE_API_KEY` / `FIREWORKS_API_KEY` / `GROQ_API_KEY` / `MISTRAL_API_KEY` / `NEBIUS_API_KEY` / `PERPLEXITY_API_KEY` / `TOGETHER_API_KEY` / `XAI_API_KEY` | Ewepụrụ na v3.8.0. Oge ojiri gaa anaghị agụ env vars ndị a - nzere sitere na Dashboard / `data/provider-credentials.json` / ezoro ezo DB. |
| `CURSOR_PROTOBUF_DEBUG`                                                                                                                                                         | Ewepụrụ na v3.8.0. Cursor executor na-eji `CURSOR_DEBUG` / `CURSOR_STREAM_DEBUG` (lee §22).                                               |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KIRO`                                                                                                                                                               | Ewepụrụ na v3.8.0. Kiro nọ na `CLI_COMPAT_OMITTED_PROVIDER_IDS` - ntụgharị ya enweghị mmetụta ọ bụla.                                     |
| `QIANFAN_API_KEY`                                                                                                                                                               | Ewepụrụ ya na ndị ọzọ na-eweta API isi mkpịsị ugodi na v3.8.0.                                                                            |

### Mmezi Uru Ọsọ

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe        | Uru `.env.example` ochie | Ndebanye aha koodu | Edoziri                                                     |
| ------------------------- | ------------------------ | ------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------- |
| `APP_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`  | `90`                     | `7`                | ✅ wepụrụ uru na-eduhie eduhie; edepụtara `7` dị ka ndabere |
| `CALL_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS` | `90`                     | `7`                | ✅ wepụrụ uru na-eduhie eduhie; edepụtara `7` dị ka ndabere |

### OpenCode config regeneration (ad-hoc tooling)

Nke `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` na-eji weghachi `opencode.json` na ụkpụrụ `limit.context` na `limit.output` ziri ezi ewepụtara na OmniRoute na-agba ọsọ. Ọ dịghị nke ọ bụla n'ime ihe ndị a achọrọ maka ịrụ ọrụ nkịtị - edemede a bụ naanị ngwaọrụ nrụpụta.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe | Ọdabara                  | Isi mmalite faịlụ                         | Nkọwa                                                                                                                        |
| ------------------ | ------------------------ | ----------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_URL`    | `http://localhost:20128` | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | Ntọala URL nke ihe atụ OmniRoute iji jụọ ajụjụ maka `/v1/models`.                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_KEY`    | _(Edobeghi)_             | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | igodo API iji nyochaa megide ebe njedebe `/v1/models` OmniRoute. Daghachi azụ na `OPENCODE_API_KEY` mgbe ịtọghị ya.          |
| `OPENCODE_API_KEY` | _(Edobeghi)_             | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | Igodo API ụdị OpenCode (`sk-...`) edere n'ime `opencode.json` eweghachitere. Daghachi azụ na `OMNIROUTE_KEY` mgbe ịtọghị ya. |

### Mkpakọ na-anọghị n'ịntanetị-eval njigide (ad-hoc tooling)

`scripts/compression-eval/index.ts` ji ya , nyocha mkpakọ na-anọghị n'ịntanetị CLI. Achọghị maka ịrụ ọrụ nkịtị - naanị ngwaọrụ nrụpụta.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe           | Ọdabara    | Isi mmalite faịlụ                   | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ---------------------------- | ---------- | ----------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_EVAL_CREDENTIALS` | `{}` (efu) | `scripts/compression-eval/index.ts` | Asambodo JSON nke onye ọrụ wetara maka onye na-eweta nke mkpakọ-eval CLI na-anọghị n'ịntanetị gosipụtara (jiri `JSON.parse` tụgharịrị). Hapụ na-edozighị maka ọsọ akọrọ. |

### VNC Nchọgharị

Nke `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts` na-eji hazie oge Chromium enweghị isi nke dabere na Docker maka ndị na-eweta ihe nchọgharị-akpaaka. Nhọrọ niile - ndabara egosiri n'okpuru.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                    | Ọdabara                        | Isi mmalite faịlụ                           | Nkọwa                                                                           |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCKER_BIN`                | `docker`                       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ụzọ na ọnụọgụ abụọ Docker a na-eji amịpụta arịa VNC.                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IMAGE`                 | `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local` | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | foto Docker maka akpa VNC Chromium.                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CHROMIUM_ARGS`         | _(ọkọlọtọ arụnyere n'ime ya)_  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Extra Chromium CLI args gafere na ihe nchọgharị dị n'ime akpa ahụ.              |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_VNC_PORT`    | `3000`                         | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | VNC ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri n'ime akpa.                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_CDP_PORT`    | `9223`                         | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Chrome DevTools Protocol ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri n'ime akpa.                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_PROFILE_DIR` | `/config`                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Akwụkwọ ndekọ aha n'ime akpa ahụ.                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_PROFILE_DIR`           | _(Edobeghi)_                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Akwụkwọ ndekọ nke ndị ọbịa maka profaịlụ ihe nchọgharị na-adịgide adịgide.      |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IDLE_MS`               | `600000`                       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge nkwụsị (ms) na-adịghị agwụ agwụ tupu ewekọta oge VNC.                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_MS`                | `1800000`                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ogologo oge kachasị (ms).                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_SESSIONS`          | `4`                            | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge VNC kacha n'otu oge.                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_READY_MS`              | `45000`                        | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge ngwụcha njikere ihe nchọgharị (ms).                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_HARVEST_MS`            | `20000`                        | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Harvest/cleanup nkwụsị (ms).                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_NETWORK`               | `omniroute-vnc-browser-login`  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Netwọk Docker raara onwe ya nye, akpa ahụ na-esonye (#12571), pụọ na akwa akwa. |
| `VIBEPROXY_DATA_DIR`                  | _(Edobeghi)_                   | `open-sse/services/notionThreadSessions.ts` | Akwụkwọ ndekọ aha maka Notion eri oge nnọgidesi ike.                            |

### Ntinye ọrụ ime

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                      | Ọdabara | Nkọwa                                                                                |
| --------------------------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN`      | –       | Inline token maka njikwa-ụgbọelu ọrụ-na-ọrụ nyocha.                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN_FILE` | –       | Uzo gaa na faịlụ nwere akara ọrụ ime (ọ masịrị ya na arịa; na-ewepụ mgbanwe inline). |

### onye na-eweta OpenRouter stats

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                  | Ọdabara   | Nkọwa                                                                             |
| ----------------------------------- | --------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OPENROUTER_PROVIDER_STATS_ENABLED` | `true`    | Tọọ `false` ka ịwụpụ ịweta OpenRouter stats onye na-eweta maka nkwalite katalọgụ. |
| `OPENROUTER_PROVIDER_STATS_TTL_MS`  | `3600000` | Cache TTL (ms) maka stats ndị na-eweta OpenRouter ewetara.                        |

### agbakwunyere Redis njide

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe          | Ọdabara     | Nkọwa                                                                 |
| --------------------------- | ----------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REDIS_BIND_HOST`           | `127.0.0.1` | Kechie adreesị maka ọrụ Redis agbakwunyere.                           |
| `REDIS_PORT`                | `6379`      | Port maka ọrụ Redis agbakwunyere.                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDIS_BIND_HOST` | –           | OmniRoute kpuchiri ekpuchi maka adreesị ejikọtara Redis agbakwunyere. |

## 24. Mwepụta v3.8.50 mgbakwunye

### Weebụ ChatGPT (Codex)

Globale Defaults für den headless Browser na ausgehenden Tool-Tunnel. Enwere m Dashboard iji nweta njikọ-Werte haben Vorrang.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                      | Ọdabara                        | Isi mmalite faịlụ                                                          | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CHROME_PATH`         | _( chọpụta na akpaghị aka)_    | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Expliziter Chrome-/Chromium-Pfad für npm-, systemd- und PM2-Betrieb.                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CHROME_PATH`                           | _( chọpụta na akpaghị aka)_    | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Gemeinsamer Fallback nke na-eme ka ọ pụta Chrome-/Chromium-Pfad.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CDP_URL`             | _(Edobeghi)_                   | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Ime CDP-Endpunkt; Docker verwendet den Sidecar auf Port `9223`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_USE_CDP_PROFILE`     | _(Akwụsịghị - gbanyụọ)_        | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/persistent-cdp-context.ts`              | Tọọ na `1` (nwere ebe ngwụcha CDP ahaziri) iji jikwaa ibe ChatGPT nke ihe nchọgharị abanyela na mpụga, kama ịbụ ihe dịpụrụ adịpụ n'otu oge; na-ekpuchikwa taabụ ChatGPT n'otu oge na 1. Gbanyụọ na ndabara. Hụ [profaịlụ CDP na-adịgide adịgide](../guides/CHATGPT-WEB-PERSISTENT-CDP.md).         |
| `CDP_PROXY_TOKEN`                       | _(Edobeghi)_                   | `docker/chatgpt-web-codex-browser/cdp-proxy.mjs`                           | Wenn gesetzt, muss jede Anfrage an den CDP-Proxy-Sidecar diesen Wert im Header `X-Omni-Cdp-Token` mitschicken (#13679). Ohne Wert leitet der Proxy unauthentifiziert weiter — dann schützt nur die Netzisolierung des Compose-Netzes `chatgpt-web-codex-net`. Erzeugen mit `openssl rand -hex 32`. |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_TUNNEL_ID`           | _(Edobeghi)_                   | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Globale OpenAI-Tunnel-ID iji nweta Codex-Tool-Runden.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_RUNTIME_KEY`         | _(Edobeghi)_                   | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Oge ọwara ọwara ụwa-Igodo; Niemals na Logs ausgeben.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CONNECTOR_NAME`      | `OmniRoute Codex v2`           | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Exakter Aha des neu erstellten ChatGPT-Custom-Connectors für die MCP-Brücke.                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_HOME`                | `<DATA_DIR>/chatgpt-web-codex` | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Dediziertes Verzeichnis für Browser-, Broker- na Tunnelzustand.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BROWSER_DIAGNOSTICS` | `0`                            | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/adapters/chatgpt-web/browser-worker.ts` | `1` ihe nchọgharị-Diagnosebilder na ebe a na-enyocha ebe a na-enyocha ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_LAUNCHER`            | _(Edobeghi)_                   | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Optionaler absoluter Pfad zu einem dauerhaften Launcher-Binary.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BUN`                 | _( chọpụta na akpaghị aka)_    | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Nhọrọ zuru oke Pfad zum Bun-Runtime-binary.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CODEX_WEB_GPT_BUN`                     | _(Edobeghi)_                   | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Legacy-Fallback für `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BUN`; neue Setups verwenden den kanonischen Namen.                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

## OmniConductor Bridge

Ndị ahịa SSE dị ogologo ndụ nke na-egosipụta ọrụ OmniConductor hub n'ime A2A TaskManager (`src/lib/conductor/`). Ịbanye - akwa mmiri na-amalite naanị mgbe edobere `CONDUCTOR_HUB_URL`. Naanị akụkụ nkesa: akara hub agaghị erute ihe nchọgharị ahụ.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe             | Ọdabara                 | Isi mmalite faịlụ                | Nkọwa                                                                                                                 |
| ------------------------------ | ----------------------- | -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CONDUCTOR_HUB_URL`            | _(efu)_                 | `src/lib/conductor/boot.ts`      | Ndabere URL nke ọdụ OmniConductor (dịka `http://127.0.0.1:7910`). Akwụsịla akwa mmiri =.                              |
| `CONDUCTOR_HUB_TOKEN`          | _(efu)_                 | `src/lib/conductor/boot.ts`      | Nkwenye Hub maka nri SSE - wepụta `spokesperson` -kind ọgbọ na hub (`POST /v1/peers`, admin).                         |
| `CONDUCTOR_ORCHESTRATOR_TOKEN` | _(efu)_                 | `src/lib/conductor/hubProxy.ts`  | nzere maka mbata A2A→ hub ọrụ ndị nnọchi anya (`POST /v1/tasks`); daa azụ na `CONDUCTOR_HUB_TOKEN` mgbe ịtọgheghị ya. |
| `CONDUCTOR_SPOKESPERSON_URL`   | `http://127.0.0.1:7920` | `src/lib/conductor/faroProxy.ts` | Base URL nke ọrụ ọnụ na-ekwuchitere (Faro) n'azụ proxy nkata dashboard (`/api/conductor/ask`).                        |

### Nhazi nhazi ọkwa-mmata

Ndị `open-sse/services/combo.ts` na `src/lib/quota/quotaScheduler.ts` na-eji maka nlele ego mmefu ego tupu arịrịọ. Nwepu - omume mbugharị ndabara adịghị agbanwe agbanwe mgbe edobereghị ya.

| Na-agbanwe agbanwe                 | Ọdabara | Isi mmalite faịlụ                           | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ---------------------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_AWARE_ROUTING`    | `0`     | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`                | Mgbe `1`, kwupụta njikọ nke mmefu ego nrịbama nke otu windo (`rateLimitOverrides.tpm`, table `provider_quota_state`) enweghị ike ị nweta ọnụ ahịa arịrịọ echere tupu izipu ya. Ọdịda-mepee mgbe ahazighị mmefu ego.                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_ESTIMATOR_CALIBRATION`  | _(na)_  | `open-sse/services/estimatorCalibration.ts` | Nhazi nzaghachi nke ihe nleba anya ihe nleba anya gburugburu-nche (#14931): kwa (onye na-eweta, ihe nlereanya, ngwaọrụ dị ugbu a) EMA nke `usage.prompt_tokens` / atụmatụ, agbakwunyere na 0.15–2.0, na ebe nchekwa naanị. `off`, `0` ma ọ bụ `false` gbanyụọ ndekọ na ngwa. |
| `GROK_SUBSCRIPTION_IMAGES_ENABLED` | `false` | `open-sse/config/imageRegistry.ts`          | Na-edeba aha `xai-oauth` (`xao`) na `grok-cli` ụzọ onyonyo. Dashboard atụmatụ ọkọlọtọ nke otu aha; mwepu DB meriri uru a.                                                                                                                                                    |
